### [1] [Asymmetric Little--Parks Oscillations as a Probe of Coupled Pairing Channels in Surface-Superconducting \texorpdfstring{PtBi$_2$}{PtBi2}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01764)
*Yuriy Yerin,Stefan-Ludwig Drechsler,Carsten Timm,Jeroen van den Brink*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文用三通道Ginzburg-Landau模型研究PtBi2窄环的Little-Parks振荡，预言面内磁场可导致正常磁通反转下平均场转变温度的不对称，并展示多分量超导的磁调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受PtBi2中非常规表面超导发现的启发，希望在同一材料制作的介观窄环中寻找其配对态在Little-Parks干涉中的特征。

Method: 采用满足三方晶格对称性的三耦合配对通道Ginzburg-Landau模型，计算窄环在固定面内磁场和穿过环的正常磁通下的超导转变与Little-Parks振荡。

Result: 固定面内磁场会使平均场转变温度在正常磁通反号时不对称，同时保持以超导磁通量子为周期的周期性；弱场下该不对称的主导贡献为面内场强的三次方。磁通奇分量遵守三重晶体对称性，并在面内磁场旋转π/3时变号。均匀环模型中单配对通道不产生该不对称，它源于三个通道的耦合；增大面内磁场还可通过通道混合提高超导成核温度。

Conclusion: Little-Parks干涉测量可独立控制磁通与场方向，从而作为探测配对态的介观手段，与能隙结构的谱学研究互补。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent discovery of unconventional surface superconductivity in PtBi$_{2}$, we theoretically study its signatures in the Little-Parks oscillations of a narrow ring made from the same material. Using a Ginzburg-Landau model with three coupled pairing channels allowed by the trigonal crystal symmetry, we show that a fixed in-plane magnetic field can make the mean-field transition temperature asymmetric under reversal of the normal flux through the ring, while preserving periodicity in the superconducting flux quantum. The leading weak-field contribution to this asymmetry is cubic in the in-plane field strength. The flux-odd component respects the threefold crystal symmetry and changes sign when the in-plane field is rotated by $\pi/3$ at fixed magnitude. Within the homogeneous ring model, this asymmetry is absent for a single pairing channel and arises from the coupling between the three channels. Our calculations further show that increasing the in-plane magnetic field can enhance the superconducting nucleation temperature through mixing of pairing channels, illustrating magnetic control of multicomponent superconductivity in mesoscopic rings. Little-Parks interferometry with independent control of flux and field direction thus offers a mesoscopic probe of the pairing state, complementary to spectroscopic studies of the gap structure.

</details>


### [2] [Theoretical Prediction of Superconducting 2D Hydrogenated Janus V2CSH and Nb2CSH MXenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01502)
*Jubair Hossan Abir,M. R. U. S. Shapon,Md. Sharear Aman,Md. Kamrul Hassan,A. K. M. Azharul Islam,Saleh Hasan Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文首次对比研究了氢/硫终止的Janus MXene单层V2CSH与Nb2CSH，基于DFT和DFPT揭示其稳定性、金属性及电子-声子耦合驱动的超导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维氢化Janus MXene因非对称表面终止可调控电子与振动性质，是超导材料新前沿；作者旨在通过金属替代比较V2CSH和Nb2CSH，建立调控超导性的设计原则。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）和密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT），系统计算结构稳定性、热稳定性、面内弹性、电子结构、声子性质及电子-声子耦合，并在Allen-Dynes框架下估算超导转变温度。

Result: V2CSH和Nb2CSH均结构/热稳定，具各向同性面内弹性、非磁性多带金属态且由过渡金属d轨道主导；S/H非对称终止产生不等价表面势和本征面外偶极。电子-声子耦合常数为0.39（V2CSH）和0.53（Nb2CSH），对应Tc为2.36 K和5.27 K；低能晶格振动主要涉及金属和硫原子，贡献两者77%的耦合。

Conclusion: 金属替代是调控Janus MXene超导性的有效设计原则，Nb2CSH是有前景的低温超导候选材料。

Abstract: Two dimensional hydrogenated Janus MXenes represent a new frontier in superconducting materials, where asymmetric surface terminations enable tunable electronic and vibrational properties. In this work, we present the first comparative study of hydrogen or sulfur terminated V2CSH and Nb2CSH monolayers using density functional theory and density functional perturbation theory. Both compounds are structurally and thermally stable, exhibiting isotropic in plane elasticity and nonmagnetic multiband metallic states dominated by transition metal d orbitals. The asymmetric S or H terminations generate nonequivalent surface potentials and intrinsic out of plane dipoles. Electron phonon coupling constants of 0.39 (V2CSH) and 0.53 (Nb2CSH) yield superconducting transition temperatures of 2.36 K and 5.27 K, respectively, within the Allen Dynes framework. Low energy lattice vibrations, primarily involving metal and sulfur atoms, account for 77% of the coupling in both MXenes. These findings establish metal substitution as a design principle for tuning superconductivity in Janus MXenes and highlight Nb2CSH as a promising candidate for low temperature superconducting applications.

</details>


### [3] [Superinsulation, electric confinement in condensed matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02841)
*M. Cristina Diamantini,Carlo A. Trugenberger*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 超绝缘是超导的对偶禁闭相：在具有涌现颗粒凝聚的超导体中，U(1)电磁场被磁单极子凝聚禁闭，形成对库珀对的线性势并导致直至有限去禁闭温度的无限电阻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解超导-绝缘转变附近的物相，并检验仅相位与无序主导图像能否解释相关实验；作者将凝聚态超绝缘与粒子物理中的禁闭-希格斯对偶联系起来。

Method: 综述超绝缘的理论框架，并结合实验验证进行讨论，包括对线性禁闭势的直接观测，同时评述相 only 与 disorder-based 解释与近期实验的不一致。

Result: 无芯 XY 涡旋变为无张力、不可观测的狄拉克弦，磁单极子被释放并凝聚；电场被禁闭为通量管，对库珀对产生线性势，表现为直至有限去禁闭温度的无限电阻。实验直接观测到线性禁闭势，支持该图像。

Conclusion: 超绝缘是电磁 U(1) 规范场禁闭的真正量子物态，与超导对偶；仅相位或无风景象难以与近期实验调和，超导-绝缘转变附近物相需在规范禁闭/对偶框架下理解。

Abstract: Superinsulation is the confining phase of condensed matter systems. It is a genuine quantum state of matter, dual to superconductivity, just as confinement in particle physics is dual to the Higgs phase. In contrast to hadron physics, the gauge field that becomes confining is not colour SU(3) but the conventional U(1) electromagnetic field. Superinsulation arises in superconductors with an emergent granular condensate when core-less XY vortices between condensate grains become tensionless, unobservable Dirac strings and magnetic monopoles are liberated and condense. This magnetic condensate confines electric fields into flux tubes, strings that generate a linear potential between Cooper pairs. These dual Abrikosov flux tubes give rise to infinite electrical resistance, persisting up to a finite deconfinement temperature. We shall review the theoretical framework of superinsulation together with its experimental validation, including the direct observation of the linear confining potential and we shall stress how phase-only approaches and disorder-based ideas for the phases around the superconductor-to-insulator transition are hard to reconcile with recent experimental observations.

</details>


### [4] [Direct imaging of the Ni $e_g$ hole orbitals in the bilayer nickelate La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01118)
*Ethan Sa\"iman,Feiyu Li,Junjie Zhang,Jean-Pascal Rueff,Victor Bal\'edent*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用Ni M1边s核心能级非共振非弹性X射线散射测定La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$中Ni $3d$空穴的角分布，得到$h_z/h_x=0.94\pm0.06$，接近Hund耦合图像，并支持两个$e_g$轨道均参与超导配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物和镍酸盐中的非常规超导模型强烈依赖$d$轨道价电子分布，但精确测定这些体系中的轨道电子或空穴占据仍困难且未解决。

Method: 在单晶La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$上采用Ni $M_1$边的$s$核心能级非共振非弹性X射线散射，确定Ni $3d$空穴的角分布。

Result: 测得空穴占据比$h_z/h_x=0.94\pm0.06$，显著高于$d_{z^2}$轨道填满模型预期的$2/3$，并接近Hund耦合图像预期值。

Conclusion: 结果表明La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$中两个$e_g$轨道均对电子配对机制有贡献，从而支持相应的超导情景。

Abstract: Models of unconventional superconductivity in cuprates and nickelates depend sensitively on the distribution of valence electrons among the $d$ orbitals. But accurately determining the orbital electron or hole occupations in these systems remains a difficult and unresolved task. Here, using $s$-core-level non-resonant inelastic x-ray scattering at the Ni $M_1$ edge, we determine the angular distribution of the Ni $3d$ holes in a single crystal of La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$. Our results demonstrate that the hole occupation ratio $\hz/\hx = 0.94\pm0.06$, thus significantly above the expected value of $2/3$ for a filled $d_{z^2}$ orbitals models and close to value expected in a Hund's-coupling picture. Our results support superconductivity scenarios in which both $e_g$ orbitals contribute to the electron-pairing mechanism in La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$.

</details>


### [5] [Ab initio exploration of hydrogen-intercalated transition metal dichalcogenides: From identifying stable structures to emergent superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00271)
*William Wenborn,Jonas Bekaert*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 第一性原理研究氢插层TMDs不同构型的结构、热力学、电子与超导性质，发现多数构型半导体化并抑制CDW，电子掺杂可使Tc升至25 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究氢插层对过渡金属硫族化物结构稳定性、电子结构、CDW不稳定性及超导性的调控，并寻找提升超导临界温度的途径。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，比较不同氢插层构型的结构、热力学稳定性、电子能带和超导性质。

Result: 多数构型中每个晶胞的两个插层H贡献两个电子，打开带隙使体系呈半导体性；动态稳定构型中有一个例外保持金属性。特定氢构型完全抑制原相CDW不稳定性。空穴掺杂可使半导体结构金属化但Tc极低；电子掺杂通过填充带隙上方高密度态将Tc提高至25 K。

Conclusion: 氢插层构型可有效调控TMDs的电子态与CDW，电子掺杂可实现较高Tc，提示可通过离子液体门控或碱金属共插层开展实验探索。

Abstract: We present a first-principles study of hydrogen-intercalated transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs), examining the structural, thermodynamic, electronic and superconducting properties of distinct intercalation configurations. In most configurations, the two intercalated hydrogen atoms contribute two electrons per unit cell, creating a band gap and thus rendering the system semiconducting. Among the dynamically stable configurations there is one exception to this rule, with inherently metallic behavior. The pristine phases of these materials are known to exhibit a charge density wave instability, which is fully suppressed by specific hydrogen configurations. Moreover, the semiconducting structures can be metallized by hole doping, albeit that the superconducting critical temperatures ($T_c$) are vanishingly low. On the other hand, electron doping drives the $T_c$ up to 25 K, by populating dense states above the gap. This finding can motivate further experimental exploration of superconductivity in hydrogen-intercalated TMDs through ionic-liquid gating or alkali co-intercalation.

</details>


### [6] [Effect of Rapid Thermal Annealing on the Superconductivity of ALD-Grown TiN thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00424)
*Z. Sadirkhanov,A. Tuigynbek,M. Surendran,A. Jakyp,L. Akisheva,A. T. Hammack,A. B. Tikhonov,A. Kemelbay*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作利用高纯氮快速热退火（RTA）后处理改善RF偏压等离子体增强原子层沉积（PEALD）多晶TiN薄膜的结构、化学与超导性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD生长的TiN因氧掺入、非化学计量和晶格无序导致超导性能退化，需要一种低热预算且CMOS兼容的后处理策略。

Method: 对RF偏压PEALD多晶TiN进行高纯氮RTA，并结合掠入射X射线衍射、XPS深度剖析及超导/输运测量评估结构与性能变化。

Result: 退火使衍射峰展宽减小、晶格参数降低并增强(111)择优取向，同时减少氧相关成键；超导转变温度提高25%，转变宽度显著变窄，薄层电阻和估计薄层动态电感降低，剩余电阻比提高，磁场耐受性改善。

Conclusion: RTA作为低热预算、半导体工艺兼容的后处理可减少材料无序并提升ALD-TiN超导均匀性，为可扩展超导量子器件提供实用优化路径。

Abstract: Titanium nitride (TiN) is a promising superconducting material for scalable quantum technologies owing to its tunable kinetic inductance and compatibility with CMOS thin-film processing. However, the superconducting performance of atomic-layer-deposited TiN can be degraded by oxygen incorporation, off-stoichiometry, and crystallographic disorder. In this work, we investigate rapid thermal annealing (RTA) in high-purity nitrogen as a post-deposition strategy to improve polycrystalline TiN films grown by RF-biased plasma-enhanced atomic layer deposition. Grazing-incidence X-ray diffraction reveals reduced peak broadening, a smaller lattice parameter, and a stronger (111) preferred orientation with increasing annealing temperature. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy depth profiling shows reduced oxygen-related bonding and a shift toward a Ti--N-dominated chemical environment after annealing. These structural and chemical changes result in a 25% increase in the superconducting transition temperature and a significant narrowing of the transition width. Annealing also reduced the sheet resistance and the estimated sheet kinetic inductance, increased the residual resistance ratio, and improved the magnetic-field tolerance. Together, the structural, chemical, and transport results are consistent with reduced material disorder and improved superconducting uniformity. Because RTA is a relatively low thermal budget and is a semiconductor fabrication compatible process, it offers a practical route to improve the quality of ALD-grown TiN for superconducting applications.

</details>


### [7] [Nodeless $d$-wave superconductivity driven by charge fluctuations in monolayer FeSe on SrTiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00534)
*Jin-Wei Dong,Sen Zhou,Jian Wang,Gang Su,Ziqiang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出Fe-Se间位库仑排斥增强的小动量p-d电荷转移涨落是单层FeSe/SrTiO3中无节点d波超导的可行配对机制，并解释了SJTM观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层FeSe/SrTiO3具有铁基超导最高Tc之一，但其配对机制与对称性长期未明；近期扫描约瑟夫森隧穿显微镜发现无节点d波超导，促使作者研究其电子起源。

Method: 构建包含Fe 3d与Se 4p轨道、 onsite与intersite库仑相互作用的扩展Hubbard模型，分析Fe-Se间位库仑排斥驱动的p-d电荷转移涨落及前向散射光学声子增强效应，并计算超导配对、能隙、态密度与约瑟夫森隧穿电导。

Result: Fe-Se间位库仑排斥强烈增强小动量p-d电荷转移涨落，产生吸引性轨道内自旋单态配对；与残余排斥性口袋间相互作用共同稳定镜像奇、无节点的B1g d波态，两个电子口袋能隙反号，Fe t2g与Se 4p均显著贡献。计算得到双峰能隙/态密度结构及半晶格周期约瑟夫森电导调制，与实验一致。

Conclusion: 电子电荷转移涨落可作为单层FeSe/STO非常规无节点d波超导的可行配对机制。

Abstract: Monolayer FeSe and Fe(Te,Se) grown on SrTiO$_3$ exhibit some of the highest superconducting transition temperatures among iron-based superconductors, yet their pairing mechanism and symmetry remain elusive. Motivated by the recent surprising discovery of nodeless $d$-wave superconductivity using scanning Josephson tunneling microscopy (SJTM) [T. Wei et al., arXiv:2610.38421 (2026)], we investigate superconductivity in monolayer FeSe/SrTiO$3$ (1L-FeSe/STO) within an extended Hubbard model incorporating both onsite and intersite Coulomb interactions among Fe $3d$ and Se $4p$ orbitals. We show that Fe-Se intersite Coulomb repulsion strongly enhances small-momentum $p$-$d$ charge-transfer fluctuations, which mediate attractive intraorbital spin-singlet pairing interactions. These electronic fluctuations may be further enhanced by forward-scattering optical phonons of the SrTiO$3$ substrate. The resulting intrapocket attraction, together with residual repulsive interpocket interactions, stabilizes a mirror-odd, nodeless $B_{1g}$ ($d$-wave) superconducting state with opposite gap signs on the two electron pockets. Both Fe $t_{2g}$ and Se $4p$ orbitals contribute substantially to this multiorbital superconductivity. The calculated superconducting gap and density of states exhibit a two-peak structure, while the Josephson tunneling conductance displays a half-lattice-period modulation, in excellent agreement with experimental observations. Our results establish electronic charge-transfer fluctuations as a viable pairing mechanism for unconventional nodeless $d$-wave superconductivity in monolayer FeSe/STO.

</details>


### [8] [Three-Dimensional FDTD Simulation of Superconducting Terahertz Emitters Using an Effective Medium Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00840)
*Ryota Kobayashi,Itsuhiro Kakeya*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文从Lawrence-Doniach模型推导出各向异性色散有效介电常数，并将其作为用户自定义材料嵌入商用FDTD求解器，用于Bi2212超导THz发射器的器件级仿真与偏振设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑、可调且偏振可控的THz源对高速链路、光谱和成像很重要；现有Bi2212超导THz发射器建模依赖重非线性求解器或各向同性经验介电模型，难以准确描述各向异性、等离子体响应和偏置依赖。

Method: 基于层状超导体的Lawrence-Doniach模型建立各向异性、色散的有效介电常数，并在商用时域有限差分（FDTD）求解器中实现为用户自定义材料；对矩形台面和截角方形台面两种器件几何进行仿真。

Result: 仿真复现了矩形台面的线偏振发射以及截角方形台面的低轴比（近圆）偏振发射，表明该材料模型能够捕捉器件几何相关的偏振特性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了连接超导理论与器件设计的CAD就绪仿真流程，可用于下一代THz源的快速搜索和参数优化。

Abstract: Compact and tunable terahertz (THz) sources with controllable polarization are sought for high-speed links, spectroscopy, and imaging. In superconducting THz emitters based on a cuprate Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+\delta}$, which have the potential to satisfy such properties, modeling often relies on heavy nonlinear solvers or empirical isotropic dielectrics, obscuring anisotropy, plasma response, and bias dependence. We derive an anisotropic, dispersive effective permittivity from the Lawrence-Doniach model for a layered superconductor and implement it as a user-defined material in a commercial Finite-difference Time-domain (FDTD) solver. Simulations of two device geometries reproduce linearly polarized emission from a rectangular mesa and low-axial-ratio (near-circular) emission from a truncated square mesa. This CAD-ready workflow links superconducting theory to device design, enabling rapid searches and parameter optimization for next-generation THz sources.

</details>


### [9] [Imaging the Current Distribution in Superconducting Transition-Edge Sensors with Scanning SQUID Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02352)
*Samantha Walker,Austin Kaczmarek,Douglas A. Bennett,Jason E. Austermann,W. Bertrand Doriese,Shannon M. Duff,Johannes Hubmayr,Kelsey M. Morgan,Daniel R. Schmidt,Daniel S. Swetz,Joel N. Ullom,Joel Weber,Michael D. Niemack,Katja C. Nowack*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用扫描SQUID显微术直接成像MoAu X射线TES在转变区电压偏置下的局域超导态与传输电流分布，发现电流分布会随偏置重构并追踪超导区变化，表明介观空间不均匀性对TES物理与设计至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TES模型大多忽略器件几何及超导引线/正常金属邻近效应引起的超导态和电流分布空间变化，导致对偏置下TES行为理解不足。

Method: 采用扫描SQUID显微术研究两种MoAu X射线TES设计；通过测量局域抗磁响应识别电压偏置在转变区内器件的超导区域，并通过传输电流产生的Oersted场重建电流分布。

Result: 观察到偏置依赖的电流分布重配置，且该重构与电流诱导的超导区域变化相一致，揭示出显著的介观空间变化。

Conclusion: 空间分辨测量表明，TES器件物理、运行和设计需纳入超导态与电流分布的介观空间不均匀性。

Abstract: Transition-edge sensors (TESs) exploit the resistive transition of a superconductor to detect minute changes in temperature. While significant progress has been made in understanding the temperature- and current-dependent resistive transition in these devices, important aspects of TES behavior under bias remain unresolved. In particular, existing TES models largely neglect spatial variations in the superconducting state and current distribution arising from device geometry and proximity effects associated with the superconducting leads and normal metal features. Using scanning superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID) microscopy (SSM), we directly image local superconducting properties and transport current in two molybdenum-gold (MoAu) X-ray TES designs. We measure the local diamagnetic response to identify superconducting regions in devices voltage-biased within the transition and the Oersted field generated by the transport current to reconstruct the corresponding current distribution. We observe bias-dependent reconfigurations of the current distribution that track current-induced changes in superconducting regions, highlighting the importance of mesoscale spatial variations for TES device physics, operation, and design.

Abstract: A Cu-rich multielement alloy containing Zn, Sn, Ag, and Au was synthesized and studied to explore its structural and physical properties. The alloy was prepared by vacuum-sealed melting of high-purity elements followed by rapid quenching in cold water. Powder X-ray diffraction confirms that the alloy crystallizes in a face-centered cubic (fcc) Cu-type structure. Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 将YIG微球浸入液氦并用腔磁力力学读取机械模式，发现超流氦并未提供低损耗热浴：机械线宽从真空6.1 Hz增至正常液氦9.1 kHz、超流84 mK下17.5 kHz，品质因数下降逾三个数量级；原因是以声辐射形式向液氦发射无隙纵声波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温机械谐振器需要散热，但高Q弱支撑热导差，光/微波读控还会加热器件；超流氦兼具优良低温热输运和无粘性超流分量，因此被设想为不引入普通流体阻尼的冷却介质。

Result: 机械线宽由真空6.1 Hz升至正常液氦9.1 kHz，并在超流84 mK下达到17.5 kHz，Q降低超过三个数量级；进入超流态未恢复低耗散。低温下磁子线宽仍强烈依赖微波功率，说明球体并未完全锚定于低温恒温器。声辐射阻抗估算给出约25 kHz线宽，与表面运动高效激发液氦中无隙纵声波一致。

Conclusion: 超流氦不是对高效辐射压缩波的机械模式而言的低损耗热浴；剪切运动可能绕开该限制。

Abstract: Thermalization is a persistent problem for cryogenic mechanical resonators. The weak supports used to obtain high mechanical quality factors are also poor thermal links, while the optical or microwave fields used for readout and control can heat the resonator above the cryostat temperature. Superfluid helium is therefore an appealing exchange medium: it has excellent low-temperature thermal transport and an inviscid superfluid component, suggesting cooling of mechanical devices without the damping associated with an ordinary fluid. Here we test this idea by immersing a \qty{250}{\micro\meter}-diameter yttrium iron garnet sphere in liquid \textsuperscript{4}He and reading out a \(\sim\qty{12.47}{\mega\hertz}\) mechanical mode using cavity magnomechanics. The mechanical linewidth increases from \qty{6.1}{\hertz} in vacuum to \qty{9.1}{\kilo\hertz} after filling the cavity with liquid helium in the normal state, and reaches \qty{17.5}{\kilo\hertz} deep in the superfluid at \qty{84}{\milli\kelvin}. Thus the mechanical quality factor is reduced by more than three orders of magnitude, and crossing into the superfluid does not recover low dissipation. At the same time, the magnon linewidth remains strongly dependent on microwave power at the lowest temperatures, showing that the sphere is not perfectly anchored to the cryostat despite the large mechanical damping. The result follows from acoustic radiation: the surface motion of the sphere efficiently launches gapless longitudinal sound into the liquid, and a radiation-impedance estimate gives a linewidth of order \qty{25}{\kilo\hertz}. Superfluid helium is therefore not a low-loss thermal bath for mechanical modes that efficiently radiate compression waves. Shear motion, on the other hand, may provide a route around this limitation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序硼烯纳米带中的线缺陷可产生本征共振隧穿，是输运调控平台，但面内机械应变对其电子输运的影响尚需系统研究。

Method: 采用理论方法研究耦合金属电极的不同宽度无序硼烯纳米带在面内单轴与双轴机械应变下的电子输运性质。

Result: 共振峰对不同电极参数保持稳健；单轴应变显著调制共振峰能量、宽度和幅度，可选择性抑制共振峰并诱导新峰；纵向与横向应变引起相反能量移动，体现显著各向异性；不同宽度中重复出现的相同共振峰对应变响应一致；双轴应变可实现多离散电平的高效电导开关，且纳米带越宽调制频率越高。

Conclusion: 研究确立了缺陷-应变工程策略，可用于调控硼烯及其他无能隙材料的电子输运。

Abstract: Resonant tunneling intrinsically exists in disordered borophene nanoribbons (BNRs) with randomly distributed line defects (LDs), providing a versatile platform for transport modulation. Here, we theoretically investigate the effects of in-plane mechanical strain on the electronic transport properties of disordered BNRs coupled to metallic electrodes. Our results demonstrate that the resonant peaks in disordered BNRs of different widths remain robust against variations in electrode parameters and can be efficiently modulated via mechanical strain. Specifically, uniaxial strain significantly modulates the energies, widths, and amplitudes of these resonant peaks, and can even suppress the peaks selectively and induce new ones. In particular, the response of the resonant energies to the applied strain exhibits remarkable anisotropy, where strains along the longitudinal and transverse directions induce opposite energy shifts. Moreover, identical resonant peaks recurring in different nanoribbon widths respond uniformly to applied strain. Biaxial strain enables highly efficient conductance switching across multiple distinct levels, with the modulation frequency increasing for wider nanoribbons. Our findings establish a promising defect-strain-engineering strategy for manipulating electronic transport in borophene and other gapless materials.

</details>


### [13] [The Agentic AI Kerr Lab: LLM Agents for Kerr Microscopy Experiments with Magnetic Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00299)
*Kilian Leutner,Yuean Zhou,Aditya Kumar,Thomas B. Winkler,Robert Fr\"omter,Hans Fangohr,Mathias Kl\"aui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出AgentKerr：由大语言模型智能体自动操作Kerr显微镜，实现磁斯格明子的实时成像、分析与激发闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁斯格明子可被磁场和电流驱动并直接成像，是基础物理与自旋电子器件的模型体系；但Kerr显微实验通常依赖人工反复执行成像、分析和激发，效率低。

Method: 用LLM智能体控制Kerr显微镜；不依赖语言模型做图像模式识别，而用训练于Kerr图像的卷积神经网络将实时图像转换为斯格明子位置、尺寸和形状等可观测量，智能体据此决策；所有硬件操作均由确定性的动作策略独立校验以保证安全。

Result: 在未给定流程的情况下，智能体自主确定如何创建、调节和移动斯格明子，并自行编写分析。

Conclusion: 该方法可迁移至其他自旋纹理，指向由研究者提出问题、智能体自主设计并实现实验以获取答案的磁成像实验范式。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are topological spin textures that can be observed directly by magnetic microscopy and driven by fields and currents, which makes them a model system for fundamental physics and candidates for spintronic devices. Magneto-optical Kerr microscopy is a method to image them in real time; however, most experiments use an inefficient cycle of consecutive imaging, analysis and excitation in which every step requires the judgement of the experimentalist operating the setup. Here we present the Agentic AI Kerr Lab (AgentKerr), in which an agent based on a large language model operates this cycle automatically and efficiently on a Kerr microscope. Rather than relying on pattern detection by a language model, a convolutional neural network trained on Kerr images turns live images into skyrmion observables such as position, size and shape, on which the agent bases its decisions. The architecture lets agents operate the instruments safely, as every hardware action is checked deterministically against an action policy, independently of the agent that issues it. With no procedure given, the agent determines by itself how to create, tune and move skyrmions, and writes its own analyses. The approach can be transferred to other spin textures and points towards magnetic imaging experiments in which the researcher poses the question and the agent finds the way to realize the experiments that yield the answer.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索电隔离铁磁体之间由磁振子介导的非局域电拖拽，并区分量子偶极拖拽与常规拖拽机制。

Abstract: We predict electrical drag between two electrically isolated metallic ferromagnets mediated by quantum dipolar magnon coupling. The resulting magnon current in the passive layer produces a measurable voltage through electron-magnon scattering. We show the combination of resonant quantum momentum transfer and magnetic tunability through the control of magnetization configurations distinguish quantum dipolar drag from conventional drag mechanism.

Conclusion: 该CoZn薄膜在接近器件应用的厚度下保持手性螺旋磁序；贝叶斯模型选择可可靠解析PNR数据并区分复杂磁态。

Abstract: Chiral magnetic materials can host skyrmions and hence are of interest for numerous spintronic applications including low-power storage. We have studied the chiral magnetism in a $\beta$-Mn structured CoZn sputtered film, with a Curie temperature of $\approx 130$ {\deg}C, by means of polarized neutron reflectometry (PNR) in order to reveal its magnetic field-temperature phase diagram. In bulk this material has a helimagnetic ground state. Here we use PNR data to show that the helimagnetism survives in a CoZn film only 100 nm thick, roughly half a helical period. Given the subtlety of differentiating the helical state from a uniform state with reduced interfacial magnetism, we have developed and employed a Bayesian approach that uses model selection to analyze our PNR data and to map out the field-temperature phase diagram of our thin film.

TL;DR: 本文用全组态相互作用（FCI）评估库仑相互作用对Si量子点中五电子pO电荷量子比特可行性的影响；结果表明，尽管轨道占据显著偏离壳层填充图像，在谷劈裂 E_VS > 50 μeV 时，无偶极矩和非零四极矩等关键特性仍得以保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始pO量子比特方案基于无库仑相互作用的壳层填充图像，需要检验库仑相互作用是否会破坏其抑制退相干并保留全电控的关键优势。

Result: 相互作用本征态会占据壳层填充图像之外的轨道壳层，激发程度由谷劈裂、轨道能量与库仑能的竞争决定；基态形成简并双峰，并在 E_VS≈50 μeV 附近发生交叉：高场侧为下谷中 |⟨L_z⟩|=1 的轨道角动量激发对，低场侧为 ⟨L_z⟩=0 的单谷激发对。尽管偏离壳层图像，E_VS > 50 μeV 时量子比特态间消失的偶极矩和非零四极张量仍保留；五电子哈密顿量的对称性保证偶极矩消失，而四极张量元素和第一激发态能隙由同一组能标决定并可通过器件工程调控。

Conclusion: 库仑相互作用会导致显著的能级重构，但在 E_VS > 50 μeV 区域pO量子比特的核心物理特性仍稳健；其无偶极耦合受对称性保护，四极耦合与能隙具备器件可调性。

Abstract: The $pO$ qubit is a recently proposed charge qubit consisting of five electrons confined in a gate-defined Si quantum dot. Because its two qubit states have no dipole moment, it couples to electric fields only through the quadrupole moment, suppressing the dominant decoherence channel of conventional charge qubits while retaining all-electrical control. The original proposal was formulated in a shell-filling picture without Coulomb interactions. In this work we perform full configuration interaction (FCI) calculations to assess the impact of the Coulomb interaction on the viability of the $pO$ qubit. We find that the interacting eigenstates occupy orbital shells beyond the shell-filling picture, with the degree of excitation governed by the competition between valley splitting, orbital energy, and Coulomb energy scales. Additionally, we find that the ground states form degenerate doublets, but of two distinct kinds. Above a crossover near a valley splitting of $E_{\rm{VS}}\approx50~\mu$eV the doublet is a pair of orbital angular momentum excitations with $|\langle L_z\rangle|=1$ in the lower valley, while below the crossover the doublet is a pair of single valley excitations with $\langle L_z\rangle=0$. Despite this substantial departure from the shell-filling picture, for $E_{\rm{VS}} > 50~\mu$eV the essential features, vanishing dipole moments and nonzero quadrupole tensor between qubit states are preserved. We show that the vanishing dipole is in fact guaranteed by the symmetry properties of the five-electron Hamiltonian. The same energy scales that govern the orbital occupation also set the quadrupole tensor elements and the first excited state energy gap, both of which are in principle tunable through device engineering.

</details>

Abstract: Three-dimensional spin textures are stabilized by microscopic interactions within their host materials. Here we show that a reconstruction constrained by the micromagnetic energy recovers the three-dimensional magnetization from such data and gains sensitivity to the underlying material interactions. Applied to the chiral magnet Co$_8$Zn$_9$Mn$_3$, the method resolves the three-dimensional helical texture even from a small fraction of the dual-axis projections typically required for a successful tomographic reconstruction, or from a single tilt axis alone. By testing which ratio of exchange to Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction in the reconstruction best reproduces the measured projections, we obtain a value for the sample that differs by about 10 % from the literature value derived from the helical winding period. Taken together, our findings suggest a way to reduce the time needed for vector tomography and to extend it to determining fundamental magnetic interactions in a material.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现电可控的量子化谷源，并利用双层石墨烯中QSH与QVH效应及螺旋边缘态来生成纯谷流。

Method: 对耦合到双层石墨烯纳米带上的两个量子点栅压进行周期性调制，通过绝热量子泵浦发射谷对；并分析量子点几何、静态栅压及量子点-纳米带耦合的影响。

Abstract: We propose a quantized valley source based on adiabatic quantum pumping in bilayer graphene in the presence of both quantum spin Hall and quantum valley Hall effects. By periodically modulating the gate voltages of two quantum dots coupled to a bilayer graphene nanoribbon, the helical edge states enable the generation of a pure valley current. Abstract: Quantum dots are electrically tuneable thermal machines capable of both energy harvesting and cooling at the nanoscale in the cryogenic regime. Though these asymmetries and nonlinearities directly affect rectification and power conversion, their origin has not been studied systematically. Using Pauli master equation for sequential tunnelling through a two-level dot, retaining the full Fermi distributions of both leads, distinct tunnel rates for the two levels, and relaxation between them, we show that the excited state acts as a second transport channel which competes with the ground-state contribution. Strong excited-state coupling combined with slow relaxation produces polarity reversals in the thermocurrent; without both, the excited state yields only additional extrema and an amplitude asymmetry across the charge degeneracy point. Experimentally, as the source temperature increases, an additional extremum emerges in the thermocurrent which develops into a polarity reversal. Using the excited-state level spacing and ground-state tunnel rate obtained independently from pulsed-gate and bias spectroscopies, the model reproduces the positions of the observed extrema and their amplitudes to within a factor of about three, placing the device in the regime of slow relaxation and strong excited-state coupling. Abstract: Liouvillian skin effects are usually associated with dissipation whose rates favor unidirectional flow. In this work, we show that directional edge localization can emerge even when population exchange between sites is exactly balanced, with a single tunable phase parameter $\alpha$ deciding their direction and a dual winding structure deciding their nature. Although this number-conserving bath is known for eigenstate selection, we demonstrate here that its phase controls a steady current, which Hamiltonian hopping converts to directional edge localization. Further, we study the spectral winding structure by independently twisting the left (ket) and right (bra) vector indices of the density matrix, revealing two distinct windings whose relationship is dictated by an antiunitary mirror symmetry. Nearest-neighbor jump operators break this symmetry: both windings coincide, giving rise to a common spectral winding $W_0(\alpha)=\pm1$, producing a unipolar skin effect in which the density accumulates at a specific edge. However, next nearest neighbor jump preserve it. The two windings are forced to be opposite, and the ket and bra are pushed to opposite edges, giving a bipolar skin effect of coherences present in eigenmodes, without edge localization of the density in the steady state. Abstract: Anisotropic semiconductors offer unique opportunities for next-generation electronics due to their direction-dependent transport. However, conventional electrical measurements provide only spatially averaged characteristics. Here, we employ bias-dependent Kelvin probe force microscopy (KPFM) to directly visualize the response of CrSBr under different electrical bias configurations. Spatially resolved KPFM maps reveal highly distinct, crystallographic-direction-dependent potential profiles. To elucidate this behavior, we employ anisotropic transport simulations that capture the charge transport and electric-field distributions in CrSBr. The close agreement between experiment and simulation provides a unified picture of anisotropic charge transport and bias-induced potential redistribution. Our experiments further reveal a boundary-associated redistribution of the transport field when bias is applied along the $a$-axis, suggesting a possible role of crystal boundaries in charge transport along the low-conductivity direction. Abstract: Artificial spin ices (ASIs) are arrays of interacting nanomagnets in which phase transitions can be imaged and controlled. Studies of the thermodynamic have focused mainly on the square and kagome lattices, and on their variants. Here we study artificial spin ice on the CaVO lattice, formed of squares and octagons. At fixed lattice constant, the ratio $r$ of the square and octagon link lengths tunes the interactions strongly while changing the geometry only slightly. Magnetic force microscopy and Monte Carlo simulations show a continuous bridge from square-ice-like to kagome-like behavior. At small $r$ the system has a single ordering transition, whereas at large $r$ it passes from a correlated paramagnet to charge order and then long-range order. The two regimes meet in a multicritical region, accurately modeled by a Blume-Capel model of the octagon toroidal moments. From this picture, we design a comparative magnetometry protocol that unambiguously separates interaction effects from single-nanomagnet effects in thermally active samples. Finally, the charge-ordered region maps onto a tunable dimer model, with a Coulomb phase and pinch points along the line between two ordered dimer phases. The CaVO ASI thus provides access to a rich phase diagram only through geometric tuning, without requiring extreme experimental conditions.

Abstract: We present an exact solution of the linearised semiclassical Boltzmann equation (SBE) beyond the relaxation-time approximation for multifold semimetals under arbitrarily oriented electric and magnetic fields. The framework treats the Berry curvature, orbital magnetic moment, Lorentz-force operator, and scattering terms on equal footing. Momentum-independent scattering-amplitudes couple the Fermi-surface sheets through intranode, interband, and internode processes. The scattering kernel has a finite rank and the SBE reduces to a linear system for a few moments, which is closed by the electron-number constraint. We apply this approach to four systems involving quadratic-corrected triple-point, Weyl, and Rarita-Schwinger-Weyl nodes, including tilted triple-point nodes. We obtain analytical results at zero field and to leading orders in the magnetic field, alongside numerical solutions for the full field dependence. Our results elucidate the roles of the topological properties, Lorentz-force operator, tilt, and intra- and internode scatterings in shaping the magnetoelectric conductivity. It cures severe shortcomings of previous treatments like incomplete ans\"atze and incomplete treatment of the Lorentz-force operator.

Both systems host multiple concentric Fermi surfaces at positive chemical potential. Within the random-phase approximation, we analyse the density response, Drude weights, dielectric function, plasmon modes and their Landau damping, and Friedel oscillations, considering both small and arbitrary momentum transfers. In our companion work, arXiv:2610.18629, we investigated pseudospin-1 triple-point semimetals, with and without a quadratic correction to their dispersion, as well as Hopf and Weyl semimetals. Comparing these systems allows us to elucidate the distinct roles of band dispersions and eigenstates in shaping collective charge dynamics. Our results demonstrate that collective charge excitations can distinguish multifold nodes with multiple Fermi surfaces and reveal information encoded in their eigenstates beyond that contained in the single-particle dispersion.

Abstract: Hybrid moir\'e excitons form a dense array of electrically tunable single-photon emitters, entailing cooperative optical responses with intriguing consequences. With the strong dipole-dipole interaction, they also realize Bose-Hubbard quantum simulators of emergent correlated matter such as Bose-Einstein condensates (BEC). Abstract: Chiral phonons carry angular momentum and couple lattice dynamics to magnetic and transport phenomena. We further show that a temperature gradient generates a nonequilibrium PAM density whose $n$th-order term in $\nabla T$ contains an energy-weighted PAM $2^n$-pole tensor and a kinematic remainder, thereby linking the response order and partial-wave channel through exact selection rules. Because the Berry curvature obeys the same symmetry constraints, this taxonomy is in one-to-one correspondence with Berry-curvature multipoles and their nonlinear Hall responses. We propose a cantilever torque measurement whose allowed angular harmonics are fixed by the MPG, with period $2\pi/n$ for a pure $n$th harmonic, providing a direct probe of the partial-wave content of PAM in both nonmagnetic and magnetic materials.

Abstract: Spin-wave-based information processing provides a promising platform for low-power nanoscale computing by exploiting coherent magnon dynamics and intrinsic nonlinear interactions. Abstract: We study the electron--phonon heat-current fluctuations in monolayer and Bernal bilayer graphene coupled to longitudinal acoustic phonons. Further, we obtain full analytical expressions in the Bloch--Gr\"uneisen (BG) and Equipartition (EP) regimes, derive their low-frequency limits, and compare them with a complete doped-intraband on-shell calculations for monolayer graphene. When the RF trajectory approaches or crosses a charge transition, the damping-like torque (DLT) associated with spin-polarized charge transfer is strongly enhanced. By contrast, the field-like torque (FLT) induced by the exchange field remains finite deeper in the Coulomb-blockade (CB) regime because it originates from virtual charge fluctuations. These regimes arise within selected eigenstate sectors, where localized states coexist with extended or critical states, leading to mobility-edge-like behavior. Finite-size scaling confirms the persistence of the re-entrant features, while the complex-energy spectrum resolves their state-selective structure. The resulting phase boundaries delineate distinct localization regimes in the $(W,\lambda)$ plane. Abstract: Generative models for crystal structure prediction (CSP) have expanded the search space for novel materials and improved screening efficiency. However, simultaneously generating stable and novel structures remains challenging because of the complex structural and physical constraints governing crystals. ST2M represents crystal structures as sequences of independent occupied Wyckoff orbits and employs a twin-branch Transformer to jointly denoise discrete and continuous variables. In our experiments, ST2M achieves a structural validity of 96.37\%. For known patterns in dataset, 23.86\% of generated structures simultaneously satisfy stability, uniqueness, and novelty criteria, representing a relative improvement of 68.15\% over the best-performing baseline. First-principles calculations identify a total of 21 previously unreported stable structures among selected candidates with low predicted energies. These results demonstrate that ST2M can generate chemically viable crystal candidates under prescribed crystallographic constraints and generalize to unknown Wyckoff patterns, providing a practical route to accelerated materials discovery.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The shear piezoelectric coefficient $e_{15}$ governs the shear-mode electromechanical coupling in piezoelectric micromachined ultrasonic transducers (PMUTs) and surface acoustic wave (SAW) devices, but measuring it typically requires destructive resonance testing on dedicated specimens. On simulated data, $e_{15}$ can instead be recovered non-destructively from sparse, noisy subsurface wavefield observations using a constrained physics-informed neural network (PINN). By building exact spatial distance functions and temporal multipliers directly into the network output, the clamped, grounded boundaries and resting initial state hold automatically, leaving the loss with only the interior equations of coupled electro-elastodynamics and the data misfit. Non-dimensional scaling brings all governing coefficients within one decade of O(1), and the setup removes the boundary and initial-condition penalty weights that soft-constraint methods must tune. In a six-way comparison (plane-strain PZT-5H, manufactured wavefield, 30 dB noise), a soft-penalty baseline with adaptive weights converges to an erroneous $e_{15}$ in four of the five runs (51.7% mean error), and fixed penalties reach an error of 0.85% only after a second-order optimization stage. The constrained formulations reach mean $e_{15}$ errors between 0.15% and 0.51% with held-out displacement errors below 1%. Within these variants, the spatial multiplier gives rapid first-order convergence, and the quadratic temporal multiplier lowers the early-time electrostatic residual by a factor of 6.8 versus a linear one. On finite-element data along one subsurface line, the full formulation recovers $e_{15}$ with 1.19% mean error at 30 dB and keeps it between 0.65% and 2.71% across a 40 to 0 dB noise sweep. Finally, training on displacements alone still recovers $e_{15}$ with mean errors of 0.45% on finite-element and 1.64% on manufactured data.

Abstract: We introduce ROSE, a deep-learning exchange--correlation energy functional built from the occupied-state density matrix.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 该推进复现平行和垂直波色散（含离子Bernstein模）；在显式推进出现长期加热的情形下总能量守恒至机器舍入误差；不产生冷束有限网格不稳定性；在RZ几何中复现Bennett pinch的m=0不稳定性；并用于从Grad-Shafranov平衡初始化的3D场反位形模拟，比较显式与隐式推进对tilt不稳定性增长和俘获磁通保持的影响。

Abstract: Hybrid particle-in-cell (PIC) models with kinetic ions and an inertialess electron fluid are widely used to simulate magnetized plasmas at ion scales, but explicit time advances are constrained by the whistler-wave CFL condition $\dt \propto \Delta x^2$, and the sub-cycling and artificial dissipation used to mitigate it carry accumulating costs. The electron pressure is advanced inside the nonlinear solve, and the scheme satisfies a discrete energy law whose single dissipation term vanishes at $\theta = 1/2$ and damps only temporally under-resolved modes for $\theta > 1/2$. The advance reproduces parallel and perpendicular wave dispersion including the ion Bernstein modes, conserves total energy to machine roundoff where the explicit advance heats secularly, does not develop the cold-beam finite-grid instability, and reproduces the $m=0$ instability of a Bennett pinch in RZ geometry. Abstract: The efficient and accurate computation of transport coefficients remains a numerical challenge in dissipative particle dynamics (DPD), which can require substantial sampling to control statistical uncertainty. We extend recent results obtained in Langevin dynamics to consider the transient subtraction method for reducing the variance of transport coefficient estimations in DPD. Numerical comparisons with the naive transient estimator show that the transient subtraction method reduces the variance at various integration times, and the reduction is more pronounced with a higher friction coefficient. These results indicate that the transient subtraction method is able to improve the statistical precision of transport coefficient estimations in DPD and reduce the number of trajectories needed to attain a prescribed standard error.

Abstract: We develop a structure-preserving full approximation scheme (FAS) for the real-space restricted Hartree-Fock equations. Forced Gram targets, occupied-frame alignment, and auxiliary-variable reconstruction provide constraint compatibility and occupied-rotation equivariance under suitable assumptions. Analysis of the full self-consistent response identifies an elliptic kinetic principal part, motivating the multilevel treatment. The resulting solver, FAS-H, combines FAS cycles with a residual-triggered horizontal Newton correction on the finest grid. Numerical experiments on eight closed-shell systems show convergence in 4-11 cycles across three grid levels. Across 24 system-grid comparisons, FAS-H is 1.60-5.02 times faster than SCF and uses 68-91% less weighted FFT work, while standalone horizontal Newton is faster in some cases.

</details>

Abstract: This study develops and implements a Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd (HHL) quantum algorithm for solving a one-dimensional linear advection equation with periodic boundary conditions. The governing equation is first discretized using a classical finite difference (FD) scheme to obtain a linear system suitable for quantum solution. Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条从微观物理定律系统构建能量稳定宏观PDE的变分学习路径，实现了跨尺度定律级迁移，并在复杂非牛顿流体黏弹性建模中验证了有效性。

Abstract: We present a generalized variational approach for constructing energy-stable macroscopic partial differential equations (PDEs) directly from microscopic physical laws. Rather than fitting the PDE evolution for prescribed variables from macroscopic trajectories, we construct an extendable variational structure by adjoining auxiliary variables to seek autonomous reduced dynamics in an enlarged state space. By defining these variables as micro--macro encoders with explicit microscopic counterparts, we cast the learning problem as the sample-wise joint identification of the reduced state representation and the variational ingredients from time-discrete data, enabling systematic law-level transfer across scales. The resulting PDEs preserve frame indifference, physical constraints, and energy stability by construction, and admit a fluctuation--dissipation-consistent stochastic extension. Numerical results for the non-Newtonian hydrodynamics of polymeric fluids show that the learned models capture the viscoelastic responses induced by nonlinear and heterogeneous molecular interactions beyond empirical closures.

</details>

*Hoang Anh Nguyen,Ali Tura*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文给出一阶声波与弹性波方程在开放域中的量子电路：用梯度海绵层阻尼所有波场，并通过Schrödingerisation将耗散动力学映射为酉演化；在1–3维从PDE推导到电路，并验证吸收效果与门级模拟结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于一阶形式哈密顿模拟的量子电路保持能量守恒，因而只描述闭域： outgoing波会在计算区域边界反射，周期边界下还会从另一侧回绕。需要以物理吸收层打开域，同时保持量子实现可行。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从声波/弹性波PDE到开放边界耗散量子电路的完整构造，证明海绵层在量子框架下具有精确且高效的分析性质，并给出多项式门复杂度的阻尼步骤分解，为开放域波动方程的量子模拟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Explicit quantum circuits for the acoustic and elastic wave equations, obtained by Hamiltonian simulation of their first-order forms, conserve energy and therefore describe closed domains: an outgoing wave reflects off the edge of the computational region or, with periodic boundaries, wraps around and re-enters from the opposite side. We open the domain with the simplest physical absorber, a graded sponge layer in which every wave field is damped at a rate that grows towards the outer edge, and implement the resulting dissipative dynamics on a quantum simulator by Schr\"odingerisation, which turns a non-unitary linear evolution into a family of unitary ones by means of a single auxiliary variable. We derive the whole chain from the partial differential equation to the circuit, in one, two and three dimensions. An independent finite-difference solver confirms that damping all wave fields absorbs far better than the common velocity-only damping mask. For the sponge the quantum analysis is sharp: recovery is exact on the whole non- negative half of the auxiliary axis at every time; that axis can be truncated periodically at a length set by the largest damping rate and the simulated time; and the success probability is a fixed constant, about 0.4, times the fraction of the energy not yet absorbed, with only one or two auxiliary qubits measured. On the circuit side, the damping step, which generic synthesis compiles into a number of controlled-NOT gates exponential in the number of qubits, factorises exactly into polynomially many multi-controlled phase gates. Gate-level simulations on up to 25 qubits agree with the classical solutions of the damped wave equations.

</details>


### [57] [Maximal correlation and optimal transformations of probability distributions in tensor-train format: a variational algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02731)
*Kayo Kinjo,Jun Ohkubo*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种张量列（矩阵乘积）格式下的变分SVD算法，用于计算离散经典分布（如自旋模型Boltzmann分布）的Hirschfeld-Gebelein-Rényi最大相关及最优变换，无需显式构造联合分布归一化矩阵，并可扩展至超大构型空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统子系统关联需预先选择一对可观测量；HGR最大相关通过在所有函数对上取最大相关系数消除该选择，但其计算依赖联合分布按边缘归一化矩阵的SVD，矩阵维度为构型数，直接计算不可行。

Method: 将联合分布以张量列/矩阵乘积格式表示，把最优变换也参数化为张量列；每次仅更新一个核心，闭式求解，并利用由分布与其余核心收缩得到的小矩阵逆逐核心完成按边缘归一化。每步迭代均为可行函数对，因此目标值是最大相关的单调递增下界，计算代价随变量数线性增长。

Result: 算法在小系统上复现精确SVD；在最多十个角度变量、10^15个构型的合成分布上恢复已知非线性最优变换；在具有2^32个构型的二分反铁磁模型上，得到的最优变换是总磁化的函数：转变温度以上呈线性，以下呈符号型，从而由分布直接读出交错序参量而非预先假定。

Conclusion: 该工作给出可扩展的变分张量列SVD方法，使HGR最大相关及最优可观测量可在超大离散分布上计算，并能从数据/分布中识别序参量和非线性关联结构。

Abstract: The correlation between two subsystems $A$ and $B$ of a many-body system is usually measured through a pair of observables chosen in advance. The Hirschfeld-Gebelein-R\'enyi maximal correlation removes the choice: it is the largest correlation coefficient between any function of the configuration of $A$ and any function of the configuration of $B$, and the two functions that reach it, the optimal transformations, are the observables that carry the correlation. Both follow from a singular value decomposition (SVD) of the joint distribution normalized by its marginals, a matrix whose dimension is the number of configurations. We show that this SVD can be computed variationally for a classical distribution of discrete variables, such as the Boltzmann distribution of a spin model, given in the tensor-train (matrix-product) format, without ever forming the matrix. The optimal transformations are sought as tensor trains; each step updates a single core in closed form, and the inverse of a small matrix contracted from the distribution and the remaining cores performs the division by the marginals core by core. Every iterate is a feasible pair, so the objective is a lower bound on the maximal correlation that increases monotonically, and the cost grows linearly with the number of variables. The algorithm reproduces the exact SVD on small systems, recovers the known nonlinear optimal transformations of synthetic distributions of up to ten angular variables ($10^{15}$ configurations), and, for the bipartite antiferromagnetic model with $2^{32}$ configurations, returns an optimal transformation that is a function of the group magnetization, linear above the transition and sign-like below it: the staggered order parameter is read off from the distribution instead of being postulated.

</details>


### [58] [Noise-resilient collective order through temporal memory in self-propelled particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03003)
*Mihir Durve,Sauro Succi,Antonio Celani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究了Vicsek型集体运动中时间记忆在抑制噪声与阻碍适应之间的权衡，并指出记忆深度是区分稳态有序性与动态恢复能力的控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间记忆可通过过滤局部噪声稳定集体运动，但持续保留旧信息也会在集体状态变化时妨碍适应；作者旨在刻画这一竞争机制。

Method: 在Vicsek型系统中比较两种对齐规则：有限历史观测缓冲区，以及内部方向状态的递归更新；并以基于核的有效记忆参数化比较稳态与瞬态响应。

Result: 增加有效记忆会使极性有序的失序转变移到更强噪声处，并可在无记忆Vicsek模型失序条件下恢复有序；两模型稳态响应在核有效记忆参数化下近似对应，但瞬态动力学不由有效记忆单独决定。集体扰动后，恢复在中等记忆深度最快，过度保留过时观测会变慢。

Conclusion: 时间整合存在权衡：更深记忆提高噪声鲁棒性，过度持久性降低响应性；记忆深度可作为分离稳态有序优化与动态恢复优化的控制参数。

Abstract: Temporal memory can stabilise collective motion by filtering noisy local observations, but persistent information can also impede adaptation when the collective state changes. We investigate this competition in Vicsek-type systems using two distinct alignment rules: a finite buffer of past observations and a recursive update of an internal directional state. In both models, increasing effective memory shifts the loss of polar order to stronger noise and can restore ordered motion under conditions for which the corresponding memoryless Vicsek model is disordered. The stationary responses of the two models show an approximate correspondence when parameterised by a kernel-based effective memory, consistent with temporal averaging playing a common role in their ordering behaviour rather than from the details of the memory implementation. Their transient dynamics, however, are not determined by effective memory alone. Following a collective perturbation, recovery is fastest at an intermediate memory depth and becomes slower when outdated observations are retained too strongly. Temporal integration therefore produces a trade-off: deeper memory improves noise resilience, whereas excessive persistence reduces responsiveness. These results identify memory depth as a control parameter that separates the optimisation of stationary order from that of dynamical recovery in noisy active systems.

</details>


### [59] [Fracture-Bench: A Controlled Benchmark Suite for Machine Learning Surrogates in Phase-Field Fracture Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03473)
*Meenu Krishnan U.,Maryam Hakimzadeh,Indrashish Saha,Meer Mehran Rashid,Lori Graham-Brady,Somdatta Goswami*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出 Fracture-Bench 相场断裂机器学习代理模型基准，用两个固定划分的相场数据集系统比较 FNO、DeepONet、Latent DeepONet、UNet 与条件扩散模型的精度、训练/推理时间和模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂的机器学习代理模型常被用于不确定性量化、反演与设计等多次查询任务，但缺乏受控证据说明哪些架构可靠、适用于哪类断裂问题以及在训练数据之外的外推能力如何。

Method: 构建两个相场数据集：Dataset 1 为 1,000 个 AT2 超弹性板缺口孔多裂纹模拟，随机非均匀双轴位移边界，44 个载荷步，128×128 网格；Dataset 2 为 4,000 个 PF-CZM 预裂纹板含两个夹杂模拟，夹杂刚度分别为基体的十倍或更软，128×256 网格。在相同划分上训练 FNO、DeepONet、Latent DeepONet、UNet 和条件扩散模型，并比较每个数据集一个测试指标、训练时间、推理时间与模型大小。

Result: Dataset 1 上 FNO 与 Latent DeepONet 平均测试误差约 10^-6，约为 UNet 和 DeepONet 的十分之一。Dataset 2 上扩散模型最准确，误差约为 UNet 的六分之一，但单次预测需 706 s，而 UNet 仅 6 ms。所有架构的大部分误差出现在裂纹局部化后的最后载荷步；Dataset 2 中存在初始裂纹间距小于一个网格单元却产生定性不同路径的案例。

Conclusion: 架构排名依赖具体任务，密集采样输入上的平均误差难以反映近分岔案例；数据、代码、划分、物理 regime 描述与泛化划分均公开，并提供纳入混合模式、三维及实验配对断裂等欠代表 regime 的协议。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogates are increasingly proposed to replace phase-field fracture simulations in many-query tasks such as uncertainty quantification, inverse analysis and design. Little controlled evidence shows which architectures are reliable, for which fracture problems and how far beyond their training data. We introduce Fracture-Bench, an open benchmark of two phase-field datasets with fixed splits, common test metrics and a description of each dataset's physical regime. Dataset~1 contains 1,000 AT2 simulations of multiple cracks growing from a notched hole in a hyperelastic plate under random non-uniform biaxial boundary displacements (44 load steps, $128\times128$ grid). Dataset~2 contains 4,000 PF-CZM simulations of a pre-cracked plate with two inclusions, each ten times stiffer or more compliant than the matrix ($128\times256$ grid). We train a Fourier neural operator (FNO), DeepONet, Latent DeepONet, a UNet and a conditional diffusion model on identical splits and compare one test metric per dataset, training time, inference time and model size. On Dataset~1, FNO and Latent DeepONet attain mean test errors of order $10^{-6}$, about ten times lower than UNet and DeepONet. On Dataset~2, the diffusion model is most accurate, with about one sixth of the UNet error, but requires \SI{706}{s} per prediction versus \SI{6}{ms} for UNet. For all architectures, most of the error occurs in the final load steps, after crack localization. Dataset~2 contains initial cracks less than one grid cell apart that produce qualitatively different paths. Architecture rankings therefore depend on the task, and errors averaged over densely sampled inputs reveal little about near-bifurcation cases. Data, codes, splits, regime descriptors and generalization splits are public, with a protocol for adding under-represented regimes such as mixed-mode, three-dimensional and experimentally paired fracture.

</details>

### [60] [Fast Neural Noise Stabilizes Associative Memory against Slow Synaptic Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00516)
*Tomoki Kurikawa,Kunihiko Kaneko*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 快速神经波动与慢速突触变异对联想记忆结构鲁棒性的影响不对称：突触噪声损害记忆形成，而神经噪声增强网络对结构扰动的稳定性；在序列学习中，平均回忆性能在中等神经噪声幅度处达到最优，而突触噪声增加会迅速降低记忆性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经系统在多时间尺度随机动力学下运行，却能可靠维持稳定功能。本文旨在探究快速神经波动与慢速突触变异如何不对称地影响联想记忆的结构鲁棒性。

Method: 采用绝热平均场框架，推导稳定性因子的解析稳态分布，以分析神经噪声与突触噪声对联想记忆稳定性和序列学习性能的影响。

Result: 突触噪声会退化记忆形成；神经噪声则使网络对结构扰动更稳定。对于多记忆的序列学习，平均回忆性能在中等神经噪声幅度处最大化，而增加突触噪声会迅速恶化记忆性能。理论表明，快速神经波动使系统远离边缘稳定性。

Conclusion: 快速神经波动通过将系统推离边缘稳定性，成为系统稳定性不可或缺的前提；神经噪声与突触噪声在联想记忆结构鲁棒性中具有不对称且关键的作用。

Abstract: Neural systems operate under multi-timescale stochastic dynamics, yet reliably maintain stable functions. We investigate how fast neural fluctuations and slow synaptic variability asymmetrically influence the structural robustness of associative memories. While synaptic noise degrades memory formation, neural noise stabilizes the network against structural perturbations. For sequential learning of multiple memories, the average recall performance is maximized at an intermediate neural-noise amplitude, whereas increasing synaptic noise rapidly degrades memory performance. Using an adiabatic mean-field framework, we derive an analytical stationary distribution of stability factors. Our theory reveals that fast neural fluctuations push the system away from marginal stability, serving as an indispensable prerequisite for systemic stability.

</details>


### [61] [Informative Fisher Modes in Neural Networks as a Result of Explicit Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00745)
*Ibrahim Talha Ersoy,Bj\"orn Ladewig,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 论文提出“任务相关的偶然对称性”是除架构对称性之外的另一条组织原则：显式破缺这些对称性会产生非平坦 Hessian 方向，并决定哪些模式携带任务信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的损失景观高维非凸，Hessian 曲率决定学习动力学；架构对称性只能解释平坦方向，尚不清楚剩余自由度中是否还有组织原则。

Method: 作者定义任务相关的 contingent symmetries，在可解析最小模型中隔离该对称性；通过调节 L2 惩罚使破缺特征可测；并分析加噪数据导致的 BBP 转变，结合线性网络解析与深度线性网络数值实验。

Result: 显式破缺 contingent symmetries 会产生非平坦学习方向；L2 惩罚可暴露破缺信号；加噪使模型经历一系列破缺对称性恢复的 BBP 转变；该机制能识别携带任务特定信息的 Hessian 模式。

Conclusion: contingent symmetry 及其破缺机制为理解深度网络学习动力学提供了可测量的组织原则，且无需显式对称群描述，有望推广到非线性网络和其他学习系统。

Abstract: Learning in a neural network takes place on a high dimensional, non-convex loss landscape, the Hessian curvature of which dictates the learning dynamics. Despite its high dimensionality, the large number of architectural symmetries explains some of the geometric of this landscape, in particular flat directions, i.e. near-zero modes of the Hessian. These architectural symmetries have therefore been suggested as an organizing principle of deep learning. It remains an open question, though, wether there are additional organizing principles in the remaining degrees of freedom in the learning dynamic. Here we identify task-dependent contingent symmetries as such an additional organizing principle. We show that non-flat learning directions emerge due to explicit breaking of these contingent symmetries. We isolate this symmetry in an analytically solvable minimal model. Furthermore, we show that, by tuning an L2 penalty, signatures of the symmetry breaking become measurable. As a consequence of the same mechanism, adding noise to the data drives the model through a sequence of broken symmetry recoveries in the form of Baik-Ben-Arous-P\'ech\'e (BBP) transitions. We demonstrate analytically for linear and numerically for deep linear networks that the contingent symmetry group remains explicit, and that the symmetry-breaking mechanism identifies which Hessian modes carry task-specific information. These signatures are measurable without a symmetry-group description and, hence, offer a route to study symmetry breaking as a more general principle in nonlinear networks and other learning systems.

</details>

*Wei Li,Yong Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在开放双曲{7,3}格点上，Comtet无序的近场方差集中并不单由衰减长度决定；波函数参与度对无序协方差有依赖，并随无序强度出现符号反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负曲率使静电格林函数指数衰减，即使径向体积指数增长仍产生有限的无序方差预算；作者检验这种近场集中是否同样控制开放双曲格点上的波函数参与度。

Method: 构造保持Comtet无序逐点单格点分布但去除非对角协方差的高斯对照；在N=847、2240、5887及15435的{7,3}图上计算完整窗口参与比、尺寸分辨平方核半径、距离壳权重、高阶参与矩与能级间距比，并用边缘匹配指数核族比较。

Result: 参与比有效指数差在W/t=0.5为-0.028、W/t=3为+0.116，扩展尺寸后符号保持；较大图平方核半径约2.45ℓ，低于截断匹配连续值3.12ℓ；距离壳权重解释该偏移，指数核族在五个核长度上均给出正指数对比。

Conclusion: 结果区分了集中方差预算与有限开放双曲系统中依赖协方差的参与响应，表明Comtet无序响应不能仅用衰减长度描述。

Abstract: Negative curvature makes the electrostatic Green function decay exponentially, producing a finite disorder-variance budget even as radial volume grows exponentially. We investigate whether this near-field concentration also determines wave-function participation on open hyperbolic lattices. A Gaussian control preserves the full one-site distribution of Comtet disorder at every vertex while removing its off-diagonal covariance. Complete-window participation ratios on $\{7,3\}$ graphs with $N=847,2240,5887$ yield an effective-exponent difference of $-0.028$ at $W/t=0.5$ and $+0.116$ at $W/t=3$; both signs persist upon extending to $N=15435$. Size-resolved squared-kernel radii lie near $2.45\ell$ on the larger graphs of this sequence, below the cutoff-matched continuum value $3.12\ell$. Distance-shell weights resolve the finite discrete measure associated with this shift, while higher participation moments and spacing ratios characterize the accompanying wave-function and spectral structure. A marginal-matched exponential-kernel family gives positive exponent contrasts at all five sampled kernel lengths at $W/t=0.5,3$, separating the Comtet response from a description based on the decay length alone. These results distinguish a concentrated variance budget from the covariance-dependent participation response of finite open hyperbolic systems.

</details>


### [63] [Exact solution of the dynamic pseudogap model: Fock-space formalism for Gaussian fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01574)
*Michael V. Sadovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出动态有限Q赝能隙模型的精确解：将无序平均格林函数映射为Fock空间中稀疏非厄米矩阵的resolvent矩阵元，并给出谱函数、态密度、边带结构及共振劈裂等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前不同时刻赝自旋顶点的不对易性阻碍了精确处理；需要统一处理涨落频率、阻尼和电子能量，以理解动态序参量涨落对赝能隙谱的影响。

Method: 电子在两个嵌套费米面patch间散射于经典高斯序参量场，场具有特征频率omega0和阻尼gamma；无序平均Green函数等于作用在玻色模式Fock空间上的稀疏非厄米矩阵resolvent的单个矩阵元（公度涨落为2个模式，非公度涨落为4个模式），并完整保留赝自旋顶点非对易性。论文推导该表示、对称性及精确极限：静态连分数、费米面处公度闭式解和二阶自能，并进行数值精确实现。

Result: 数值精确得到所有涨落频率、阻尼和电子能量下的谱函数与态密度；谱函数边带梳接近omega0的整数倍；准粒子阻尼由能量依赖的退相干率决定；当准粒子能量与omega0/2共振时，在旋转框架中得到静态涨落能隙，导致准粒子峰发生共振劈裂。

Conclusion: 该精确映射与非厄米矩阵求解为动态有限Q赝能隙模型提供了可控框架，揭示了动态涨落下边带结构、能量依赖退相干以及共振劈裂等关键物理。

Abstract: We present an exact solution of the dynamic finite-$Q$ pseudogap model, in which electrons scatter between two nested Fermi-surface patches off a classical Gaussian order-parameter field with characteristic frequency $\omega_0$ and damping $\gamma$. The disorder-averaged Green's function is shown to be a single matrix element of the resolvent of a sparse non-Hermitian matrix acting on a Fock space of two (commensurate fluctuations) or four (incommensurate fluctuations) bosonic modes with complex energies. The non-commutativity of the pseudospin vertices at different times, which prevented an exact treatment so far, is retained in full. We derive the representation, its symmetries, and the exact limits it contains: the static continued fractions, the closed-form commensurate solution at the Fermi surface, and the second-order self-energy. A numerically exact implementation gives spectral functions and densities of states for all values of the fluctuation frequency, damping and electron energy. We find that the sideband comb of the spectral function stays close to multiples of $\omega_0$ and that quasiparticle damping is governed by an energy-dependent decoherence rate. At resonance of a quasiparticle energy with $\omega_0/2$, we obtain the static fuctuating-gap in a rotating frame, which leads to resonant splitting of the quasiparticle peak.

</details>


### [64] [A Clifford-index approach connecting boundary Dirac fermions to sigma-model topological terms in the periodic table of topological insulators and superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01619)
*Y. X. Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文建立指标理论框架，从八类实Altland–Zirnbauer的最小无质量边界Dirac模型出发，推导出边界sigma模型拓扑项，从而在算符层面连接周期表的两类刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体与超导体的周期表分别由边界Anderson局域化和动量空间K理论给出；二者物理联系需要从底层费米子理论导出边界sigma模型拓扑项。

Method: 构造八类实AZ的最小无质量边界Dirac模型；引入插值两个sigma场纹理的椭圆柱算符，利用分级Clifford对称代数定义商指标，并证明其保护零模指标等于端点纹理的相对同伦类；通过保持核指标类的形变将阻碍局域化为Clifford缺陷，用精确振子核计算固定生成元归一化；借助场无关同余与共同正则化得到实反对称Pfaffian代表，并用Witten谱流论证将其符号输运与柱算符mod-two指标相联系。

Result: 得到一个最小物理模块的非平凡Z2 theta权重；结合复母体反常、物理Clifford多重性与实靶周期，给出单位球面水平的实类Wess–Zumino项。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了边界Dirac理论与周期表对应sigma模型拓扑项之间的算符级联系。

Abstract: The periodic table of topological insulators and superconductors emerges from two distinct approaches: boundary Anderson localization and momentum-space K-theory. Connecting these approaches physically requires deriving the boundary sigma-model topological terms from the underlying fermion theories. We develop an index-theoretic framework for minimal massless boundary Dirac models in the eight real Altland--Zirnbauer classes. Its central ingredients are an elliptic cylinder operator interpolating between two sigma-field textures, a quotient index defined by the cylinder's graded Clifford symmetry algebra, and an index theorem identifying its protected zero-mode index with the relative homotopy class of the endpoint textures. To establish this correspondence, we deform the cylinder texture while preserving the kernel's index class and localize the obstruction to a Clifford defect. An exact oscillator-kernel calculation fixes the generator normalization. A field-independent congruence and a common regularization provide a real antisymmetric Pfaffian representative whose sign transport is related to the cylinder mod-two index by Witten's spectral-flow argument. This yields the nontrivial $\mathbb{Z}_2$ theta weight for one minimal physical module. Combining complex-parent anomalies with physical Clifford multiplicities and real-target periods also gives the real-class Wess--Zumino terms with unit spherical level. The framework thus provides an operator-level connection between boundary Dirac theories and the sigma-model topological terms associated with the periodic table.

</details>


### [65] [Generative diffusion in long-range correlated landscapes: Speciation cascade and the foam structure of the score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02955)
*Jerome Garnier-Brun,Giulio Biroli,Marc M\'ezard,Jean-Philippe Bouchaud*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文通过一个可解析的长程相关高维高斯随机势 Gibbs 测度模型，揭示扩散模型反向过程的有效性源于按 Parisi 纯态树逐级“物种分化”的机制，并将精确 score 描述为具有板状单元和激波边界的生成泡沫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型在图像和视频生成中达到先进水平，但其有效性、尤其是完整反向过程相对于单步或自回归方法的优势，以及该优势适用于何种数据，仍缺乏理论解释。作者旨在理解反向过程在长程相关复杂数据中的作用。

Method: 考虑长程相关高维高斯随机势的 Gibbs 测度这一解析可处理数据模型，聚焦精确 score；结合复制对称破缺与 Parisi 纯态树分析，并构造适用于 O(1) 能量间隙的 Larkin 论证，将 score 刻画为由板状单元和激波状边界组成的泡沫。

Result: 发现原先的 speciation threshold 变为 speciation window：反向轨迹逐步解析完整 RSB 测度的 Parisi 树，从粗分支逐级承诺到单一纯态；这些承诺发生在噪声水平下纯态仅由 O(1) 能量区分、超出标准复制鞍点描述的范围。在方差爆炸过程中，该窗口后样本向数据支撑收缩，并在所选纯态内重建；精确 score 的去噪误差随这些阶段下降，其剖面由 Parisi 函数决定。

Conclusion: 作者提出生成泡沫及其单元边界可能对训练扩散模型在长程相关数据上如何重新分配生成样本质量、从而决定误差分布具有重要作用。

Abstract: While state of the art for image and video generation, the fundamental reasons for the effectiveness of generative diffusion remain unclear. In particular, the observed advantage of following an entire reverse process with respect to single-shot or autoregressive methods, and for what type of data it may hold, is not well understood. In this work, we aim to better understand the role of the reverse process by considering a complex yet analytically tractable data model, the Gibbs measure of a high-dimensional Gaussian random potential with long-range correlations. Focusing on the exact score, we show that the speciation threshold, in which a trajectory commits to one class of the data, becomes a speciation window, in which the reverse process progressively resolves the Parisi tree of pure states of the full replica-symmetry breaking measure, committing to ever finer branches down to a single pure state. Importantly, these commitments take place at noise levels where the noisy sample distinguishes the pure states only through an energy of order one, beyond the reach of standard replica saddle points. Constructing a Larkin argument suited to such $O(1)$ energy gaps, we describe the score as a foam, with slab-like cells separated by shock-like boundaries. In the variance-exploding process we consider, this window is followed by a shrinkage of the samples towards the data support, and finally by reconstruction within the selected pure state. The denoising error of the exact score follows these stages, dropping during the speciation window with a profile set by the Parisi function alone, and then within the selected state. We believe that the generative foam we describe, and in particular the boundaries between cells, could play an important role in determining where the errors of trained diffusion models redistribute the mass of the generated samples when the data are long-range correlated.

</details>

### [66] [Internal friction and frequency-dependent conductivity in the bivariate site energy model for the mixed alkali effect in glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03591)
*Justus Leiber,Sven Lohmann,Quinn Emilia Fischer,Philipp Maass*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文将新建立的二元随机位点能量景观热激活跃迁理论扩展用于解释混合碱玻璃中的交流离子电导和内耗异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合碱效应是理解玻璃中离子输运的基础现象，表现为用一种离子逐步替换另一种离子时多种离子输运性质发生显著变化；作者此前理论已成功描述直流极限下的分电导及其激活能。

Method: 基于二元随机位点能量景观中的热激活跃迁理论，考虑按广义Fermi分布描述的位点占据统计，并依据线性不可逆热力学处理混合动力学。

Result: 该理论能够捕捉交流离子电导中混合碱效应的主要特征，以及内耗的特殊行为；摘要未给出定量拟合或实验数据细节。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架可统一解释混合碱玻璃中直流与交流离子电导及内耗相关异常，表明位点占据统计和混合动力学是描述混合碱效应的关键。

Abstract: The mixed alkali effect is fundamental to understanding ion transport in glasses. It manifests itself in strong changes of various ionic transport properties when one type of ion is successively replaced by another type. Recently, we have developed a new theory of thermally activated hopping in bivariate random site energy landscapes that takes into account site occupation statistics according to generalized Fermi distributions and mixture kinetics in accordance with linear irreversible thermodynamics. The theory was successfully applied to predict the behavior of partial conductivities and their activation energies at low frequencies corresponding to the dc limit. Here we show that the theory is capable to capture the salient features of the mixed alkali effect observed in ac ion conductivities and in the peculiar behavior of the internal friction.

</details>


### [67] [Heterostructure morphology stability maps in multiphase nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00575)
*Elodie Sandraz,Chris Wolverton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于Potts模型和DFT表面/界面能的预测策略，构建稳定性图谱以预测多相纳米颗粒的基态界面构型与形貌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米组合学中多相纳米颗粒 megalibrary 快速发展，亟需可从组成相能量出发预测其内部界面架构的计算方法。

Method: 将各组成相的DFT表面能与界面能作为输入，用q态Potts模型离散表示多相纳米颗粒及环境自旋，以自旋间相互作用表征相间界面能、与环境自旋相互作用表征表面能，并用Monte Carlo搜索最低能量自旋构型，进而构建稳定性图谱。

Result: 建立双相纳米颗粒界面形貌稳定性图谱并分析三种双相形貌关系，用于预测样品双相体系并与实验数据比较；将网格搜索扩展至三相体系，发现11种三相纳米颗粒界面形貌，比先前枚举多9种，并预测样品三相体系及复核实验报道的三相形貌。

Conclusion: 表面能与界面能最小化之间的权衡驱动界面形貌形成；该Potts-Monte Carlo框架可基于DFT能量预测多相纳米颗粒基态界面架构，并扩展至双相和三相形貌稳定性预测。

Abstract: In the rapidly growing field of nanocombinatorics, the advent of megalibraries of multiphase nanoparticles (NPs) has created a need for predictive computational approaches. A notable characteristic of multiphase NPs is the internal interface architecture that arises from the joining of constituent phases at boundaries. We propose a strategy to predict the ground-state interface architecture from the DFT-calculated surface and interfacial energies of all constituent phases as input and use this strategy to create stability maps to predict multiphase NP morphologies. A Potts model, a $q$-state generalization of the Ising model, in which $q-1$ spins represent discretized regions of a constituent phase, provides a flexible, discretized representation of a $(q-1)$-phase nanoparticle with arbitrary interfacial join morphology. Interactions between spins represent interactions between phases, or interfacial energies. To model the surface of a nanoparticle, an additional spin represents the surrounding environment and interaction energies with this spin are surface energies. We apply a Monte Carlo algorithm to search for the lowest-energy spin configuration. The trade-off between surface and interfacial energy minimization drives the formation of an interface morphology. We calculate a biphase nanoparticle interface morphology stability map to study the relation between the three biphase morphologies we found. Next, we use the stability map to predict NP morphologies of sample biphase systems and compare these to experimentally available data. We repeat the grid search approach for triphase nanoparticle interface morphologies and discover 11 triphase morphologies, nine more than previously enumerated. We then predict sample triphase systems and finally revisit experimentally reported triphase morphologies.

</details>


### [68] [Structural and Optical Properties of PVP-Modified ZnS Nanoparticles for Photocatalytic Dye Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01660)
*Vigneshwari S R,Dushyant Singh,Subha Maity,Tharundev V V,Mukesh K. Kumawat,Mukesh Dhanka,Krista R. Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: PVP修饰改变ZnS纳米颗粒的光学性质并显著增强紫外光照下亚甲基蓝的脱色，同时保持主相为立方闪锌矿ZnS；但吸光度下降不能证明完全矿化。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察PVP添加对ZnS纳米颗粒的结构、表面、光学性质及亚甲基蓝脱色性能的影响。

Method: 制备原始与PVP修饰ZnS样品；采用XRD、电子显微术/HRTEM/电子衍射、Tauc法、100–300 K拉曼光谱、可见光致发光及254 nm紫外照射210 min的MB脱色实验，并设无催化剂对照。

Result: XRD确认所有样品为立方闪锌矿ZnS，Scherrer相干域尺寸约3.10–3.31 nm；显微观察显示团聚，HRTEM与电子衍射证实S1和S4存在局部晶区；直接Tauc带隙由S1的3.69 eV降至S4的3.58 eV；升温时主要TO类拉曼带软化，PL变化主要集中在绿-黄重叠区；210 min后S1–S4的664 nm MB谱带损失分别为19.33%、58.18%、70.00%、94.42%，无催化剂对照为6.58%。

Conclusion: PVP修饰在保持ZnS主相的同时调控其光学响应并提高MB脱色效率，但仅凭染料特征带衰减不足以判定完全矿化，需进一步提供矿化证据。

Abstract: Pristine and PVP-modified ZnS nanoparticles were investigated to examine how PVP addition affects structural, surface, optical, and methylene blue (MB) decolorization properties. X-ray diffraction confirmed cubic zinc-blende ZnS in all samples, with apparent Scherrer coherent-domain sizes of 3.10-3.31 nm. Electron microscopy revealed agglomeration, while HRTEM and electron diffraction confirmed locally crystalline regions in S1 and S4. The estimated direct Tauc gap decreased from 3.69 eV for S1 to 3.58 eV for S4. Between 100 and 300 K, the principal TO-like Raman band softened on heating, while visible photoluminescence changed predominantly within the overlapping green-yellow region. After 210 min under 254 nm irradiation, MB-band losses near 664 nm reached 19.33%, 58.18%, 70.00%, and 94.42% for S1-S4, compared with 6.58% for the catalyst-free control. PVP modification therefore alters optical and dye-decolorization responses while preserving the principal ZnS phase; however, dye-band attenuation alone does not establish complete mineralization.

</details>


### [69] [FLAKE: Friction and Lattice Analysis of Kinetics and Energetics of rigid 2D clusters on crystalline substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01805)
*Andrea Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: FLAKE 是一个用于模拟刚性二维团簇在周期或准周期衬底势上滑动静态与 Langevin 动力学的 Python 软件包，可高效研究几何与衬底对称性对能垒和摩擦的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小固体岛在晶态表面上的平滑流动/黏滑、自由转动/角度锁定等行为主要由岛与表面的相对取向、尺寸和晶格对称性决定；这类问题对纳米摩擦、胶体单层实验和结构超滑很重要，但全原子模拟成本过高。

Method: 开发 FLAKE（Friction and Lattice Analysis of Kinetics and Energetics）Python 包，模拟刚性二维团簇在周期或准周期衬底势上的静力学和 Langevin 动力学。

Result: FLAKE 能够快速、系统地探索几何和衬底对称性如何控制能量景观与摩擦；已成为三项已发表的结构超滑和胶体摩擦研究的主要模拟工具。

Conclusion: FLAKE 为研究几何与衬底对称性调控的纳米摩擦和结构超滑提供高效模拟工具，适合替代成本高昂的全原子模拟进行系统参数扫描。

Abstract: When a small solid island slides across a crystalline surface, the outcome (smooth flow or stick-slip, free rotation or angular locking) is controlled almost entirely by the geometric relationship between the island and the surface: their relative orientation, size and lattice symmetry. These phenomena are central to nanoscale friction, to the interpretation of colloidal monolayer experiments and to structural superlubricity. FLAKE (Friction and Lattice Analysis of Kinetics and Energetics) is a Python package for simulating the statics and Langevin dynamics of a rigid 2D cluster sliding over a periodic or quasiperiodic substrate potential. It enables fast, systematic exploration of how geometry and substrate symmetry control the energy landscape and friction, studies that are prohibitively expensive with fully atomistic simulations. FLAKE has been the primary simulation tool for three published studies on structural superlubricity and colloidal friction.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CISS 已在轻元素和室温下被观察到，但弱自旋轨道耦合难以直观解释其大小；耗散与电子关联的作用此前未被统一纳入同一机制。

Result: 该竞争给出被手性声子影响电子所占比例及温度依赖；自旋轨道耦合本身未被重整化，但由于其耦合的轨道矩和自旋矩被库仑关联增强，SOC 表现为有效增大。

Conclusion: 室温轻元素 CISS 可由手性声子辐射、耗散竞争与电子关联共同解释；关键不是增强 SOC 常数，而是关联增强 SOC 所耦合的矩。

Abstract: Chirality-induced spin selectivity is established even in light elements at room temperature; dissipation and electron correlations are implicated, here combined in one mechanism. Injected electron wave packets coherently radiate chiral phonons; electronic absorption competing with phonon decay sets the fraction electrons receive and its temperature dependence. Spin-orbit coupling itself is not renormalized; it becomes effectively large because both the orbital and spin moments it couples are enhanced by Coulomb correlations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明范德华异质结器件工作过程中载流子注入与功能界面的作用，并检验仅用电场效应是否能解释其光电响应。
