Conclusion: PL可作为工作状态下范德华异质结中载流子注入的直接光学探针；空间分辨PL光谱是定位决定器件工作的关键界面的通用工具。

Abstract: We investigate the bias-dependent photoluminescence (PL) response of a WSe$_2$/MoS$_2$ heterostructure diode. Under forward bias, trion-related emission in WSe$_2$ is markedly enhanced, whereas reverse bias produces minimal spectral changes. This asymmetric response cannot be explained by electric-field effects alone and instead reflects carrier injection at the functional interface. These results demonstrate that PL provides a direct optical probe of carrier injection in an operating van der Waals heterostructure. More broadly, this approach establishes spatially resolved PL spectroscopy as a versatile tool for pinpointing the interfaces governing device operation in van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Y2CdX4化合物在光电子和能量转换领域的应用潜力，并理解硫族元素替换对材料性能的调控规律。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论计算结构优化、力学性质、电子能带结构（含自旋轨道耦合）、介电函数与光学响应、热电输运性质，以及电荷密度、电子局域函数和Bader电荷分析。

Result: 晶格参数从S到Te系统增大，体模量由83 GPa降至68 GPa和54 GPa；间接带隙分别为2.42、2.08和1.71 eV，加入自旋轨道耦合后进一步收窄；吸收边约从2.4 eV红移至1.7 eV；Y2CdSe4具有较好的Seebeck系数与功率因子平衡；成键共价性从硫化物到碲化物增强。

Conclusion: Y2CdX4系列化合物具有可调带隙、光学响应和热电性能，在可调光电子器件与能量转换应用方面具有潜力。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive density functional theory (DFT) investigation of the structural, mechanical, electronic, optical, thermoelectric, and bonding properties of Y2CdX4 (X = S, Se, Te) compounds for potential optoelectronic and energy-conversion applications. Structural optimization reveals a systematic expansion of the equilibrium lattice parameters from S to Te. The calculated bulk moduli decrease from 83 GPa for Y2CdS4 to 68 GPa for Y2CdSe4 and 54 GPa for Y2CdTe4, indicating progressive lattice softening. Electronic band-structure calculations predict indirect band gaps of 2.42, 2.08, and 1.71 eV for Y2CdS4, Y2CdSe4, and Y2CdTe4, respectively, with further narrowing upon inclusion of spin-orbit coupling. The calculated dielectric functions, refractive indices, absorption coefficients, reflectivity, optical conductivities, and electron energy-loss spectra reveal systematic changes in optical response across the chalcogen series. The absorption onset shifts toward lower photon energies, from approximately 2.4 eV in Y2CdS4 to 1.7 eV in Y2CdTe4. Thermoelectric calculations indicate promising Seebeck coefficients and power factors, with Y2CdSe4 exhibiting a favorable balance of transport properties. Charge-density distributions, electron localization functions, and Bader charge analyses suggest increasing covalent bonding contributions from sulfide to telluride. These findings highlight the potential of Y2CdX4 compounds for tunable optoelectronic and energy-conversion applications.

</details>


### [73] [On-the-fly $\Delta$-machine learned force fields for warm dense matter using a Yukawa reference model: Application to magnesium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00170)
*Sayan Bhowmik,John E. Pask,Phanish Suryanarayana*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一种用于温稠密物质（WDM）的在线Δ机器学习力场（Δ-MLFF）分子动力学框架：以Yukawa屏蔽库仑参考模型为基础，用机器学习学习其与Kohn-Sham DFT之间的能量、力和应力残差，从而兼顾DFT精度与高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WDM的从头算分子动力学模拟计算成本极高，而标准机器学习力场在同等成本下误差较大；需要一种可在模拟过程中在线修正、精度更高且能大幅加速的MLFF方法。

Method: 采用Yukawa参考模型描述离子间主导的屏蔽库仑相互作用，其参数在线拟合至Kohn-Sham DFT数据；采用基于核方法和贝叶斯线性回归的MLFF学习Yukawa模型与KS-DFT之间的能量、原子力和应力差。先在温稠密碳上与文献KS-DFT结果对比验证，再用于计算镁在5.22–10.44 g/cm^3、600 kK–1.5 MK条件下的性质。

Result: Δ-MLFF在相近成本下对能量、原子力和应力的误差显著低于标准MLFF，并将从头算MD加速最多三个数量级。对镁的计算表明：压力、自扩散系数和剪切黏度随温度与密度呈幂律；动态结构因子出现明确定义的离子声学模，其负色散是强耦合液体的特征；Stokes-Einstein关系在所考虑条件下误差在10%以内。

Conclusion: Δ-MLFF是一种准确且高效的WDM分子动力学方法，可显著加速从头算模拟并可靠预测状态方程、结构和输运性质。

Abstract: We develop a framework for on-the-fly $\Delta$-machine learned force field ($\Delta$-MLFF) molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of warm dense matter (WDM). In particular, we employ a Yukawa reference model that captures the dominant screened-Coulomb interactions between the ions, with its parameters fit on the fly to Kohn--Sham density functional theory (DFT) data, and an MLFF scheme based on the kernel method and Bayesian linear regression that learns the differences in the energies, atomic forces, and stresses between the Yukawa model and Kohn--Sham DFT. We verify the accuracy and performance of the formalism by comparing the predicted properties of warm dense carbon with Kohn--Sham DFT results in the literature. In so doing, we demonstrate that $\Delta$-MLFF has significantly lower errors in the energies, atomic forces, and stresses than the standard MLFF scheme at a comparable cost, while accelerating ab initio MD simulations by up to three orders of magnitude. We apply this framework to calculate the equation of state, structure, and transport properties of magnesium at densities of 5.22 to 10.44 g/cm$^3$ and temperatures of 600 kK to 1.5 MK. We find that the pressure, self-diffusion coefficient, and shear viscosity obey power laws in temperature and density, that the dynamic structure factor exhibits a well-defined ion-acoustic mode whose negative dispersion is characteristic of strongly coupled liquids, and that the Stokes--Einstein relation holds to within 10% over the conditions considered.

</details>


### [74] [In the age of big data and AI: do we still need chemical bonds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00228)
*Mohammed Al-Fahdi,Aakash Naik,Katharina Ueltzen,Luc Walterbos,Tom Demeyere,Philipp Benner,Janine George*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 文章主张人工智能不会使化学键概念过时，而是提供了评估与精炼键合概念的新框架，并提出化学键可视为电子结构的可学习表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习模型主要依赖组成和结构信息即可取得优异预测性能，因此需要回答显式化学键概念是否仍然必要，以及如何用机器学习重新审视键合模型。

Method: 对Allegro和MACE嵌入进行线性探测以检验其是否编码键合描述符；在电子结构特征化的MEGNet（ESF-MEGNet）中引入键合描述符，并与仅使用键长等信息进行比较。

Result: Allegro和MACE嵌入可通过线性探测恢复键合描述符，R^2>0.95；键合描述符提供超出键长的信息，使体积模量和剪切模量等性质的预测误差降低15%。

Conclusion: 化学键可理解为电子结构的可学习表示，不同键合模型是对电子信息的替代性压缩，可通过预测能力、维度和复杂度评估；机器学习将关注点从模型是否具有物理动机转向其是否捕获解释和预测材料行为所需信息；若存在材料基因组，应能通过化学键表示编码，否则需修正对化学键的理解。

Abstract: Chemical bonding is the language through which chemistry rationalizes materials structure, stability, properties, and reactivity. Meanwhile, modern deep-learning models achieve remarkable predictive performance using representations based largely on compositional and structural information, raising the question of whether explicit bonding concepts remain necessary. In this work, we argue that artificial intelligence does not render chemical bonding obsolete but provides a new framework for evaluating and refining bonding concepts. We show that, through linear probing, Allegro and MACE embeddings recover bonding descriptors with (R^2 > 0.95), indicating that bonding emerges naturally from Allegro's and MACE's latent spaces. We also show that bonding descriptors provide information beyond bond distance in our electronic-structure-featurized MEGNet (ESF-MEGNet) model, reducing prediction error for properties including bulk and shear moduli by 15%. We discuss implications for interpretable machine learning, synthesizability prediction, and generative materials design, and suggest joint-embedding approaches for learning more general bonding representations. More broadly, we propose that chemical bonding can be understood as a learnable representation of electronic structure. From this perspective, different bonding models represent alternative compressions of the underlying electronic information and can be evaluated through their predictive power, dimensionality, and complexity. The age of machine learning shifts the focus from whether a bonding model is physically motivated to whether it captures the information required to explain and predict materials behavior. If a materials genome exists, it should be encodable through a representation of chemical bonding. If no bonding representation can capture this information, our understanding of the chemical bond may require revision.

</details>


### [75] [Quantum dynamics of ultrasmall magnetic skyrmions in atomic-defect landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00304)
*Samir Lounis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 超小磁斯格明子在原子缺陷势场中可表现量子力学行为；由于陀螺力主导，其两个坐标成为正则共轭量，系统等效为最低朗道能级问题，并呈现量子化陀螺轨道、量子脱钉和强耦合束缚等新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁斯格明子是未来信息技术的重要候选，但在器件中其命运由遇到的缺陷决定。超薄薄膜中的斯格明子可小至几纳米、仅携带数十个磁矩，而原子缺陷会在原子尺度重塑其能量景观，因此需要研究超小斯格明子是否以及如何表现量子行为。

Method: 作者基于第一性原理参数化的原子自旋模拟，获得PdFe/Ir(111)中Cr、Fe吸附原子及二聚体与斯格明子的相互作用势剖面；在此基础上分析陀螺力主导下的斯格明子量子动力学，将其映射为有效磁场中最低朗道能级的粒子，并考察缺陷束缚、鞍点散射、量子脱钉、嵌入杂质强耦合以及阻尼和温度效应。

Result: 发现超小斯格明子可量子化：有效磁长度约0.6 Å，陀螺轨道量子化能级间距为20--85 μeV；不仅钉扎缺陷可束缚斯格明子，反直觉地排斥型缺陷也可形成束缚。能量景观鞍点充当量子分束器，临界电流以下量子脱钉速率可达1--100 Hz。嵌入杂质会强束缚斯格明子，使其不再表现为刚体粒子，进入强耦合区。典型超薄薄膜Gilbert阻尼下量子能级仍可分辨，热--量子交叉位于40--160 mK。

Conclusion: 该工作预言了磁斯格明子的量子陀螺运动及其缺陷调控机制，指出可通过毫开尔文扫描隧道显微术中的针尖诱导逃逸统计和电子自旋共振STM，在原子级定制缺陷处观测斯格明子的量子化陀螺运动。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are prime candidates for future information technology, and their fate in devices is settled by the defects they encounter. In ultrathin films, skyrmions can be as small as a couple of nanometers, carrying only a few tens of magnetic moments, while atomic defects reshape their energy landscape on the atomic scale. Here, we demonstrate that such ultrasmall skyrmions can behave quantum mechanically and that their quantum dynamics is unusual. Since the gyrotropic force dominates, the two coordinates of a ferromagnetic skyrmion are canonically conjugate and the skyrmion behaves as a particle in the lowest Landau level of an effective magnetic field, with a magnetic length of 0.6~\AA. Utilizing skyrmion-defect interaction profiles of Cr and Fe adatoms and dimers in PdFe/Ir(111), obtained from atomistic spin simulations parametrized from first principles, we unveil quantized gyrotropic orbits with level spacings of 20--85~$\mu$eV, bound not only by pinning defects but also, counter-intuitively, by repulsive ones. Saddle points of the energy landscape act as quantum beam splitters for skyrmions, and quantum depinning reaches rates of 1--100~Hz below the critical current. Embedded impurities, in contrast, bind the skyrmion so strongly that it ceases to behave as a rigid particle, defining a strong-coupling regime. For the Gilbert damping typical of ultrathin films, the quantized levels remain well resolved. The thermal-to-quantum crossover lies between 40 and 160~mK, which is within reach of millikelvin scanning tunneling microscopy, where tip-induced escape statistics and electron spin resonance STM can reveal the quantized gyrotropic motion of skyrmions pinned at defects crafted atom by atom.

</details>

*Ola Kenji Forslund,Andrzej Ptok,Chun Lin,Aksel Kobialka,Wan Yu Chen,Ugne Miniotaite,Martin M{\aa}nsson,Yasmine Sassa,Timur Kim,Cephise Cacho,Jian Rui Soh,Chin Shen Ong,Vladislav Borisov,Olle Eriksson,Johan Chang*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 结果提供Co3Sn2S2中拓扑平带磁性的证据，建立了拓扑、电子关联与磁性相互交织的微观场景。

Abstract: Correlations and topology are central pillars in the study of emergent electronic phases. Topological flat bands near the Fermi level unite these concepts, providing a route towards correlated topological phenomena and unconventional magnetism. Despite theoretical predictions, momentum-resolved observation of a topological flat band at the Fermi level has remained elusive. Using angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy, we observe a flat band at the Fermi level within the topologically non-trivial electronic structure of the magnetic kagome Weyl semimetal Co$_3$Sn$_2$S$_2$. We further uncover a strong temperature dependence in which the flat band shifts across the Fermi level, accompanied by a reconstruction of the Fermi surface. Strikingly, this evolution occurs on the same temperature scale as previously reported anomalies in the magnetic response. Together, these results provide evidence for topological flat band magnetism in Co$_3$Sn$_2$S$_2$, establishing a microscopic setting in which topology, electronic correlations, and magnetism become intertwined.

</details>


### [77] [Machine Learning Aided Computational Discovery of Boride Substrates for Borophene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00533)
*Luqing Wang,Qunfei Zhou,Qiucheng Li,Venkata Surya Chaitanya Kolluru,Hui Li,Albert Tsui,Joshua Paul,Pierre Darancet,Mark C. Hersam,Maria K. Y. Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合DFT与机器学习，在1356种硼化物表面中筛选与硼烯结合适中的候选衬底，并发现AlB2、AgB2等Al/Ag基硼化物最有前景。

Abstract: Borophene, a monolayer of boron, is distinctive among two-dimensional materials owing to its unique properties, including polymorphism and potential applications in quantum technologies and energy conversion and storage. Nevertheless, its applications are hindered by the strong binding of borophene to metal substrates, which impedes the realization of freestanding borophene. Alternative substrates with tunable binding to borophene are therefore highly desirable. Since boride layers arise during borophene synthesis on metals, borides provide potential alternative substrates for borophene synthesis. In this work, we investigate the binding energies and interactions between borophene and 1356 boride surfaces for two borophene phases (v1/6 and v1/9), using density functional theory (DFT) calculations and machine learning (ML). Importantly, incorporating ML-predicted charge transfer as an input feature to a subsequent ML model enables physics-informed predictions of binding energies. Additional DFT calculations on four representative cases were performed to validate the ML predictions. SHAP analysis reveals that the majority of key descriptors are associated with delocalized electrons, highlighting the role of electronic properties in governing binding strength. Combined DFT and ML screening identifies 65 and 73 promising boride surfaces for the v1/6 and v1/9 phases, respectively. Al- and Ag-based borides, including AlB2 and AgB2, emerge as predominant candidates for both phases. The computationally predicted candidate substrates and the deduced design guidelines provide a charge-transfer-informed ML strategy for substrate selection and represent promising steps toward the experimental realization of freestanding borophene.

</details>


### [78] [Elucidating the topological nature of molecular beam epitaxy grown high Curie temperature (Mn1+ySb2-yTe4)x(Sb2-yMnyTe3)1-x using angle resolved photoelectron spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00548)
*Candice R. Forrester,Jisoo Moon,Sina Mohammadi,Asish Kundu,Amy Lopez Navarro,Kaushini Wickramasinghe,Abhay Pasupathy,Anil K. Rajapitamahuni,Maya N. Nair,Elio Vescovo,Lia Krusin-Elbaum,Maria C. Tamargo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用ARPES研究高居里温度磁性量子材料(Mn1+ySb2-yTe4)x(Sb2-yMnyTe3)1-x的能带结构，结果支持其可能为磁性拓扑绝缘体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该MnxSb2-xTe4类材料理论实验分歧大、结构复杂无序，拓扑性质难以确认，且新MBE样品TC高于100 K，需实验厘清其拓扑本质。

Method: 对x=0.65和x=0.92两个样品进行角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）测量，结合光子能量依赖研究，分析体态、表面态及组分对能带结构的影响，并进行变温ARPES测量。

Result: 观察到表面态和体态相关特征，支持非平庸拓扑性质；未发现与Weyl半金属一致的费米弧；不同载流子密度导致费米能级移动，从而观察到表面态Dirac点起始；变温ARPES提示材料磁转变时可能存在能带劈裂。

Conclusion: 结果指向该材料体系可能具有磁性拓扑绝缘体性质。

Abstract: Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) is used to elucidate the band structure of (Mn1+ySb2-yTe4)x(Sb2-yMnyTe3)1-x, a new magnetic quantum material grown using molecular beam epitaxy that has a Curie temperature (TC) above 100K, much higher than those reported for other materials of the MnxSb2-xTe4 class. Conflicting and sparse theory and experiments, as well as the complex disordered structure of the materials make it difficult to establish their topological nature. Here we explore this question by performing ARPES studies for two samples with x=0.65 and x=0.92, respectively. Images of the band structures of these two samples were acquired using photon energy dependent studies. The bulk and surface states of this complex material system, and the effects of composition on the band structure were investigated. Evidence of surface state features as well as bulk related ones were obtained, supporting their non-trivial nature. No signature of Fermi-arcs consistent with Weyl semimetals were seen. A shift in the Fermi level of the two samples due to different carrier densities allowed observation of the onset of a Dirac point in the surface states. Temperature dependent ARPES suggests possible band splitting as the materials undergo magnetic transitions, helping to understand the magnetic interactions. Our results point to their likely magnetic topological insulating nature.

</details>


### [79] [SeriesXRD: automated reflection tracking and phase identification in high-pressure X-ray diffraction series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00611)
*Rush Maples,Zackary Rehfuss,Hongyi Liu,Sheng Ran*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SeriesXRD 是一个开源工作流，将高压同步辐射 XRD 序列作为整体分析，而非逐帧独立处理，用于在强背景和金刚石散射干扰下自动识别、指认并追踪弱衍射峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次高压同步辐射 XRD 实验可产生数百至数千帧，而弱反射的分离、指认和跨帧追踪仍多依赖人工；对金刚石压砧中的微米/纳米样品，压砧、垫片、传压介质和压标散射常主导信号，形成严重瓶颈。

Method: SeriesXRD 对衍射序列进行处理：抑制方位局域的金刚石散射、扣除背景、跨帧拟合与追踪反射、利用压力相关晶格参数匹配已知相、扣除高置信度指认贡献，并将持续存在但未解释的反射分组为候选样品相关或未知相特征。

Result: 在约30 nm厚层状UOTe薄片、3.5–50.7 GPa、1288帧的数据上验证；该数据强背景主导，仅约4%帧可检测到UOTe衍射。SeriesXRD恢复了原人工分析识别的样品相关反射，并将相应相指认与残差分析流程从超过两个月人工时间缩短为隔夜自动运行；全序列拟合17143个峰，14395个通过质量标准，90%被接受峰指认到已知背景或参考相。

Conclusion: SeriesXRD可将大规模、强背景主导的衍射序列转化为可处理的相指认结果和一致的未解释反射集合，并保留每个决策的可追溯记录。

Abstract: High-pressure synchrotron X-ray diffraction increasingly produces hundreds to thousands of detector frames in a single experiment, while separating, assigning, and tracking weak reflections across an entire series often remain manual. This bottleneck is particularly severe for micron- and nanoscale samples in diamond-anvil cells, where scattering from the anvils, gasket, pressure medium, and pressure markers can dominate the sample signal. We present SeriesXRD, an open-source workflow that analyzes diffraction as a series rather than as independent patterns. The program suppresses azimuthally localized diamond scattering, subtracts background, fits and tracks reflections between frames, matches known phases using pressure-dependent lattice parameters, subtracts confidently assigned contributions, and groups persistent unexplained reflections for follow-up as candidate sample-associated or unknown-phase features. We demonstrate the method on 1288 frames collected from an approximately 30-nm-thick layered UOTe flake in a diamond-anvil cell over 3.5-50.7 GPa. The dataset is strongly background dominated, with detectable UOTe diffraction in only approximately 4% of the frames. SeriesXRD recovered the sample-associated reflections identified in the original manual analysis while reducing the corresponding phase-assignment and residual-analysis workflow from more than two months of researcher time to an overnight automated run. Across the full series, 17,143 peaks were fitted, 14,395 passed quality criteria, and 90% of accepted peaks were assigned to known background or reference phases. SeriesXRD therefore converts large, background-dominated diffraction series into a tractable set of phase assignments and coherent unexplained reflections while retaining a traceable record of each decision.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Synthesis Conditions: Linking In-situ Growth Dynamics to Carbon Nanotube Forest Properties through Multimodal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00739)
*Shun Muroga,Takashi Tsuji,Shunsuke Sakurai,Don N Futaba*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究将CVD气流条件与原位时间分辨CNT森林生长曲线结合，提出观察窗口分析，表明仅靠静态合成条件不足以完整描述生长；生长中后期信息可预测产率、最大高度和寿命，为自主合成提供中期决策点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往机器学习研究多将CVD静态合成条件直接映射到最终性质，隐含假设这些条件已完整描述生长过程。作者旨在检验该假设，并确定预测目标性质所需信息在合成过程中何时出现。

Method: 整合气流条件与垂直取向CNT森林的原位生长曲线；采用观察窗口分析，仅向模型提供截至不同观测时刻的生长曲线，评估预测性能随时间的变化；对归一化生长曲线聚类；比较仅气流与加入生长曲线对产率、密度、G/D比、最大高度和寿命的预测；用置换特征重要性识别关键气体变量。

Result: 仅气流条件可预测初始生长速率，但不能预测生长寿命，且无法解释产率变化（R^2<0）。加入生长曲线后产率预测提升至R^2约0.4-0.5，相关信息主要出现在生长中后期而非起始阶段；密度和G/D比无一致改善。10分钟生长的中段可中等准确估计最大高度和寿命。水蒸气和H_2是最具信息量的气体变量，均与催化剂活性相关。

Conclusion: 合成条件未解释的变异并非单纯噪声，而反映演化中的催化剂状态。该工作确立了生长中期决策点，可用于自主合成中提前终止无望批次，并进一步实现批内生长条件调整。

Abstract: Machine learning for chemical vapor deposition (CVD) of carbon nanotubes (CNTs) has largely mapped static synthesis conditions to final properties, implicitly treating these conditions as a complete description of growth. Here, we test this assumption by integrating gas-flow conditions with in-situ time-resolve growth profiles (growth curves) of vertically aligned CNT forests. We introduce an observation-window analysis, in which prediction is evaluated as a function of observation time by providing models with the growth curve only up to progressively later times, to determine not only whether a property can be predicted, but when the necessary information becomes available during synthesis. Clustering of normalized growth curves revealed distinct growth patterns independent of final forest height. The gas conditions alone could predict the initial growth rate but not the growth lifetime and failed to capture the variation in yield (R^2<0). Adding the growth curve improved yield prediction (R^2~0.4-0.5), with the relevant information emerging during the intermediate and later stages of growth rather than at its onset. In contrast, density and G/D ratio, which forest height does not record, showed no consistent improvement. The maximum height and lifetime could be estimated with moderate accuracy midway through the 10-min growth. Permutation feature importance identified water vapor and H_2, both linked to catalyst activity, as the most informative gas variables. These results indicate that variation unexplained by synthesis conditions is not merely noise but reflects an evolving catalyst state, and they establish mid-growth decision points for autonomous synthesis, enabling early termination of unpromising batches and, ultimately, in-batch adjustment of growth conditions.

</details>


### [81] [IRIS: an agentic eye on symmetry-derived paths and energy landscapes of structural phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00761)
*Sukriti Manna,Sraddha Agrawal,Ramamoorthy Ramesh,Panchapakesan Ganesh,Pierre Darancet,Subramanian K. R. S. Sankaranarayanan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: IRIS 是一个代理式工作流，可将自然语言请求和晶体结构自动转化为对称适配的能量景观与拟合的 Landau 能量模型，并用确定性门控保障结构、计算和拟合的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电与多铁功能依赖结构相变，但可解释的能量景观和 Landau 自由能系数构建通常依赖专家经验；从第一性原理获得系数需沿对称导出结构路径采样 Born-Oppenheimer 能面，尤其对耦合多维序参量需要晶体学分析、可靠结构生成和大量计算。

Method: IRIS 结合语言模型解析请求并选择受约束的恢复动作，使用确定性门控决定结构、计算和拟合是否被接受；其流程从第一性原理/DFT 采样对称派生路径，并拟合对称适配的 Landau 能量模型。

Result: 在六种化合物上的应用包括六维 BiFeO3 景观，可分离极性与倾转振幅之间的竞争与方向锁定，并排序均匀开关路径；受控回放检测出全部 53 个注入故障，关闭门控则出现 14 个静默错误；四次前瞻性 BaTiO3 运行仅用 96 次 DFT 计算、总计 34 分钟即生成拟合景观，且无需人工决策。

Conclusion: IRIS 使物理模型构建成为可复用的计算能力，并提供了以显式验证为基础的科学自动化框架。

Abstract: Structural phase transitions underpin ferroelectric and multiferroic functionality, but constructing interpretable energy landscapes remains dependent on specialist expertise. Predicting this behaviour rests on Landau free energies expanded in symmetry-adapted order parameters: their coefficients classify a transition, expose the couplings between distortions, set switching paths and barriers, and parametrize phase-field and effective-Hamiltonian models at larger scales. Obtaining these coefficients from first principles means sampling the Born--Oppenheimer energy surface along symmetry-derived structural paths, which requires crystallographic analysis, reliable structure generation and extensive computation, especially for coupled multidimensional order parameters. Here we introduce IRIS, an agentic workflow that converts plain-language requests and crystal structures into symmetry-adapted energy landscapes and fitted Landau energy models. Deterministic gates govern acceptance of structures, calculations and fits, while language models interpret requests and select constrained recovery actions. Applications across six compounds include a six-dimensional BiFeO$_3$ landscape that separates competition between polar and tilt amplitudes from directional locking and ranks homogeneous switching pathways. Controlled replays detected all 53 injected faults; disabling gates allowed 14 silent errors. Four prospective BaTiO$_3$ runs produced fitted landscapes from 96 density-functional calculations in 34 minutes total without intervening human decisions. IRIS makes physical model construction a reusable computational capability and provides a framework for scientific automation grounded in explicit verification.

</details>


### [82] [Diffusion-Based Generative Screening of ABX$_3$ Perovskite Compositions for Bandgap-Targeted Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00766)
*Sunraj Patel,Krishanu Biswas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用预训练扩散模型生成ABX3钙钛矿候选，并通过连通性筛选、性质预测和第一性原理验证评估其结构拓扑、化学空间覆盖与预测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体生成模型可探索超越固定原型的成分与原子排布，但需确认生成物在特定材料族中保持定义性拓扑且预测性质可靠。

Method: 用预训练扩散模型生成ABX3候选；以连通性过滤器区分三维角共享钙钛矿与同名义化学计量结构；统计B位多重性、有序双钙钛矿及卤化物富集；用MP训练的ALIGNN预测带隙并在随机/严重B位不相交划分测试；对6个结构做第一性原理计算。

Result: 筛选集含显著B位多重性及有序双钙钛矿；相对Materials Project富集Cl/Br/I，但B位元素均已在MP中。ALIGNN带隙分布向1.4 eV偏移，目标窗口比例8.2%接近MP参考7.3%，但因MatterGen与ALIGNN标签来源不同不能证明目标条件富集。ALIGNN在按化学式分组随机测试表现好，但在严重B位不相交测试不优于常数基线。6个结构的第一性原理显示弛豫可改变结构分类，预测与计算带隙可显著不同。

Conclusion: 结果既显示晶体扩散模型的化学空间覆盖能力，也表明其输出作为材料候选前需进行结构拓扑与预测可靠性检查，尤其需独立标签、严格泛化测试及第一性原理验证。

Abstract: Crystal-generation models can explore compositions and atomic arrangements beyond a fixed set of crystal prototypes, but their usefulness for a specific materials family depends on whether the generated structures retain the defining topology and whether their predicted properties remain reliable across the sampled chemistry. Here, a pretrained diffusion model is used to generate candidate ABX$_3$ perovskites and to examine their structural, chemical and electronic-property distributions. A connectivity-based filter distinguishes three-dimensional corner-sharing perovskites from structures that share the same nominal ABX$_3$ stoichiometry. The accepted set contains substantial B-site multiplicity, including ordered double-perovskite configurations that are inaccessible to the two-prototype substitution reference used here. The generated set is enriched in chloride-, bromide- and iodide-containing compositions relative to the Materials Project, while all generated B-site elements are already represented in that database. On the downstream MP-trained ALIGNN scale, the predicted bandgap distribution is shifted towards 1.4 eV, but the target-window fraction (8.2\%) is close to the MP reference fraction (7.3\%); the separate MatterGen and ALIGNN label sources prevent this comparison from establishing target-condition enrichment. An ALIGNN model performs well on a formula-grouped random test split but does not outperform a constant baseline on a deliberately severe B-site-disjoint test. First-principles calculations on six generated structures show that relaxation can alter structural classification and that predicted and calculated gaps can differ appreciably. These results identify both the chemical-space coverage offered by crystal diffusion and the structural and predictive checks needed before its outputs can be treated as materials candidates.

</details>


### [83] [Radiofrequency plasma spheroidization of lightweight complex concentrated superalloy Al10Nb20Ta15Ti30V5Zr20](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00895)
*Franti\v{s}ek Luk\'a\v{c},Jakub Kle\v{c}ka,Ji\v{r}\'i Kozl\'ik,Krist\'ina Bartha,Jozef Vesel\'y,Kate\v{r}ina Fickov\'a,Jakub Mina\v{r}\'ik,Jan \v{C}\'i\v{z}ek,Josef Str\'ask\'y*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出机械合金化结合射频感应耦合等离子体球化制备难熔复杂浓缩合金（RCCA）球形粉末的方法，并以Al10Nb20Ta15Ti30V5Zr20为例验证其适用于增材制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造对成分可定制的高质量金属粉末需求增加，但RCCA因高熔点和化学活性高，采用传统冶金方法制备粉末困难。

Method: 先通过机械合金化制备RCCA粉末，再采用射频感应耦合等离子体球化处理；利用三维原子探针断层扫描研究复杂碳化物形成。

Result: 获得的Al10Nb20Ta15Ti30V5Zr20粉末呈球形，显微组织以BCC相为主并含局部B2相，适合增材制造工艺；并表征了复杂碳化物。

Conclusion: 机械合金化+射频感应耦合等离子体球化可制备适用于增材制造的球形RCCA粉末，为高熔点、高活性复杂浓缩合金粉末制备提供可行路线。

Abstract: Recent advances in additive manufacturing (AM) have significantly increased the demand for high-quality metal powders with tailored composition. For refractory complex concentrated alloys (RCCA) preparing such powders by conventional metallurgical method remains challenging due to their high melting point and chemical reactivity. In this paper, we present a method to produce spherical RCCA powders (here Al10Nb20Ta15Ti30V5Zr20) via mechanical alloying followed by radio-frequency inductively coupled plasma spheroidization. The resulting powder is suitable for AM processes because of its spherical morphology and a microstructure consisting of major BCC phases with local B2 phase. Complex carbide formation was studied by 3D atom probe tomography.

</details>


### [84] [Efficient Interpolation of the Bethe-Salpeter Hamiltonian at Arbitrary Momenta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00928)
*Stefano Mocatti,Xiangzhou Zhu,Giulio Volpato,Pierluigi Cudazzo,Matteo Calandra,Giovanni Marini*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种倒空间方法，可从粗第一性原理网格重构任意晶体动量下的静态Bethe-Salpeter方程哈密顿量，并以Wannier插值Bloch态、介电响应重构屏蔽库仑相互作用，实现高效且高精度的激子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BSE计算依赖密集k/q网格，成本高，难以获得任意动量下的激子色散与振幅；需要将电子-空穴跃迁动量与转移动量从底层ab initio采样中解耦。

Method: 在最优平滑Wannier子空间内插值Bloch态，并由介电响应重构屏蔽库仑相互作用，从而在任意晶体动量下重建静态BSE哈密顿量；在单层hBN、单层MoS2和体相GaAs上与显式ab initio计算基准对比。

Result: 对密集网格Bloch态、介电屏蔽和BSE光学谱均与显式计算高度一致；在所考虑网格上估计最高22倍加速。任意转移动量采样可直接获得长波长激子色散：在hBN和MoS2中解析出长程电子-空穴交换导致的非解析行为，动量远低于实用均匀网格间距；在GaAs中局部细化跃迁动量采样，以远少于常规均匀网格的BSE点获得三重简并最低激子的收敛结合能，并与实验和参考计算吻合。

Conclusion: 该框架可在任意晶体动量给出激子能量和振幅，为将ab initio激子效应纳入非局域电子-声子顶点、扩展至激子-声子相互作用及非平衡激子动力学奠定基础。

Abstract: We develop an efficient reciprocal-space method to reconstruct the static Bethe-Salpeter (BSE) Hamiltonian at arbitrary crystal momenta from coarse first-principles grids. Bloch states are interpolated within an optimally smooth Wannier subspace, while the screened Coulomb interaction is reconstructed from the dielectric response. This decouples the electron-hole transition momenta and transferred momentum from the underlying ab initio sampling. We benchmark the method on monolayer hBN, monolayer MoS$_2$, and bulk GaAs against explicit ab initio calculations, finding excellent agreement for dense-grid Bloch states, dielectric screening, and BSE optical spectra. Furthermore, our cost analysis estimates speedups of up to $22\times$ on the grids considered here. Arbitrary transferred-momentum sampling gives direct access to the long-wavelength exciton dispersion: in hBN and MoS$_2$, we resolve the nonanalytic behaviour induced by long-range electron-hole exchange at momenta well below the spacing of practical uniform grids. For bulk GaAs, locally refined transition-momentum sampling yields a converged binding energy for the threefold-degenerate lowest exciton, in close agreement with experiment and reference calculations, using far fewer BSE points than conventional uniform meshes. As the present framework provides exciton energies and amplitudes at arbitrary crystal momenta, it opens the way to incorporating ab initio excitonic effects into nonlocal electron-phonon vertices and to extensions towards exciton-phonon interactions and nonequilibrium exciton dynamics.

</details>


### [85] [Ab Initio strain-dependent vibrational analysis of different silicene structures for 2D devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01166)
*Archishman Gupta,Ankit Arora*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过DFT/DFPT和BTE研究各向同性拉伸/压缩应变下不同拓扑单层硅烯的声子动力学、热输运与电子性质，指出hhk-硅烯是电热器件与应变电子学的潜力材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估应变对不同拓扑单层硅烯热输运和电子性质的影响，寻找兼具稳定性、合适带隙和可调热性能的候选材料，用于下一代电热器件及应变电子学应用。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）和密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）进行声子动力学研究；对单层硅烯施加各向同性拉伸和压缩应变；结合玻尔兹曼输运方程（BTE）分析热输运性质，并对各拓扑进行电子结构和振动性质分析。

Result: hhk-硅烯具有分子稳定性、类体硅带隙和可接受的、可由应变调控的热性能；其带隙随应变呈近线性单调递减趋势，且热学和电子性能对应变敏感。

Conclusion: hhk-硅烯因稳定、带隙合适且热/电子性能可应变调控，是有前景的下一代电热器件材料，并适合应变电子学应用。

Abstract: We report first-principles phonon dynamics studies in isotropically strained mono-layer silicene films of various topologies by density functional theory and density functional perturbation theory methods. The strain introduced in the sheets are of both tensile and compressive nature and its effect on the thermal transport properties are analysed using the Boltzmann Transport Equation (BTE) reported. We performed electronic and vibrational analysis of each silicene topology and concluded that hhk-silicene is a potential candidate for next generation electro-thermal devices due its molecular stability, bulk-Si like bandgap and tolerable thermal performance that can be engineered by strain. The novel hhk-silicene topology offers excellent thermal and electronic capabilities that are sensitive to strain along with the band gap following a near-linear monotonically decreasing trend making it suitable for strain-tronics applications.

</details>


### [86] [Learning materials spectra across modalities and scales with structure-conditioned neural fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01417)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱可观测量蕴含材料在能量、动量等多维实验坐标中的丰富信息，但直接从原子结构学习光谱响应面临结构构型复杂和光谱观测空间高维复杂的双重挑战。

Method: 构建结构条件神经场框架：用等变结构编码器表示原子结构，用坐标条件网络建模连续光谱响应函数，从而在多种观测坐标上预测光谱可观测量；并采用有限光谱监督微调以捕捉应变和组分相关光谱变化。

Result: 框架可准确预测从一维态密度到多维散射函数等不同观测域的光谱可观测量；有限光谱监督微调可捕捉应变和组分依赖的光谱变化，并成功迁移到包含数百个原子的合金构型，计算可扩展性在超过10^4个原子的构型上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了通用且计算可扩展的结构-光谱关系学习方法，为光谱分析及未来材料表征、逆问题和实验设计提供了基础。

Abstract: Spectroscopic observables encode rich information about material behavior across diverse, often high-dimensional domains of energy, momentum, and other experimental coordinates, providing critical insights into the physical properties underlying material functionalities and applications. However, learning these responses directly from atomic structures remains challenging due to the complexity of both structural configurations and spectroscopic observation spaces. Here, we develop a structure-conditioned neural-field framework that learns continuous spectroscopic response functions by coupling an equivariant structural encoder with a coordinate-conditioned network. The framework accurately predicts spectroscopic observables across diverse observation domains, ranging from one-dimensional densities of states to multidimensional scattering functions. We show that fine-tuning with limited spectral supervision captures strain- and composition-dependent spectral changes, with validated transfer to alloy configurations containing hundreds of atoms and computational scalability demonstrated for configurations exceeding $10^4$ atoms. Our work establishes a versatile and computationally scalable approach to learning structure-spectroscopy relationships, providing a foundation for spectroscopic analysis and future materials characterization, inverse problems, and experimental design.

</details>


### [87] [Fredholm approximations to the Bethe-Salpeter equation with spherical-Bessel kernels for Wannier-Mott excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01570)
*Christian Tantardini,Benedikt Maurer,Claudia Draxl*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种确定性径向近似方法，用于快速初筛直接带隙体材料中的 Wannier-Mott 激子结合能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态 Bethe-Salpeter 方程计算激子结合能成本较高，尤其受布里渊区采样和随机采样限制，需要一种无需随机采样、可用于快速初步筛选的紧凑模型。

Method: 从四点 BSE 及其 Bloch 电子-空穴基矩阵表示出发，在有效质量极限和 Tamm-Dancoff 近似下保留一个价带和一个导带；对屏蔽直接相互作用作中心投影并忽略裸电子-空穴交换。通过分波分解分离角动量通道，将相互作用核表示为实空间屏蔽库仑相互作用球贝塞尔变换，并用 Nyström 离散化得到实对称径向矩阵，用标准本征求解器求解；径向矩阵维度由径向网格决定，与计算屏蔽所用的 BZ 采样无关。

Result: 在 all-electron full-potential 代码 exciting 中实现。对体 GaAs，在匹配采样下紧凑径向问题将常规矩阵 BSE 的结合能复现到 1 meV 以内；进一步细化可在更密屏蔽网格上达到相同容差的有限网格稳定性，且不增加径向矩阵尺寸。

Conclusion: 该确定性径向近似适用于包络在晶胞尺度缓变的扩展激子，在有效质量和中心屏蔽相互作用描述足够准确时，可作为一种快速、稳定的体材料激子结合能初筛工具。

Abstract: We develop a deterministic radial approximation to the static Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE), designed for rapid preliminary screening of Wannier-Mott exciton binding across direct-gap bulk materials. The approximation replaces the electron-hole transition problem by small radial eigenvalue problems that require no stochastic sampling. Starting from the four-point BSE and its matrix representation in a Bloch electron-hole basis, we retain one valence and one conduction band in the effective-mass limit within the Tamm-Dancoff approximation. We introduce a central projection of the screened direct interaction and omit bare electron-hole exchange. The resulting model applies to spatially extended excitons whose envelopes vary slowly on the scale of the unit cell, provided that the effective-mass description and central screened interaction adequately represent the exciton of interest. A partial-wave decomposition separates the angular-momentum channels and expresses their interaction kernels as spherical-Bessel transforms of the real-space screened Coulomb interaction. Nystr\"{o}m discretization produces real symmetric radial matrices suitable for standard eigensolvers. Their dimension is set by the radial grid independently of the Brillouin-zone sampling used to calculate screening. We implement the formulation in the all-electron, full-potential code $\texttt{exciting}$. For bulk GaAs, the compact radial problem reproduces the corresponding conventional matrix-BSE binding energy within $(1~\mathrm{meV})$ at matched sampling. Further refinement achieves finite-mesh stability within the same tolerance on denser screening meshes without increasing the radial matrix size.

</details>


### [88] [Atomistic Insights into MOCVD Growth of GaAs using a Closed Loop Autonomous System Combining Directed Reinforcement Learning and Kinetic Monte Carlo Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01646)
*Hrishi V. Harsora,Ashutosh Krishna Amaram,Raghavan Ranganathan,Tarun K. Agarwal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种将前向动力学蒙特卡洛（KMC）与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）闭环耦合的框架，直接利用实验生长数据校准MOCVD反应速率参数，并在GaAs体系中验证其可解释性与预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MOCVD工艺优化依赖昂贵、迭代的实验试错；CFD和MD难以兼顾实验尺度与原子精度；KMC可桥接尺度，但其动力学速率参数通常依赖昂贵的DFT计算。

Method: 构建前向KMC生长模型，并将其与MCTS优化器闭环耦合，在高维参数空间中搜索；利用各反应速率参数的速率控制度引导搜索，且不引入材料特异性假设，适用于可简化为速率控制事件的前向动力学沉积过程。

Result: 在GaAs的MOCVD中，校准后的模型复现了实测生长速率的特征温度依赖性，并恢复了由前驱体化学吸附向脱附转变所定义的三个已知动力学区间。

Conclusion: 该框架可将常规测量的生长数据转化为具有机理可解释性的沉积过程模型，为MOCVD工艺优化提供数据驱动且可解释的建模途径。

Abstract: Conventional process optimization of the Metal-Organic Chemical Vapor Deposition (MOCVD) growth parameters to achieve high-quality thin films relies on iterative, resource-intensive experimental runs. While predictive modeling can circumvent this cost and effort, simulating thin-film growth is constrained by the vastly different length and time scales of the deposition process. While continuum (e.g., Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD)) and first-principles methods (e.g., Molecular Dynamics (MD)) struggle to balance experimental length/time scales and atomistic accuracy, Kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) bridges this divide by resolving atomistic events whilst remaining tractable at experimental length and time scales. However, the accuracy of KMC relies on kinetic rate parameters that require costly density functional theory (DFT) calculations. In order to circumvent this, we present a framework that calibrates the rate parameters directly against experimental growth data. A forward KMC growth model is coupled in a closed loop with a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) optimizer that navigates the high-dimensional parameter space, with the search steered by the degree of rate control of each reaction rate parameter. The approach invokes no material-specific assumptions and hence, any deposition process whose forward kinetics can be reduced to a set of rate-controlled events is amenable to the same treatment. We demonstrate it for the MOCVD of GaAs, where the calibrated model captures the characteristic temperature dependence of the measured growth rate. The framework is also able to recover the three well known kinetic regimes characterized by change in the rate determining step from precursor chemisorption to desorption. As a result, the framework converts routinely measured growth data into a mechanistically interpretable model of the deposition process.

</details>


### [89] [Origin of bias-voltage-dependent transient negative capacitance in PZT ferroelectric capacitor with labyrinthine-like domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01697)
*Ganga S. Kumar,Kusumpal Yadav,Miral Verma,Parushottam Majhi,Pragati Sharma,Kaustuv Chatterjee,Sandip Bysakh,Sudipta Goswami,Prabir Pal,Chandan K. Ghosh,S. B. Roy,Vasant Sathe,Devajyoti Mukherjee,Dipten Bhattacharya*

TL;DR: 该文揭示偏压通过改变PZT多畴铁电电容器的畴切换路径及中间态畴壁面积，调控瞬态负电容大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬态负电容在铁电电容器中具有可调性，但其微观起源，尤其是偏压依赖的畴切换路径如何影响该效应，尚不清楚。

Result: 应变与局域成分变化导致迷宫畴形成；拉曼模分裂和极化松弛模式显示存在偏压依赖的中间态切换路径；相场模拟证实中间态畴壁面积随偏压和阻尼参数变化。

Conclusion: 偏压变化改变畴切换路径，每条路径对应特定的中间态畴壁面积；该净畴壁面积决定瞬态负电容幅值，从而解释PZT铁电电容器中瞬态负电容的可调性。

Abstract: We investigated how the bias-voltage-dependent variation in the ferroelectric domain switching pathways affects the transient negative capacitance in multidomain PZT ferroelectric capacitor with labyrinthine-like domains having, primarily, 90$^\circ$ walls. Using the vector piezoresponse force microscopy, x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, and energy dispersive x-ray spectroscopy, we show that, together with strain, local variation in composition results in the formation of labyrinthine domains in epitaxial films of thickness $\sim$1 $\mu$m. Clear signature of emergence of bias-voltage-dependent pathways could be observed in the Raman modes exhibiting splitting due to an intermediate state - different from the initial and final states - at the ``intermediate state of switching". The polarization relaxation patterns also show different states emerging depending on the bias voltage characteristics. The phase-field simulation of relaxation of ``intermediate state of switching" corroborates the experimental results and also exhibits dependence of the domain-wall area on the bias voltage and damping parameter. Combining the results of experiments and theoretical calculation, we show, therefore, that the variation of bias voltage leads to a change in the domain switching pathways and each pathway is associated with specific domain-wall area at the ``intermediate state of switching". Since, the net domain-wall area at the ``intermediate state of switching" governs the magnitude of transient negative capacitance, this work thus traces the origin of tunability of transient negative capacitance in PZT ferroelectric capacitor.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过光学建模与材料工艺优化，评估并实现了AgBiS2纳米晶作为近红外（NIR）子电池在叠层光伏中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AgBiS2在单结光伏中效率提升迅速，但其作为叠层电池NIR吸收层的潜力尚未被探索；同时其纳米晶薄膜输运受限，厚度仅30–50 nm，严重限制NIR响应。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了AgBiS2作为稳定、可扩展NIR吸收层用于叠层光伏的重要潜力，并证明配体交换工艺可突破其薄膜厚度与输运限制。

Abstract: AgBiS2 has rapidly risen in efficiency as single-junction photovoltaics (PVs), but its potential for tandem PVs is unexplored. Herein, we perform optical modeling based on the experimental absorption spectrum of AgBiS2, revealing that, as the NIR sub-cell, efficiencies exceeding 47.9% are achievable in triple-junction tandems, superseding tandems using Pb/Sn perovskite NIR cells (35.9%). However, fulfilling this potential requires overcoming the limited transport properties of AgBiS2 nanocrystal films, which constrains film thicknesses to only 30-50 nm, severely restricting NIR response. A solution-phase ligand exchange process is herein developed, allowing one-step deposition of passivated AgBiS2 nanocrystals that maintain efficient charge-carrier extraction despite an order of magnitude increase in film thickness. These films enable 24.0%-efficient four-terminal tandems with visible-light-absorbing lead-halide perovskite sub-cells, and monolithic tandems successfully demonstrating voltage addition. AgBiS2 PVs have high stability, retaining >90% of the original efficiency after 3 months of storage in dry air without encapsulation. This work establishes the significant potential of AgBiS2 as a stable and scalable NIR absorber for tandem PVs.

### [91] [Temperature-Dependent Raman Signatures of Local Symmetry Breaking in Vacancy Double Perovskite Cs$_2$SnI$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01830)
*Tomke E. Glier,Nicholas J. Weadock,Julian A. Vigil,Cole Shank,Xixi Qin,Gabriel J. Graf,Hemamala I. Karunadasa,Volker Blum,Michael F. Toney,Dmitry Reznik*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用温度依赖近共振拉曼散射，并结合基于密度泛函理论的声子计算；通过分析声子强度、能量和谱线线型来表征结构响应随温度的演化。

Result: 低温拉曼响应中出现禁戒声子模，证明存在局域对称破缺但无全局结构相变；200 K以下多声子散射显著增强且对称禁戒拉曼模出现；声子强度的温度依赖符合唯象Arrhenius行为，活化能为15–20 meV。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling the optical, thermal and mechanical properties of K$_2$PtCl$_6$-type double perovskites is essential for the design of lead-free, high-performance thermoelectric and optoelectronic materials. In this study, we investigate Cs$_2$SnI$_6$ using temperature-dependent near-resonance Raman scattering, complemented by density functional theory-based phonon calculations. The appearance of Raman-forbidden phonon modes in the Raman response at cryogenic temperatures provides strong evidence for local symmetry breaking that does not develop into a global structural phase transition. Through detailed analysis of phonon intensities, energies, and spectral lineshapes, we characterize the temperature evolution of the symmetry-broken structural response of Cs$_2$SnI$_6$. Below 200 K, multi-phonon scattering is strongly enhanced, and symmetry-forbidden Raman modes emerge. The temperature dependence of phonon intensities follows a phenomenological Arrhenius behavior, revealing an activation energy of 15-20 meV. These results establish temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy as a sensitive probe of local structural distortions and anharmonic structural dynamics in Cs$_2$SnI$_6$, providing a robust experimental basis for discussing the microscopic origins of symmetry breaking and electron-phonon coupling in halide double perovskites.

</details>


### [92] [Symmetry-Based Transport Criterion and Robust Design for RuO$_2$-type Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01944)
*Zhaoqing Li,Yi Liu,Yizheng Wu,Zhe Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出结合对称性输运判据与RuO2/MnO2超晶格平台的策略，为d波交错磁性的实验验证提供零背景、可操作的路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO2是否具有d波交错磁序仍存争议，且现有输运证据受自旋劈裂效应与时间反演偶自旋霍尔背景纠缠而难以判别。

Method: 一方面识别自旋霍尔背景被严格禁止的晶格几何，建立零背景输运判据；另一方面用RuO2/MnO2超晶格通过界面磁邻近稳健诱导d波交错磁序，并进行对称性分辨的自旋输运分析。

Result: 在该平台中预测到非常规响应，包括可实现无场磁化翻转的共线自旋流以及可观的自旋极化电荷流。

Conclusion: 该工作超越对体材料基态的争论，给出可用于明确验证d波交错磁性的实验路线图。

Abstract: Altermagnetism combines vanishing net magnetization with momentum-dependent spin splitting, enabling spin-transport functionalities without ferromagnetism. Although rutile RuO$_2$ has been proposed as a d-wave altermagnet, its magnetic ground state remains highly controversial. Moreover, existing transport verifications suffer from severe ambiguity due to the entanglement of spin-splitting effects and time-reversal-even spin-Hall contributions. Here, we introduce a two-pronged strategy to break the current verification impasse. First, we establish a definitive, symmetry-based transport criterion: by identifying specific crystallographic geometries where the spin-Hall background is strictly forbidden, we provide a zero-background window for unambiguous altermagnetic detection. Concurrently, to bypass the uncertain bulk energetics, we propose the RuO$_2$/MnO$_2$ superlattice as a prototypical platform to robustly enforce the d-wave altermagnetic order via interfacial magnetic proximity. Within this platform, symmetry-resolved spin-transport analysis reveals unconventional responses, including collinear spin currents enabling field-free magnetization switching and sizable spin-polarized charge currents. By combining a universal transport criterion with a robustly engineered material platform, our work transcends the debates over bulk properties, providing an actionable, definitive experimental roadmap for verifying d-wave altermagnetism.

</details>


### [93] [Unoccupied spin texture of ordered Bi films on vicinal Ge substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02085)

Result: Ge(223)上有序Bi(9911)邻晶薄膜的未占据态自旋符号序列与计算的Bi(111)台阶初始态谱函数一致；Ge(443)和Ge(331)上保留的赝立方Bi畴则表现出相对于宏观邻晶面参考的自旋极化表面态响应。

Abstract: Vicinal surfaces offer a compelling testing ground in surface science: breaking the high symmetry of a singular surface introduces periodic step arrays that can modify surface states on the terraces and influence epitaxial ordering; the interplay between these effects governs morphology and electronic structure, especially in heavy, spin--orbit-coupled materials. We combine low-energy electron diffraction (LEED) and spin-polarized inverse photoemission (SP-IPES) with ab-initio calculations for the Bi$(1\,1\,1)$ reference, for the first time on a true high-index vicinal bismuth surface. On the ordered Bi$(9\,9\,11)$ vicinal film selected by Ge$(2\,2\,3)$, the measured unoccupied-state spin-sign sequence matches the calculated initial-state spectral function of Bi$(1\,1\,1)$ terraces. Conversely, the pseudo-cubic Bi domains retained on Ge$(4\,4\,3)$ and Ge$(3\,3\,1)$ exhibit a characteristic spin-polarized surface-state response referenced to the macroscopic vicinal surface. These results show that the substrate cut selects not only the morphology of the Bi film, but also the crystallographic reference frame of its unoccupied spin texture.

### [94] [Accelerating phase-field simulations of grain growth via tensor decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02118)
*Chaoqian Yuan,Dingxiang Zhu,Ye Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于张量分解的模型降阶框架，用于加速多晶材料晶粒生长的相场模拟，在保持晶粒形貌与生长动力学精度的同时显著降低计算与存储成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场模型能有效模拟多晶材料晶粒生长，但解析多个相变量并在细空间/时间尺度上求解会带来高昂计算与内存开销，限制其大规模应用。

Method: 利用演化相场变量的低秩结构，将高维解表示为空间分解的张量格式，并在降阶表示中直接进行数值计算；在线计算时通过子空间模态更新算法控制分解模态数，以平衡效率与近似精度。

Result: 在典型晶粒生长问题上与常规全阶有限元模拟对比，所提方法能准确再现晶粒形貌演化和晶粒生长动力学的主要特征，同时大幅提高计算效率。

Conclusion: 该框架有望将相场模拟扩展到更大的空间区域、更细的离散化以及包含大量相变量的系统。

Abstract: Phase-field models provide a powerful framework for simulating grain growth phenomena in polycrystalline materials, but their application to large-scale systems is limited by the high computational and memory costs associated with resolving multiple phase variables over fine spatial and temporal scales. This paper presents a tensor decomposition based model reduction framework for accelerating phase-field simulations of grain growth. The proposed method exploits the underlying low-rank structure of the evolving phase-field variables and represents the high-dimensional solution in a spatially decomposed tensor format. By performing the numerical computations directly within this reduced representation, the framework decreases storage requirements and reduces the cost of repeatedly solving the phase-field equations. The number of modes for the decomposed approximation is controlled during the online computation with a subspace mode-updating algorithm, to balance computational efficiency and approximation accuracy. The method is assessed using representative grain growth problems, and its predictions are compared with those obtained from conventional full-order finite element simulations. The results demonstrate that the formulation can accurately reproduce the evolution of grain morphology and the principal characteristics of grain growth kinetics while substantially improving computational efficiency. The proposed framework therefore provides a promising approach for extending phase-field simulations to larger spatial domains, finer discretizations, and systems containing a large number of phase variables.

</details>


### [95] [Theory of the charge response of the boron-vacancy spin in monolayer hexagonal boron nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02137)
*Dongyeon (Tyler),Kim,Theodore Scharlotta,Daniel Hashemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在六方氮化硼的硼空位 V_B^- 中，近邻电荷的方位而非仅距离会显著改变自旋哈密顿量的零场分裂参数 E；碳/氧施主计算与三站点模型揭示了由两个 e' 空穴自旋不平衡驱动的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V_B^- 是重要的二维自旋传感器，但近邻电荷位置与其自旋哈密顿量之间的微观联系仍不清楚，尤其距离之外的方向效应缺乏定量理解。

Method: 对距离 3.85–8.08 Å 的碳和氧施主进行 PBE/HSE 第一性原理计算，比较冻结晶格下 C1 与反转方位角的 C1m；拟合场加梯度模型；用三站点模型描述电荷势对三个空位氮上 e' 空穴自旋分布的影响，并提取 E 与自旋不平衡的比例常数。

Result: 在 4.35 Å 处将碳施主方位角反转（C1→C1m）使 |E| 比值降至 0.467（PBE），而仅考虑距离时为 1，且 E 轴跨过镜像线；预先拟合的场加梯度模型预测比值为 0.443，HSE 静态计算为 0.432，PBE 弛豫为 0.600。E 通过约 8 GHz/μB 的常数随自旋不平衡变化，PBE 下 D 几乎不敏感；三站点模型以一个拟合屏蔽常数将冻结晶格 E 重现至 83 MHz RMS。

Conclusion: 方向效应源于电荷在三个空位氮处势能不同，从而重新分配两个 e' 空穴的自旋，E 跟踪该自旋不平衡。结果为 V_B^- 的电荷-自旋耦合提供微观图像，但仅针对自由单层中的单一电荷态，绝对 |E| 在不同近似间可有数十百分比变化。

Abstract: The boron vacancy $V_{\mathrm{B}}^{-}$ in hexagonal boron nitride is a leading two-dimensional spin sensor, but the microscopic connection between nearby charge positions and its spin Hamiltonian remains poorly understood. Calculations for carbon and oxygen donors 3.85--8.08 $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}$ away show that direction matters, not only distance. In the frozen lattice, reversing a carbon donor's azimuth at 4.35 $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}$ (C1 to C1m) more than halves $|E|$ and turns its axis across the mirror line. The ratio is 0.467 with the Perdew--Burke--Ernzerhof (PBE) functional, against 1 for distance alone. A field-plus-gradient model fitted to six donor configurations, including C1 but excluding C1m, predicted a ratio of 0.443 before C1m was calculated. Heyd--Scuseria--Ernzerhof hybrid statics give 0.432 and PBE relaxation 0.600. Exploratory analysis suggests why direction matters: a charge's potential differs at the three vacancy nitrogens and moves the spin of the two $e'$ holes among them, more when the charge faces one nitrogen than the gap between two. $E$ follows this spin imbalance through one constant, $c\approx8$ GHz per $\mu_{\mathrm{B}}$, across donors and applied fields, whereas in PBE $D$ is nearly insensitive to it. A three-site model with one fitted screening constant reproduces the frozen-lattice $E$ to 83 MHz rms. Results are for one charge state in a free-standing monolayer; absolute $|E|$ varies by tens of percent across approximations.

</details>


### [96] [Thermodynamic Underpinnings Affecting Compressive and Tensile Plasticity in Refractory Compositionally Complex Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02202)
*Eric A. Lass,Carla Joyce C. Nocheseda,Christopher Tandoc,Yong-Jie Hu,Peter K. Liaw*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出基于CALPHAD热力学的Gamma延性判据，用于预测难熔复杂浓缩合金的拉伸塑性，并尝试结合氧溶解度同时描述本征与非本征脆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: R-CCAs在极端条件下有应用前景，但高温力学性能优异的合金常缺乏室温拉伸延性；现有VEC、Pugh比、Rice判据和D参数等半经验指标多与压缩塑性相关，仅DFT基D参数与拉伸塑性相关性较强，因此需要更具热力学基础的延性判据。

Method: 构建CALPHAD热力学延性参数Gamma，纳入BCC与HCP相的自由能差、BCC(110)表面界面自由能以及经电负性修正的晶格畸变；并将Gamma与CALPHAD计算的氧溶解度结合，以纳入间隙氧等外因脆化贡献。

Result: Gamma参数与拉伸延性的相关性优于D参数，在压缩塑性方面表现相当；其改进幅度不大，但体现了热力学基础判据对捕捉本征延性行为的必要性，同时简要讨论了间隙氧含量对脆性的外因影响。

Conclusion: 热力学 grounded 的Gamma判据可作为预测R-CCAs拉伸塑性的更合理起点；将Gamma与CALPHAD氧溶解度联合，是同时捕捉本征与外因延性贡献的一阶尝试。

Abstract: Refractory compositionally complex alloys (R-CCAs) show great promise as next generation structural materials for extreme conditions, but alloys with excellent high temperature mechanical properties most often exhibit virtually zero room temperature tensile ductility, hindering potential commercial application. Several semi-empirical metrics have been employed for predicting plasticity in R-CCAs, including valence electron concentration, Pugh ratio, Rice criteria, and the D-parameter. While many currently available ductility metrics correlate very well when compressive plasticity is considered, only the density functional theory (DFT) based D-parameter shows a strong correlation in tension. A new ductility metric is developed using CALculation of PHAse Diagrams (CALPHAD) thermodynamics, Gamma, with includes the free energy difference between the body-centered cubic (BCC) and hexagonal close-packed (HCP) phases, the interfacial free energy of a (110) BCC surface, and an electronegativity-corrected lattice distortion. The Gamma parameter exhibits better correlation with tensile ductility than D-parameter and performs equally well in compression. While this improvement is modest, a thermodynamically grounded ductility metric is clearly required to capture intrinsic ductility behavior. Extrinsic contributions to brittle behavior in R-CCAs, namely interstitial oxygen content, are also briefly discussed. Combining the Gamma ductility parameter with CALPHAD-calculated oxygen solubility is proposed as a first order attempt to capture both intrinsic and extrinsic contributions to ductile behavior in R-CCAs.

</details>


### [97] [Ferroelectric Switching in x-cut MgO-doped Thin-Film Lithium Niobate: Implications for Periodic Poling and Domain-Wall Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02233)
*Niels Staal,Michael R\"using,Tobias Babai-Hemati,Boris Koppitz,Nivedita Vishnukumar,Laura Bollmers,Noah Spiegelberg,Philipp Mues,Sebastian Lengeling,Tobias Schwabe,J. Christoph Scheytt,Christof Eigner,Lukas M. Eng,Laura Padberg,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作系统测量了x切5 mol% MgO掺杂薄膜铌酸锂在共面电极下的铁电翻转，发现高矫顽场主要由翻转动力学和电极几何导致，而非薄膜本征性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄膜铌酸锂是集成光子和畴壁电子器件的重要平台，但其周期性极化通常需要超过30 kV/mm的电场，远高于块体同成分和MgO掺杂铌酸锂的矫顽场（约21 kV/mm和4.5 kV/mm），且薄膜中的铁电翻转机制认识不足，成为器件集成瓶颈。

Method: 对x切、5 mol% MgO掺杂薄膜铌酸锂采用与周期性极化兼容的共面电极，结合正-上-负-下（PUND）方法和二次谐波显微术（SHM）进行系统的铁电滞回测量，并分析电压扫描速率和电极几何对矫顽场的影响。

Result: 测得自发极化强度为(0.72±0.02) C/m²，与块体值一致；矫顽场随扫描速率和电极几何在24–40 kV/mm间变化。高矫顽场来源包括快速电压扫描、指状电极和共面表面电极几何。结合块体数据，矫顽场在五个数量级扫描速率范围内遵循单一幂律，表明高场由翻转动力学主导而非本征薄膜性质。表观内建电场依赖电极几何，反向翻转会留下埋藏且电学隔离的畴。

Conclusion: 研究为可规模化、与制备工艺兼容的周期性极化提供了指导，有助于突破薄膜铌酸锂器件集成的主要瓶颈，并为薄膜铌酸锂畴壁器件的写入和擦除操作提供依据。

Abstract: Thin-film lithium niobate (TFLN) revives a mature ferroelectric as a wafer-scale platform for integrated photonics and, increasingly, for domain-wall electronics such as nonvolatile memories and memristors. Both applications rely on controlled ferroelectric switching, which is poorly understood in TFLN: periodic poling consistently requires electric fields above 30~kV/mm, far above the coercive fields of about 21~kV/mm and 4.5~kV/mm of bulk congruent and 5~mol\% MgO-doped lithium niobate, respectively. Here, we present systematic ferroelectric hysteresis measurements on x-cut, 5~mol\% MgO-doped TFLN with coplanar electrodes as used for periodic poling, combining the positive-up-negative-down (PUND) method with second-harmonic microscopy (SHM). We obtain a spontaneous polarization of $(0.72\pm0.02)$~C/m$^2$, consistent with bulk values, while the coercive field varies between 24 and 40~kV/mm with the voltage ramp rate and depends on the electrode geometry. We identify three contributions to the high coercive fields: (i) fast voltage ramps, (ii) finger electrodes, and (iii) the coplanar surface-electrode geometry. Together with bulk data, the coercive field follows a single power law of the ramp rate over five orders of magnitude, showing that the high fields are dominated by switching dynamics rather than by intrinsic thin-film properties. Moreover, the apparent internal field depends on the electrode geometry, and reverse switching leaves buried, electrically isolated domains. These results provide guidelines for scalable, fab-compatible periodic poling---one of the principal bottlenecks of TFLN device integration---and for the write and erase operation of TFLN domain-wall devices.

</details>


### [98] [Band Alignment of Grafted versus Directly Bonded Si/Ge Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02259)
*Pargam Vashishtha,Yang Liu,Tsung-Han Tsai,Yi Lu,Samuel A. Haessly,Chenyu Wang,Nanditha M. Dissanayake,Edward,K. Huang,Zhenqiang Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过对比Al2O3介导的半导体嫁接与直接键合，发现Si/Ge异质结的界面形成方式可显著调控其能带对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体异质结的电子性质受异质界面局部键合与静电环境影响；作者旨在研究界面形成机制如何改变Si/Ge异质结的能带对齐。

Method: 采用界面敏感的X射线光电子能谱结合Kraut分析提取能带偏移，并用拉曼光谱区分两种界面构型。

Result: 两种结构均为I型能带对齐，但价带偏移不同：直接键合Si/Ge约为0.08 eV，而嫁接的Si/Al2O3/Ge约为0.21 eV，同时导带偏移相应减小；拉曼光谱可区分两种界面构型，核心能级位移和能带偏移表明电子结构对界面键合及静电环境敏感。

Conclusion: 即使材料对相同，界面形成路径也可作为调控异质结能带对齐的额外自由度，为界面工程化的电子与光电子器件提供途径。

Abstract: The electronic properties of semiconductor heterostructures are influenced by the local bonding and electrostatic environment at the heterointerface. Here, we investigate how the interface-formation mechanism modifies the band alignment of Si/Ge heterostructures by directly comparing Al2O3-mediated semiconductor grafting with direct bonding. Interface-sensitive X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy combined with Kraut analysis reveals type-I band alignment for both structures, but with different band offsets: the valence-band offset increases from ~0.08 eV for directly bonded Si/Ge to ~0.21 eV for grafted Si/Al2O3/Ge, accompanied by a corresponding reduction in the conduction-band offset. Raman spectroscopy further distinguishes the two interfacial configurations. The different core-level shifts and band offsets indicate that the electronic structure is sensitive to the interfacial bonding and electrostatic environment introduced by the two integration schemes. These results demonstrate that, even for an identical Si/Ge material pair, the interface-formation pathway provides an additional degree of freedom for controlling heterojunction band alignment, offering a route toward interface-engineered electronic and optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [99] [Defect Chemistry of Hafnia-Based Ferroelectrics under Oxide-Electrode Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02317)
*Sehyeok Jeon,Aloysius Soon,Yongjin Shin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用密度泛函理论计算研究LSMO电极化学约束下HfO2中氧空位及La、Sr、Mn相关缺陷的形成与稳定性，并扩展到Hf0.5Zr0.5O2和ZrO2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HfO2/LSMO外延异质结因电极依赖的缺陷化学而受关注，但在LSMO约束的化学环境下缺陷的形成与稳定性仍缺乏系统理解。

Method: 使用密度泛函理论，在LSMO稳定性限定的O化学势范围内系统计算HfO2中的氧空位以及La、Sr、Mn相关间隙和替位缺陷，并计算正交相HfO2中的缺陷复合，进一步比较Hf0.5Zr0.5O2与ZrO2。

Result: LSMO稳定性显著收窄可及O化学势窗口并改变缺陷形成能；La和Sr替位缺陷主要受优选离子价态控制，氧空位和Mn替位缺陷的电荷态对局域原子环境敏感；正交HfO2中缺陷复合具有负结合能，表明有利缔合，La与Mn相关缺陷复合存在热力学竞争且相对稳定性依赖费米能级和氧化学势；Hf0.5Zr0.5O2和ZrO2显示共同氧空位趋势，而LSMO衍生缺陷稳定性更依赖宿主材料和晶相。

Conclusion: 结果为理解电极约束化学环境和宿主结构如何影响HfO2基材料中的缺陷形成、电荷补偿及缺陷复合稳定性提供了热力学基础。

Abstract: HfO$_2$ heterostructures with La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$ (LSMO) epitaxial electrodes have attracted interest as ferroelectric systems with electrode-dependent defect chemistry. However, the formation and stability of defects under LSMO-constrained chemical conditions remain poorly understood. In this work, we use density functional theory calculations to systematically investigate oxygen vacancies and La-, Sr-, and Mn-related interstitial and substitutional defects in HfO$_2$ under chemical environments constrained by the LSMO electrode. We find that LSMO stability substantially narrows the accessible O chemical potential range relative to bulk HfO$_2$, altering the predicted defect formation energies. While La and Sr substitutional defects are governed primarily by preferred ionic valence states, oxygen vacancies and Mn substitutional defects exhibit charge states sensitive to the local atomic environment. In addition, defect-complex calculations in orthorhombic HfO$_2$ reveal negative binding energies, indicating favorable defect association. The La- and Mn-related defect complexes exhibit thermodynamic competition, with their relative stability depending on the Fermi level and oxygen chemical potential. Lastly, extending the analysis to Hf$_{0.5}$Zr$_{0.5}$O$_2$ and ZrO$_2$ reveals common oxygen-vacancy trends, while the stability of LSMO-derived defects depends more strongly on the host material and crystal phase. These results provide a thermodynamic basis for understanding how electrode-constrained chemical environments and host structures influence defect formation, charge compensation, and defect-complex stability in HfO$_2$-based materials.

</details>


### [100] [Fermi surface and field-induced Lifshitz transition in the antiferromagnet NbMnP exhibiting an anomalous Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02360)
*Taichi Terashima,Yuya Hattori,David Graf,Yuki Arai,Hitoshi Sugawara,Hisashi Kotegawa,Ibuki Terada,Michi-To Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 对Q=0反铁磁体NbMnP进行Shubnikov-de Haas振荡测量，发现低场SdH频率与基于中子衍射确定磁结构的第一性原理能带计算一致，并在高场观察到Lifshitz转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NbMnP表现出大反常霍尔效应，需要检验第一性原理计算对其费米面和磁输运性质的可靠性，并理解高场下磁输运行为。

Method: 在最高17.5 T和41.5 T磁场下测量SdH振荡，将低场频率与基于B3g+B2u非共线磁结构的第一性原理能带计算比较，并提取有效质量。

Result: 低场SdH频率与计算能带结构吻合；有效质量相对计算能带质量增强约2倍；41.5 T高场测量显示伴随SdH频谱显著演化的Lifshitz转变。

Conclusion: 第一性原理计算可为功能反铁磁材料开发提供可靠工具，但理解其高场磁输运性质时必须考虑磁场诱导的费米面变化。

Abstract: We performed Shubnikov--de Haas (SdH) oscillation measurements on the $\mathbf{Q}=0$ antiferromagnet NbMnP, which exhibits a large anomalous Hall effect. The SdH frequencies observed in the low-field region up to 17.5~T are well described by first-principles band-structure calculations based on the $B_{3g}+B_{2u}$ noncollinear magnetic structure determined by neutron diffraction. The effective masses are enhanced by approximately a factor of two relative to the calculated band masses. High-field measurements up to 41.5~T reveal a Lifshitz transition accompanied by a pronounced evolution of the SdH frequency spectrum. These results confirm that first-principles calculations provide a reliable tool for the development of functional antiferromagnetic materials, while also demonstrating that field-induced changes in the Fermi surface itself need to be taken into account in understanding their high-field magnetotransport properties.

</details>


### [101] [The effect of local stacking fault fluctuations on dislocation glide and the interplay with solid solution strengthening in Al-Mg alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02386)
*Hyunsoo Lee,Xin Liu,Liam Myhill,Yang Li,David Rodney,Pierre-Antoine Geslin,Nikhil Chandra Admal,Yinan Cui,Giacomo Po,Enrique Martinez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在浓Al-Mg随机合金中，层错能涨落产生的层错力具有空间相关性，尤其沿滑移方向呈稳健负相关，并显著提高位错滑移的临界分切应力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浓合金中不同尺寸溶质的随机排列引起非平凡相关的位移与应力场涨落，并造成层错能局域涨落，从而对约束层错的偏位错施加波动的层错力；这些层错噪声的空间相关特征及其对位错滑移的影响尚需厘清。

Method: 针对不同Mg含量的模型浓Al-Mg随机合金，分析螺型、刃型和混合位错的滑移行为，表征层错力的空间相关性，并讨论层错能涨落与固溶噪声之间的相互作用。

Result: 层错力呈空间相关，类似长程弹性应力，并沿滑移方向表现出稳健负相关；其他方向的相关性较弱但可不可忽略，取决于原子间相互作用；层错力涨落显著贡献位错滑移所需的临界分切应力。

Conclusion: 层错能涨落并非无关噪声，其空间相关性和方向性会显著影响浓合金中的位错滑移阻力；浓合金强化与塑性模型应考虑层错噪声与固溶噪声的联合作用。

Abstract: Concentrated alloys are characterized by a random arrangement of solute atoms of different sizes, which generates spatially fluctuating displacement and stress fields with non-trivial correlations. This chemical disorder also induces local fluctuations in stacking-fault (SF) energies, giving rise to fluctuating SF forces acting on the partial dislocations bounding the SF. In this work, we investigate the characteristics of the SF noise, including spatial correlations and the impact of such SF energy fluctuations on dislocation glide. Specifically, the gliding behavior of screw, edge, and mixed dislocations in a model concentrated Al-Mg random alloy with varying Mg content is analyzed. The interplay between SF energy fluctuations and solid-solution noise is also discussed. We show that SF forces are spatially correlated, in close analogy with long-range elastic stresses, and exhibit a robust negative correlation along the slip direction. We identify the physical origin of these correlations and demonstrate that they significantly contribute to the critical resolved shear stress for dislocations to glide. In contrast, correlations in other directions are weaker but can still be non-negligible, depending on the underlying interatomic interactions.

</details>


### [102] [Machine-Learned Interatomic Potential for the Mo-S-Al-O System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02403)
*Emir Bilgili,Nicholas Taormina,Simon R. Phillpot,Youping Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 作者构建了 Mo-S-Al-O 体系的机器学习神经演化势 NEP，用于研究 MoS2 在 c 面蓝宝石 α-Al2O3(0001) 上的异质外延，并在界面能、缺陷与晶界等性质上高精度复现 DFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要兼具 DFT 精度和计算效率的势函数，以研究 MoS2/蓝宝石异质外延中界面、取向依赖能量及原子尺度缺陷形成机制。

Method: 基于约 110 万条经筛选的 DFT 构型蒸馏出 63,611 个 PBE+D3 结构训练 NEP，并采用能量差损失函数直接优化界面与取向依赖能量。

Result: 留出集能量/力误差为 29 meV/atom 和 0.23 eV/Å；晶格常数误差 <0.3%，弹性常数 <17%，声子色散平均偏差 <2.5%，表面能 <1.3%；界面粘附能最小值误差 <0.8%，界面能 <2.2%，并复现最稳定单层取向；边缘与点缺陷能量序 Spearman ρ=0.995，镜像孪晶界 ρ=1.0，倾斜晶界能误差 <8%（ρ=0.98），层错能垒误差 <14%。

Conclusion: 该 NEP 可作为研究二维异质外延中缺陷形成原子机制的高精度模拟工具。

Abstract: We develop a machine-learned neuroevolution potential (NEP) for the Mo-S-Al-O system, targeting MoS$_2$ heteroepitaxy on c-plane sapphire, $\alpha$-Al$_2$O$_3$(0001). The potential is trained on 63,611 PBE+D3 structures distilled from about 1.1 million curated density-functional-theory (DFT) configurations, using an energy-difference loss function that directly targets interfacial and orientation-dependent energies. On held-out data, the energy and force errors are 29 meV/atom and 0.23 eV/$\mathring{A}$. Validated against DFT on a suite of physical properties, the NEP reproduces the lattice constants within 0.3 %, the elastic constants within 17 %, the phonon dispersions with mean deviations below 2.5%, and the surface energies within 1.3 %. At the MoS$_2$/sapphire interface, it reproduces the adhesion energy minimum within 0.8 %, the interfacial energy within 2.2 % and the most stable orientation of the monolayer. It preserves the DFT-predicted energetic ordering of edges, point defects (Spearman $\rho$ = 0.995) and mirror-twin boundaries ($\rho$ = 1.0), and reproduces tilt grain-boundary energies within 8 % ($\rho$ = 0.98) and stacking-fault energy barriers within 14 %. The resulting potential enables future studies of the atomic-scale mechanisms of defect formation in two-dimensional heteroepitaxy.

</details>


### [103] [Moir\'e reconstruction at a twisted SrTiO3 interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02464)
*Pengcheng Fei,Meiyu Wang,Suzhen Liu,Zhongwei Zhang,Yang Wu,Bo Hao,Hanhao Zhang,Dingcheng Ma,Shijie Yang,Weiye Nie,Jiacheng Wang,Jibo Xu,Geliang Shen,Junjie Wang,Chi Ding,Huifeng Chen,Sheng Xu,Xinrui Yang,Lu Han,Haocheng Quan,Ling-Ling Ma,Zaiyao Fei,Liqi Zhou,Songhua Cai,Zhengbin Gu,Yu Deng,Jian Sun,Di Wu,Yuefeng Nie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用具有明确SrO-TiO2界面终止的原子级平整SrTiO3薄膜，直接观察到埋藏界面处不同于刚性堆垛几何莫尔的周期性结构重构，并发现其伴随极性畸变、Ti电子结构调制及微波电导增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转氧化物双层是研究莫尔物理的重要平台，但氧化物表面污染、粗糙度及AO/BO2双终止会阻碍埋藏界面结构的识别；因此需要以明确终止的氧化物膜来澄清扭转界面的本征重构与物性。

Method: 使用原子级平整且以SrO-TiO2界面构型为主的SrTiO3自支撑薄膜，构建扭转双层，并对埋藏界面进行直接结构表征，同时关联界面极性畸变、Ti电子结构与微波响应。

Result: 在埋藏界面观察到莫尔周期的结构重构，其不同于刚性堆叠产生的几何莫尔；重构界面具有类头对头极性畸变、莫尔调制的Ti电子结构，并在原本顺电绝缘的SrTiO3中表现出增强的微波导纳。

Conclusion: 明确界面终止条件下，扭转钙钛矿氧化物中的莫尔重构可成为诱导新兴电学行为的一条途径。

Abstract: Twisted oxide double layers have emerged as a promising platform for exploring moir\'e physics, including polar topologies and emergent electronic phases. However, the intrinsic complexity of oxide surfaces, such as contamination, roughness, and the presence of two distinct terminations (AO and BO2), can complicate the identification of buried interfacial structures. Here, using atomically flat SrTiO3 membranes with a predominantly SrO-TiO2 interfacial configuration, we directly visualize a moir\'e-periodic structural reconstruction at the buried interface that is distinct from the geometric moir\'e of rigid stacking. The reconstructed interface hosts head-to-head-like polar distortions, moir\'e-modulated Ti electronic structure, and enhanced microwave admittance in otherwise paraelectric and insulating SrTiO3. Our work demonstrates that, with a well-defined termination, moir\'e reconstruction could serve as a route to emergent electrical behavior in twisted perovskite oxides.

</details>


### [104] [Data-driven candidate structure ensemble generation beyond the convex hull with crystALL approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02584)
*Adam M. Krajewski,Ricardo Amaral,Jonathan W. Siegel,Luke A. Myers,Rushi Gong,Shun-Li Shang,Zi-Kui Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文主张材料发现不应只搜索凸包上的基态稳定结构，而应预测真实条件下由稳定/亚稳态组成的统计遍历构型集合；为此提出数据驱动方法 crystALL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动材料发现过度偏向仅基于基态 DFT 的“新稳定结构”，难以发现亚稳相性质、解释实验现象和全面建模热力学行为，并可能随迭代产生类似 LLM 模型坍缩的 AI/ML 发现偏差。

Method: crystALL 利用 OPTIMADE 与 MPDD 中约 500 万个弛豫结构，通过物种替换、pySIPFENN 机器学习特征空间分析、聚类与能量最小化/性质匹配进行代表性结构选择，并构建用于实验或从头算验证的候选集合；同时以匿名化成分区间搜索处理实验成分不确定性。

Result: 摘要未给出定量性能结果；作者称 crystALL 是务实且强大的数据驱动方法，可发现凸包之外的构型集合，强调结构多样性而非仅稳定性，并能匹配传统方法难以处理的实验观测。

Conclusion: crystALL 的集合聚焦理念优于主流凸包搜索范式，可减少 AI/ML 材料发现偏差，并支持亚稳相、实验解释和热力学建模等更全面的材料研究。

Abstract: Real materials consist of multiple stable or metastable states under real-world conditions, existing as statistical ergodic ensembles of configurations rather than ideal crystals. Yet, data-driven computational materials discovery increasingly biases itself toward finding "new stable structures" from ground-state DFT calculations alone. This limits our long-term ability to discover materials whose properties emerge from metastable phases, to explain experimental observations, or to model thermodynamic behavior comprehensively. Predicting ergodic sets of atomic configurations of known and hypothetical solids is a fundamental challenge in theoretical crystallography, approached via many complementary methods. We discuss this conceptually and introduce crystALL, a pragmatic and powerful data-driven approach to discovering ensembles beyond the convex hull, embracing structural diversity over stability alone, enabling robust experiment matching in cases evading traditional methods. crystALL leverages 5 million relaxed structures from OPTIMADE and MPDD to rapidly generate a comprehensive candidate set by: (1) species substitutions; (2) ML feature-space analysis with pySIPFENN; (3) representative structure selection via clustering and energy-minimization or property matching; and (4) construction of the final candidate ensemble for experimental or ab initio validation. We also uniquely address the common challenge of experimental composition uncertainties spanning many integer stoichiometries by new efficient searches across anonymized compositional ranges rather than fixed formulas. Overall, crystALL's ensemble-focused philosophy provides critical advantages over the prevailing convex-hull-searching paradigm, which often fails to account for key experimental observations and increases AI/ML discovery bias over time - akin to model collapse in LLMs retrained on iteratively generated content.

</details>


### [105] [Nonadiabatic molecular dynamics simulation with external field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02606)
*Chengji Li,Fan Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出CPA-NAMD-extF：在预计算有限温度离子轨迹上，将显式外场耦合与电子密度自洽更新结合，实现外场驱动电子响应的第一性原理密度矩阵模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPA-NAMD无法模拟外场下的载流子动力学，需要一种兼顾经典路径近似计算效率与外场响应能力的方法。

Method: 在CPA-NAMD框架中引入显式外场项并自洽更新电子密度，保持经典路径近似的效率；用时变偶极、有限场电流和线性光学响应与常规TDDFT基准对比验证。

Result: 基准测试与TDDFT吻合良好；成功应用于晶态和非晶SiO2的线性吸收谱及二维杂化钙钛矿(BA)2CsPb2Br7。有限温度计算表明二阶光电流响应受结构涨落强烈影响，实空间DC电流密度揭示显著的Br中心涡旋电流并分析其微观起源。

Conclusion: 该方法将CPA-NAMD扩展到外场驱动电子响应模拟，适用于大尺度、结构复杂且有限温度体系，并能揭示结构涨落与局域电流特征。

Abstract: Nonadiabatic molecular dynamics simulations with classical path approximation (CPA-NAMD) cannot simulate the carrier dynamics under an external field. We develop CPA-NAMD-extF, a first-principles density-matrix framework for calculating field-driven electronic responses along precomputed finite-temperature ionic trajectories. The method combines explicit external-field coupling with self-consistent updates of electronic density while retaining the computational efficiency of the classical-path approximation. Benchmarks against regular TDDFT methods show close agreement for time-dependent dipoles, finite-field currents, and linear optical responses. Applications to the linear absorption spectrum of crystalline and amorphous SiO$_2$ demonstrate its capability to large and structurally complex systems. This method is also applied to the 2D hybrid perovskite $(\mathrm{BA})_2\mathrm{CsPb}_2\mathrm{Br}_7$. Finite-temperature calculations show that the second-order opto-current response is strongly affected by structural fluctuations. Real-space DC current density further reveals pronounced Br-centered vortex currents, and its microscopic origin is investigated.

</details>


### [106] [Collective dipole effects amplify field-driven ion transport in amorphous Li$_3$PO$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02617)
*Haruka Doyu,Anh Khoa Augustin Lu,Satoshi Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用机器学习势与机器学习Born有效电荷模型进行分子动力学模拟，比较非晶Li3PO4与含Li空位晶体在强电场下的Li迁移，发现非晶相具有更强的非线性场增强，机制是周围环境原子随跃迁运动导致电场做功分布变宽，从而使强辅助跃迁主导漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶固体电解质常优于晶体，但在强电场下无序如何影响离子输运尚不清楚，尤其缺乏从线性到强非线性区的微观机制理解。

Method: 采用机器学习原子间势和机器学习Born有效电荷模型的分子动力学模拟，测量非晶Li3PO4与含Li空位晶体中Li的迁移率，并覆盖线性至强非线性电场区间。

Result: 非晶相的电场增强显著更强；迁移离子在两相中携带相同电荷，因此差异不来自载流子本身。非晶网络中周围原子随跃迁协同运动，使电场做功在不同跃迁间分布很宽；由于跃迁速率随做功指数增长，最强电场辅助的跃迁可主导漂移。

Conclusion: 无序通过环境而非载流子增强电场驱动输运：非晶网络中环境原子的协同运动放大电场辅助跃迁，这一集体偶极机制可能普遍存在于无序离子导体中。

Abstract: Amorphous solid electrolytes often outperform their crystalline counterparts, but how disorder shapes ionic transport under strong electric fields is poorly understood. We use molecular dynamics with a machine-learned interatomic potential and a machine-learned Born-effective-charge model to measure Li mobility in amorphous Li$_3$PO$_4$ and in a crystal with Li vacancies, from the linear to the strongly nonlinear regime. The amorphous phase shows a much stronger field enhancement, and we trace this to a collective dipole effect. The migrating ion carries the same charge in both phases. Alone it would not make the amorphous phase respond more strongly. What makes the difference is the environment. In the amorphous network the surrounding atoms move with the hop. Their motion makes the work the field performs vary widely between hops. Since hopping rates grow exponentially with the work, the most strongly assisted hops can dominate the drift. Disorder thus enhances field-driven transport through the environment rather than the carrier itself, a mechanism that may operate in disordered ion conductors in general.

</details>


### [107] [Universal model of the growth of III-V tetrahedral semiconductors on polar surfaces: pyramids and glue atoms. VI. Surface diffusion on a nonmetal-terminated surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02723)
*Pawel Strak,Stanis{\l}aw Krukowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 从头算模拟研究了GaN、GaP、GaAs和GaSb非金属端接表面上吸附物的表面扩散与脱离，揭示扩散通过三角锥结构的枢轴旋转进行，并给出非金属吸附原子的脱离势垒及宏观大的扩散长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明四面体半导体非金属端接表面上混合覆盖吸附物的扩散机制、激活势垒和脱离行为，为理解吸附原子向台阶输运及表面生长过程提供微观依据。

Method: 采用从头算计算和能量路径确定程序，模拟GaN(000-1)及GaP、GaAs、GaSb(-1-1-1)表面的吸附物扩散；覆盖结构为第一层Ga吸附原子及其上方非金属原子构成的三角锥。通过初态能量与激活络合物能量之差获得扩散势垒，并分别假设终态为原子态和分子态计算非金属吸附原子的脱离能。

Result: 三角锥结构在表面跳跃中保持稳定，扩散通过另外两个基底Ga吸附原子围绕一个不动Ga吸附原子旋转（枢轴）并进入原空位实现。所有原子向原子态脱离的势垒很高，约5 eV；向分子态脱离时，N约1 eV，P和As高于2 eV，Sb未确定。

Conclusion: 非金属吸附原子的表面扩散长度宏观上很大，表明其可抵达台阶；分子态脱离势垒差异与N2高等离解能相关。

Abstract: Ab initio calculations were used to simulate surface diffusion of adsorbates on the nonmetal-terminated surface of tetrahedral semiconductors: GaN, GaP, GaAs and GaSb. These surfaces are wurtzite gallium nitride, i.e. GaN(000-1) surface, zinc blende gallium phosphide, i.e. GaP(-1-1-1) gallium arsenide, GaAs (-1-1-1) and gallium antimonide GaSb (-1-1-1) surfaces. The mixed coverage consists of Ga adatoms in the first layer with a nonmetal atom attached above them forming a triangular-based pyramid. The pyramidal structures are stable during surface jumps involving rotations around one of the basal Ga adatoms. Thus, the diffusion proceeds via rotation (pivot) of the pair of the other basal Ga adatoms around an immobile Ga adatom moving them into new, originally empty sites.
