Using an energy-path determination procedure, the diffusion barriers were obtained as the difference between the initial site energy and the activated complex state energy. The detachment energy of the NM adatom was obtained assuming a final state in atomic and molecular form. The energy barrier for detachment to the atomic state is high for all atoms, on the order of 5 eV. For the molecular state these barriers are different: smaller for nitrogen, on the order of 1 eV, which is related to the high dissociation energy of the N2 molecule. For P and As the barrier is above 2 eV. For Sb it was not determined. Accordingly, the surface diffusion length is macroscopically large, indicating that all NM adatoms arrive at the steps.

</details>


### [108] [Universal model of the growth of III-V tetrahedral semiconductors on polar surfaces: pyramids and glue atoms. VII. Step kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02735)
*Pawel Strak,Stanis{\l}aw Krukowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用从头算模拟研究GaN、GaP、GaAs和GaSb非金属终止表面台阶处晶格锥体的附着动力学，发现锥体不能直接并入台阶，需额外非金属“胶”原子辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解四面体半导体非金属终止表面上台阶生长动力学，以及Ga吸附原子和高覆盖度下稳定Ga-非金属锥体如何并入晶格台阶。

Method: 用从头算计算模拟新晶格片段（ pyramids）在GaN(000-1)、GaP(-1-1-1)、GaAs(-1-1-1)和GaSb(-1-1-1)表面台阶处的附着动力学；考虑扩散物种为高覆盖度Ga吸附原子或与非金属原子结合形成稳定锥体的Ga吸附原子，并分析来自气相或从表面台面脱附的非金属原子作为“胶”原子的作用。

Result: 这些锥体到达台阶后不能直接附着，因为锥体底部的Ga吸附原子不能直接并入台阶；它们需要额外非金属“胶”原子参与才能并入台阶。

Conclusion: 非金属“胶”原子是该类非金属终止Ga基半导体表面台阶并入锥体晶格片段的关键媒介，揭示了台阶生长中直接附着受限及非金属辅助并入机制。

Abstract: Ab initio calculations were used to simulate the kinetics of attachment of new segments of the crystalline lattice (pyramids) at steps on the nonmetal-terminated surface of tetrahedral semiconductors: GaN, GaP, GaAs and GaSb. These surfaces are wurtzite gallium nitride, i.e. GaN(000-1) surface, zinc blende gallium phosphide, i.e. GaP(-1-1-1) gallium arsenide, GaAs(-1-1-1) and gallium antimonide GaSb(-1-1-1) surfaces. Diffusion species are gallium adatoms at high coverage or gallium adatoms connected with nonmetal atom creating stable pyramids. These pyramids arrive at the steps, and cannot be attached be directly because the Ga adatom at the pyramid base cannot be attached directly. Therefore they are incorporated into the step by additional nonmetal atoms glue atoms arriving from the vapor or detached from other segments of the surface terraces.

</details>


### [109] [High-Entropy A-Site Disorder Enhances Oxygen Evolution Reaction Activity and Stability in Nickelate Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02796)
*Min-Ju Choi,Le Wang,Krishna Prasad Koirala,Hyoju Park,Mark E. Bowden,Elizabeth Helen Krenkel,Aiping Chen,Jueli Shi,Conan Weiland,Iradwikanari Waluyo,Adrian Hunt,Peter V. Sushko,Yingge Du*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用外延A位高熵稀土镍酸盐薄膜，揭示外延应变如何通过缺陷形成、Ni价态和稀土衍生电子态调控析氧反应（OER）活性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土镍酸盐是活跃的OER氧化物电催化剂，但其A位化学、氧化学计量和外延应变敏感；尤其在化学无序的稀土亚晶格中，应变如何影响缺陷、电子结构与催化性能尚需阐明。

Method: 制备外延A位高熵(La0.2Pr0.2Nd0.2Sm0.2Eu0.2)NiO3薄膜，结合结构与谱学表征（含O K边X射线吸收谱）和第一性原理计算，比较拉伸与压缩应变对Ruddlesden-Popper缺陷、NiO6网络连通性、Ni氧化态、RE 4f-O 2p-Ni 3d杂化及OER性能的影响。

Result: 拉伸应变促进Ruddlesden-Popper层错形成、打断角共享NiO6网络并降低平均Ni氧化态；费米能级附近占据态密度基本不变，但O K边XAS显示低能未占据态增加。计算表明拉伸应变使RE 4f衍生态向费米能级移动并增强RE 4f-O 2p-Ni 3d杂化。由此获得更早的OER起始电位、更强的预催化Ni氧化还原特征和高于压缩应变薄膜及代表性单A位镍酸盐的活性；高熵镍酸盐较LaNiO3具有更强抗OER诱导结构退化能力。

Conclusion: A位构型无序与外延应变可协同调控缺陷形成和稀土衍生电子态，为提升钙钛矿氧化物电催化剂性能提供了设计路径。

Abstract: Rare-earth nickelates are among the most active oxide electrocatalysts for the oxygen evolution reaction (OER), owing to their tunable Ni-O covalency, near-unity eg occupancy, and sensitivity to A-site chemistry, oxygen stoichiometry, and epitaxial strain. Here, we use epitaxial A-site high-entropy (La0.2Pr0.2Nd0.2Sm0.2Eu0.2)NiO3 thin films to reveal how epitaxial strain influences defect formation, Ni valence, electronic structure, and OER activity in a chemically disordered rare-earth (RE) sublattice. Tensile strain promotes Ruddlesden-Popper fault formation, interrupts the corner-sharing NiO6 network, and lowers the average Ni oxidation state. Although the occupied density of states near the Fermi level remains largely unchanged, O K-edge X-ray absorption spectroscopy reveals increased low-energy unoccupied states under tensile strain. First-principles calculations suggest that tensile strain shifts RE 4f-derived states toward the Fermi level, enhancing RE 4f-O 2p-Ni 3d hybridization. These coupled structural and electronic changes lead to earlier OER onset, stronger pre-catalytic Ni redox features, and higher activity than compressively strained films and representative single-A-site nickelates. The high-entropy nickelate also shows enhanced resistance to OER-induced structural degradation compared with LaNiO3. These results suggest that A-site configurational disorder and epitaxial strain can jointly tune defect formation and rare-earth-derived electronic states, providing a route for improving perovskite oxide electrocatalysts.

</details>


### [110] [Magnetic order in Co[Cr(CN)$_6$]$_{2/3}\cdot z$H$_2$O and its interplay with correlated vacancy order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02820)
*Rebecca E. Witte,Elodie A. Harbourne,John Cattermull,Tristan N. Dolling,Simon J. Cassidy,Fabio Orlandi,Pascal Manuel,Andrew L. Goodwin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用中子粉末衍射解析了Co[Cr(CN)6]2/3·zH2O的磁结构，并结合Monte Carlo模型表明其磁有序对六氰基金属酸盐空位关联具有稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBAs常偏离立方母结构，其六氰基金属酸盐空位分布与磁有序之间的关系尚不清晰，且需澄清磁致伸缩行为是否具有普适性。

Method: 采用中子粉末衍射测定钴(II)六氰基铬酸盐的磁结构，检查磁有序温度附近的磁致伸缩；随后构建简单Monte Carlo模型，分析空位分布及空位关联对磁有序的影响。

Result: 未观察到磁有序温度处的磁致伸缩，与相关PBA的报道行为不同；模型显示磁有序对空位的程度和关联性质均较稳健，仅在临界区出现轻微影响。

Conclusion: 空位关联并非该类PBA磁有序的主要决定因素，因此相同化学组成但空位关联不同的PBAs可能表现出相似的磁行为。

Abstract: Prussian blue analogues (PBAs) are versatile hybrid framework materials that often exhibit local and long-range departures from their cubic parent structure, influencing their functionality. Long-range magnetic order is common in PBAs, owing to magnetic interactions between transition metal ions via bridging cyanide ligands in the structure. Here we report the magnetic structure of cobalt (II) hexacyanochromate, Co[Cr(CN)$_6$]$_{2/3}\cdot z$H$_2$O, determined using neutron powder diffraction. No evidence for magnetostriction at the magnetic ordering temperature is observed in contrast to the reported behaviour of a related PBA. Following experimental characterisation of the magnetic order of this PBA, we employ a simple Monte Carlo model to gain a better understanding of the relationship between hexacyanometallate vacancy distributions and magnetic ordering behaviour in these materials. This model shows that magnetic ordering is robust to the extent and nature of vacancy correlations, with only minor effects noticeable in the critical region, suggesting that PBAs with the same composition but different vacancy correlations might be expected to show similar magnetic behaviour.

</details>


### [111] [Molecular Curvature Enables Tubular {\pi}-Stacked Pathways in Three-Dimensional Conductive Metal-Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02945)
*Weishan Li,Jia-Xiang Zhang,Tongyang Zhao,Hao Chen,Tianyang Chen,Ze-Fan Yao,Jin-Hu Dou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用本征扭曲的蝴蝶形DBC配体构建镧系MOF，获得具有管状π堆叠通道和可调孔环境的互穿三维框架，并实现混合电子-质子传导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导电MOF通常依赖平面芳香配体的常规π堆叠；作者希望以本征扭曲配体作为几何设计手段，调控芳香堆积并构筑非常规三维导电框架。

Method: 采用蝴蝶形dibenzo[g,p]chrysene-2,3,6,7,10,11,14,15-octaol（DBC）配体与镧系离子组装系列MOF，通过单晶X射线衍射解析结构，并研究镧系取代对堆积距离和孔环境的影响及PrDBC晶体的电子-质子传导性能。

Result: 结构显示形成互穿三维框架，芳香片段间π-π相互作用产生轴向延伸的管状π堆叠路径，并伴有一维富氢键位点通道；镧系取代保持管状框架拓扑但调节芳香堆叠距离和局部孔环境。PrDBC单晶表现为混合电子-质子传导，在338 K和30%相对湿度下电子电导率达1.62×10⁻³ S cm⁻¹，质子电导率达4.37×10⁻⁴ S cm⁻¹。

Conclusion: 分子曲率可作为构筑三维导电MOF的结构设计策略，将管状芳香堆叠与可调孔环境结合，用于混合电子-质子传导。

Abstract: Conductive metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) are commonly constructed from planar aromatic ligands, whose geometries favor conventional {\pi}-stacking arrangements. However, intrinsically twisted ligands offer an alternative geometric approach to directing aromatic packing and constructing unconventional framework architectures. Here, we employ intrinsically twisted, butterfly-shaped dibenzo[g,p]chrysene-2,3,6,7,10,11,14,15-octaol (DBC) ligands to construct a series of lanthanide MOFs. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction reveals an interpenetrated three-dimensional framework in which {\pi}-{\pi} interactions between the aromatic fragments generate axially extended tubular {\pi}-stacked pathways, alongside one-dimensional channels rich in hydrogen-bonding sites. Lanthanide substitution preserves the overall tubular framework topology while modulating aromatic stacking distances and local pore environments. PrDBC single crystals exhibit mixed electron-proton transport, with electron and proton conductivities reaching 1.62 x 10-3 and 4.37 x 10-4 S cm-1, respectively, at 338 K and 30% relative humidity. This work highlights molecular curvature as a structural design strategy for constructing three-dimensional conductive MOFs that integrate tubular aromatic stacks with tunable pore environments for mixed electron-proton transport.

</details>


### [112] [Anion Engineering Enables Concurrent Optimization of Phonon and Carrier Transport in AMg$_2$X$_2$ Zintl Thermoelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02961)
*Ning Wang,Jincheng Yue,Hui Zhang,Xingchen Shen,Wenling Ren,Shuyao Lin,Yanhui Liu,Yue-Wen Fang,Tian Cui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用第一性原理、机器学习原子间势和玻尔兹曼输运理论研究AMg2X2 Zintl化合物的声子与载流子输运，发现高阶四声子散射和阴离子取代可协同优化热导率与n型热电性能，CaMg2Bi2在900 K预测峰值ZT为3.68。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能热电材料需要同时抑制晶格热输运并增强载流子输运，因此需厘清AMg2X2体系中声子与载流子输运的微观机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算、机器学习原子间势和玻尔兹曼输运理论，研究CaAl2Si2型AMg2X2（A=Ca,Sr,Ba；X=P,As,Sb,Bi）Zintl化合物的声子与载流子输运，并进行模式分辨分析。

Result: X由P变为Bi时，声子带隙展宽限制三声子散射通道并提升四声子过程重要性；在CaMg2Bi2和BaMg2Bi2中，900 K下四声子散射主导低频载热模弛豫。Bi基系列中κ_L随Ca到Ba反常升高；CaMg2Bi2因低频群速度降低和更强非谐散射而具有更低κ_L，计入粒子型与相干贡献后900 K时κ_L为0.23 W·m−1K−1。Mg-X键共价性增强带来带边色散增强、载流子有效质量降低和导带收敛，利于n型输运，CaMg2Bi2在900 K预测峰值ZT为3.68。

Conclusion: 结果表明声子谱结构与高阶非谐性共同决定热输运，而阴离子取代引起的电子结构变化可显著改善n型热电性能，为AMg2X2体系的热电优化提供机制依据。

Abstract: The simultaneous suppression of lattice heat transport and enhancement of carrier transport is central to the development of high-performance thermoelectric materials. Here, we investigate the microscopic mechanisms governing phonon and carrier transport in CaAl$_2$Si$_2$-type AMg$_2$X$_2$ (A = Ca, Sr, Ba; X = P, As, Sb, Bi) Zintl compounds using first-principles calculations, machine-learning interatomic potentials, and Boltzmann transport theory. As X changes from P to Bi, the widening phonon band gap restricts three-phonon scattering channels and increases the relative importance of four-phonon processes. In CaMg$_2$Bi$_2$ and BaMg$_2$Bi$_2$, four-phonon scattering dominates the relaxation of low-frequency heat-carrying modes at 900 K. The Bi-based series also exhibits an anomalous increase in lattice thermal conductivity from Ca to Ba despite the increasing cation mass. Mode-resolved analysis links the lower thermal conductivity of CaMg$_2$Bi$_2$ to reduced low-frequency group velocities and stronger anharmonic scattering, with Ca-related mid-frequency vibrations providing additional four-phonon scattering channels. Including both particle-like and coherent contributions, its lattice thermal conductivity reaches 0.23 W$\cdot$m$^{-1}$K$^{-1}$ at 900 K. Meanwhile, the increasing covalent character of the vertical Mg-X bonds is accompanied by enhanced band-edge dispersion and reduced carrier effective masses. These electronic changes, together with conduction-band convergence, favor n-type transport and yield a predicted peak $ZT$ of 3.68 at 900 K in CaMg$_2$Bi$_2$. These results elucidate the interplay of phonon spectral structure and higher-order anharmonicity in thermal transport, alongside the distinct electronic response to anion substitution.

</details>


### [113] [Ultrafast Polar Topology Reversal via THz-Driven Inertial Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03108)
*Denan Li,Shi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文通过多尺度建模证明，在Pt/PbTiO3/Pt铁电纳米电容器中可稳定并利用THz脉冲实现极性拓扑反转，其机制为阻尼辅助的惯性开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电异质结构中的拓扑极性结构具有超越普通畴的功能态，但极性拓扑反转需要相反取向局域极化的协同翻转，均匀电场难以自然驱动，因此实现拓扑反转具有挑战性。

Method: 采用多尺度建模方法，研究Pt/PbTiO3/Pt纳米电容器中电极控制的边界条件对四方相PbTiO3极化的调控，并分析THz脉冲驱动的拓扑反转动力学。

Result: 电极边界条件驱动PbTiO3形成竞争的类正交和类菱方极化态，构成涡旋阵列、skyrmion晶格、偶极螺旋和偶极波等拓扑结构；同一面外THz脉冲可在数皮秒内可逆地将双q偶极波在简并拓扑变体间切换并保持空间调制。该过程由阻尼辅助惯性开关实现：THz脉冲注入能量驱动大角度极化旋转跨越浅势垒，耗散移除多余能量并将系统锁定在反转态。

Conclusion: 铁电纳米电容器可作为可编程极性拓扑的简单平台，并通过耗散动力学实现低功耗、THz速度的拓扑控制。

Abstract: Topological polar structures in ferroelectric heterostructures offer functional states beyond ordinary domains. As polarization reversal underpins ferroelectric devices, switching between degenerate topological variants, which we refer to as topology reversal, would greatly expand the ways to control polar textures. However, polar topology reversal is challenging because it requires coordinated flipping of oppositely oriented local polarizations, a process not naturally driven by a uniform electric field. Using a multiscale modeling approach, we show that polar topology can be stabilized and reversed in a simple Pt/PbTiO$_3$/Pt nanocapacitor. Electrode-controlled boundary conditions drive tetragonal PbTiO$_3$ into competing orthorhombic- and rhombohedral-like polarization states, which form building blocks for diverse topological structures, including vortex arrays, skyrmion lattices, dipole spirals, and dipole waves. We further show that the same out-of-plane THz pulse can reversibly switch a double-$\mathbf q$ dipole wave between degenerate topological variants within a few picoseconds, while preserving its spatial modulation. This process is enabled by damping-assisted inertial switching, in which the THz pulse injects energy to drive large-angle polarization rotation across shallow barriers, while dissipation removes excess energy and traps the system in the reversed state. These results establish ferroelectric nanocapacitors as a simple platform for programmable polar topology and low-power, THz-speed control through dissipative dynamics.

</details>


### [114] [Characterisation of surface layers formed on plasma-facing components in controlled fusion devices: Role of heavy ion elastic recoil detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03160)
*Petter Str\"om,Per Petersson,Marek Rubel,Armin Weckmann,Sebastijan Brezinsek,Arkadi Kreter,S\"oren M\"oller,Krzysztof Rozniatowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用氮-15和MoF6示踪注入后回收的TEXTOR托卡马克壁部件，分析限器瓦上的沉积模式以推断材料迁移；Mo沉积显示侵蚀与即时再沉积，N-15与Mo共沉积，但表面粗糙度限制了深度分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定托卡马克壁材料在等离子体作用下的迁移与沉积规律，并评估示踪实验对理解侵蚀、再沉积和共沉积过程的有效性。

Method: 对TEXTOR示踪实验后取出的碳纤维复合材料与细晶石墨环形限器瓦，采用飞行时间重离子弹性反冲探测分析（ToF heavy ion ERDA）表征元素组成及近表面深度分布。

Result: Mo沉积模式表明材料迁移由侵蚀和即时再沉积主导；N-15与被沉积的Mo一起被捕获。最外层400 nm内可获得一定深度分布信息，但样品表面粗糙度严重限制分辨率。Mo在100 nm最外层浓度最高，F和N-15剖面较不规则；还检出硼化产生的B-10/B-11、早期示踪的C-13、边缘冷却用N-14以及Inconel壁侵蚀金属。

Conclusion: 示踪结合ERDA可揭示壁材料迁移、沉积及共沉积特征，但表面粗糙度是近表面深度分辨的主要限制因素。

Abstract: Wall components retrieved from the TEXTOR tokamak after tracer experiments with nitrogen-15 and molybdenum hexafluoride (MoF6) injection were studied to determine deposition patterns and, by this, to conclude on material migration. Toroidal limiter tiles made of carbon fibre composites and fine grain graphite were examined using time-of-flight heavy ion elastic recoil detection analysis. Molybdenum deposition patterns indicated migration based on erosion and prompt re-deposition. Nitrogen-15 was trapped together with the deposited molybdenum. Some information on the depth distribution of species in the top 400 nm layer of the limiters was obtained; however surface roughness of the samples strongly limited resolution. In the case of molybdenum, the largest concentration was found in the 100 nm outermost layer, whereas fluorine and nitrogen-15 displayed more irregular profiles. Other species, besides deuterium fuel and carbon-12, were also identified: boron-10 and boron-11 originating from boronisations, carbon-13 from earlier tracer experiments, nitrogen-14 from plasma edge cooling and metals eroded from the Inconel wall.

</details>


### [115] [A Topology-Based Approach to Representative Volume Element Assessment in Solid Oxide Cell Anodes Using Large Volume Tomography and Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03176)
*Aleksandra Kukie{\l}ka,Maksym Szemer,Szymon Buchaniec,Katarzyna Berent,Grzegorz Brus*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于持续同调（persistent homology）的拓扑框架，用于评估等离子聚焦离子束-扫描电镜重建的Ni-YSZ固体氧化物电池阳极的代表性体积单元（RVE）；通过比较子体积与最大参考体积的归一化持续图像L1距离，实现跨观测尺度的连续拓扑相似性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVE判定依赖若干几何描述符的收敛，可能给出不同的代表性尺度估计，难以保证用于建立统计意义上 microstructure-property 关系的代表性。

Method: 重建体积为121.8 μm × 83.97 μm × 45.99 μm；用持续同调表征孔隙网络，将各子体积的归一化持续图像与最大可用参考体积的持续图像用L1距离比较；用经验指数模型描述拓扑距离随体积变化，并与孔隙率和比表面积的结果对比；在三个独立合成实现上测试框架。

Result: 拓扑距离随体积增大而下降，并在研究范围内可由经验指数模型描述（R2在0.972–0.983）；与孔隙率和比表面积比较显示几何描述符与拓扑描述符的尺度依赖行为存在差异；三个合成实现验证了该框架用于多孔微结构拓扑相似性定量评估的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了不依赖单一收敛阈值的连续拓扑相似性度量，可作为传统几何描述符方法的补充或替代，用于多孔微结构代表性尺度的定量评估。

Abstract: Determining the representative volume element is essential for establishing statistically meaningful microstructure-property relationships. Conventional approaches rely on the convergence of selected geometric descriptors, which may yield different estimates of the representative volume. Here, we propose a topology-based framework for assessing the representative scale of a nickel-yttria-stabilized zirconia solid oxide cell anode reconstructed using plasma focused ion beam-scanning electron microscopy (121.8 um x 83.97 um x 45.99 um). Persistent homology was used to characterize the pore network, and normalized persistence images of subvolumes were compared with those of the largest available reference volume using the L1 distance. This provides a continuous measure of topological similarity across observation scales, rather than defining representativeness through a single convergence threshold. The topological distance decreases with increasing volume and is described by empirical exponential models over the investigated range (R2 in [0.972, 0.983]). Comparison with porosity and specific surface area highlights differences in the scale-dependent behavior of geometric and topological descriptors. The framework was also tested on three independent synthetic realizations, demonstrating its potential for quantitative assessment of topological similarity in porous microstructures.

</details>


### [116] [Pressure-correlation phase diagram unraveling quantum phase transitions in altermagnetic $\epsilon$-FeOOH and realization of piezo-anomalous Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03180)
*Arijit Mandal,Nayana Devaraj,Birabar Ranjit Kumar Nanda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文通过第一性原理构建压力-电子关联相图，揭示ε-FeOOH中由电子关联、晶体场和Hund耦合竞争驱动的三个可调量子相变，并预测可由压力调控的反常霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清ε-FeOOH中高压下自旋态、电子相、结构对称性和自旋量子化轴（SQA）如何随电子关联与压力演化，并探索其altermagnetism与反常霍尔输运的可调控性。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算建立压力-关联相图，分析高自旋-低自旋（HS-LS）交叉、电荷转移绝缘体-莫特绝缘体（CTI-MI）转变、Pnnm到P21nm结构相变、SQA变化以及反常霍尔电导（AHC）。

Result: 发现Pc1处HS-LS交叉伴随CTI-MI转变和SQA由ŷ变为x̂，源于晶体场劈裂增大与Hund耦合减弱；Pc2处Pnnm到P21nm结构相变与电子关联无关；强关联下低压区出现Pc3，使SQA由ẑ变为ŷ并验证实验。两相均具altermagnetism，且仅SQA沿x̂和ŷ时存在有限AHC。

Conclusion: ε-FeOOH可实现压力可调的多重量子相变和压电-反常霍尔效应：较低压力下开启AHC，较高压力下改变其方向，为关联材料中输运调控提供平台。

Abstract: We establish a pressure-correlation phase diagram that yields multiple pressure-tunable quantum phase transitions in iron oxyhydroxide ($\epsilon$-FeOOH) across three critical pressures, $P_{c1}>P_{c2}>P_{c3}$. The transitions arise from the competition among electron-correlation, crystal-field, and Hund's coupling. First-principles calculations reveal that the earlier reported high-spin (HS) to low-spin (LS) crossover at correlation-sensitive $P_{c1}$ is accompanied by a transition from charge-transfer to Mott insulating (CTI-MI) phase, and a change in the spin-quantization axis (SQA) from $\hat{y}$ to $\hat{x}$. The HS-LS crossover and CTI-MI transition is driven by the simultaneous increase in crystal-field splitting and decrease in Hund's coupling. The critical pressure $P_{c2}$ governing the structural transition from centrosymmetric $Pnnm$ phase to noncentrosymmetric $P2_1nm$ phase is found to be independent of electron-correlation. When correlation is strong, a third critical pressure ($P_{c3}$) emerges in the lower-pressure regime of the phase diagram. Validating the recent experimental observation, $P_{c3}$ is responsible for changing the SQA from $\hat{z}$ to $\hat{y}$. By exhibiting altermagnetism in both $P2_1nm$ and $Pnnm$ phases and possessing finite anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) only for SQA along $\hat{x}$ and $\hat{y}$, $\epsilon$-FeOOH enables a unique piezo-anomalous Hall effect where one can switch on the AHC at a lower pressure and change its direction at a higher pressure.

</details>


### [117] [Piezogyration and elastogyration in ternary chalcopyrites from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03365)
*Asier Zabalo,Fernando G\'omez-Ortiz,Aldo H. Romero,Eric Bousquet*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出第一性原理方法计算完整的压旋光与弹旋光张量，并应用于三元黄铜矿晶体，揭示弹旋光响应继承自母体闪锌矿结构、弹性柔度调控压旋光，以及通过旋光张量迹描述应变诱导手性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然光学活性源于光学响应的空间色散并由二阶旋光赝张量描述；机械应力与应变对应的压旋光和弹旋光效应虽经长期实验研究，但缺乏系统的第一性原理描述。

Method: 建立第一性原理计算框架，计算完整的压旋光张量和弹旋光张量，并应用于代表性三元黄铜矿晶体；分析未应变旋光、母体闪锌矿结构、弹性柔度张量及旋光张量迹对手性响应的作用。

Result: 未应变晶体的旋光性不能很好预测弹旋光响应大小；弹旋光响应可从母体闪锌矿结构继承；弹性柔度张量进一步塑造压旋光响应，在AgGaS2中尤为显著；旋光张量迹作为唯一线性于张量分量的赝标量贡献，可用于探索应变诱导手性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了系统研究压旋光和弹旋光效应的第一性原理方法，并表明应变可诱导手性，尤其对非手性但具光学活性的晶体具有重要意义。

Abstract: Natural optical activity arises from the spatial dispersion of the optical response and is described by the rank-two gyration pseudotensor. Its dependence on mechanical stress and strain, quantified by the piezogyration and elastogyration tensors, respectively, has been investigated experimentally for decades. However, a systematic first-principles description of these effects remains largely unexplored. Here we present a first-principles methodology to compute the full piezogyration and elastogyration tensors, and apply it to a set of representative ternary chalcopyrite crystals. The gyration of the unstrained crystal turns out to be a poor predictor of the magnitude of the elastogyration response, which we find to be a property inherited from their parent zinc-blende structures. The elastic compliance tensor further shapes the piezogyration response, with particularly pronounced effects in AgGaS$_2$, a well-known achiral yet optically active crystal. Finally, we explore strain-induced chirality through the trace of the gyration tensor, the only pseudoscalar contribution to the natural optical activity that is linear in the tensor components and encodes information about chirality.

</details>


### [118] [Amplitude-conserving frequency demultiplexer based on a spin wave delay line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03423)
*Ga\"el Yann Thiancourt,Thibaut Devolder*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文从理论上研究微米宽磁导体中自旋波波包的激发、传播与收集，并据此提出一种在空间和时间上实现模拟频率解复用的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传播型自旋波有望用于信号处理与信息传输，因此需要理解波包在磁导体中的时空演化，并探索其在模拟频率解复用中的应用。

Method: 通过理论分析高斯场脉冲激发的传播型自旋波波包，考察激发持续时间、自旋波色散关系以及自旋波线宽对波包时空演化的影响，并总结不同频率波包在时空上分离的规则。

Result: 阐明了激发时长、色散和线宽在决定波包时空演化中的作用，给出了携带不同频率的波包实现时空分离的条件，并提出一种基于60×5 μm²自旋波延迟线的保幅频率解复用器。

Conclusion: 研究表明微米宽磁导体中的传播型自旋波波包可用于模拟频率解复用，并为基于自旋波延迟线的保幅频率解复用器设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Propagating spin waves have emerged as promising candidates for signal processing and information transmission. We present a theoretical investigation of the generation, propagation and collection of spin-wave wave packets in micrometer-wide magnetic conduits, highlighting their potential for analogue frequency demultiplexing in space and time. We clarify the roles of the excitation duration and of the spin wave dispersion relation, as well as the influence of the spin-wave linewidth in setting the spatio-temporal evolution of propagating spin-wave wavepackets excited by Gaussian field pulses. We issue rules for the spatio-temporal separation of wavepackets that carry different frequencies. We finally propose an amplitude-conserving frequency demultiplexer based on a spin wave delay line of 60x5 $\mu$m$^2$ footprint.

</details>


### [119] [Local and itinerant orbital contributions to the gyromagnetic response of paramagnetic metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03426)
*Garima Ahuja,Mirco Sastges,Dongwook Go,Shobhana Narasimhan,Stefan Bl\"ugel,Yuriy Mokrousov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出计算顺磁金属有效旋磁张量g'的第一性原理框架，一致纳入局域与巡游轨道贡献、自旋轨道耦合以及态变化和占据变化效应；对V和Pt的计算表明轨道响应可显著修正g'并与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道电子学利用轨道角动量产生和操控角动量流以控制磁化，因此需要定量厘清轨道角动量如何贡献磁矩并决定支配磁化动力学的旋磁响应。

Method: 建立第一性原理框架计算顺磁金属的有效旋磁张量g'，一致包含局域和巡游轨道贡献、自旋轨道耦合、态变化效应与占据变化效应，并将其应用于V和Pt。

Result: V和Pt的轨道顺磁响应与自旋贡献相当或更大，并使g'显著偏离自由电子值；原子中心近似可将轨道响应低估约2倍，忽略占据变化会带来显著误差；计算所得g'与实验一致，且强烈依赖能带填充。

Conclusion: 现代轨道磁性理论是描述旋磁响应的必要组成部分；该框架可用于识别具有增强轨道功能的材料，并表明可通过电子结构调控角动量响应。

Abstract: Orbitronics exploits orbital angular momentum to generate and manipulate angular-momentum currents for controlling magnetisation, offering new approaches to magnetic memory and logic. A quantitative description of this process requires establishing how orbital angular momentum contributes to magnetic moments and, consequently, to the gyromagnetic response that governs magnetisation dynamics. Here, we present a first-principles framework for calculating the effective gyromagnetic tensor (g') of paramagnetic metals, consistently incorporating local and itinerant orbital contributions, spin-orbit coupling, and both state- and occupation-change effects. Applying the framework to V and Pt, we find that orbital paramagnetic responses are comparable to or larger than spin contributions and substantially modify g' from the free-electron value. The atom-centered approximation can underestimate the orbital response by approximately a factor of two, while neglecting occupation changes produces significant errors in the calculated g'. Our calculated g' values for V and Pt agree with experiment, while their pronounced dependence on band filling reveals a route to engineering angular-momentum responses through electronic-structure control. The results establish the modern theory of orbital magnetism as an essential ingredient for describing gyromagnetic response and provide a framework for identifying materials with enhanced orbital functionalities.

</details>


### [120] [Cage-enforced i-wave altermagnetism in boron-carbon clathrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03492)
*Meiyan Wang,Li Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在MB3C3硼碳笼合物中，客体-主体框架将交错磁自旋劈裂的旋转对称性与晶体结构解耦：有序笼子消除两个客体位间的平移，使任意补偿态磁性客体均获得立方i波交错磁性和六个对称性强制的节点面；应变可将其调控为d波或g波。GdB3C3被用作验证平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知交错磁的自旋劈裂由连接两套磁亚晶格的旋转决定，且通常被晶体结构固定，缺乏独立调控自由度；作者希望利用客体-主体框架分离这两个角色，获得可由磁客体和应变调控的交错磁平台。

Method: 提出MB3C3硼碳笼合物平台，利用有序笼子移除两个磁性客体位之间的平移对称性，以对称性分析确定立方i波交错磁性和节点面；通过应变改变对称性类别；以GdB3C3为实例评估补偿反铁磁基态、能带自旋劈裂、Néel矢量与螺旋度分辨光学响应，以及高压热力学稳定性和常压动力学稳定性。

Result: GdB3C3的补偿反铁磁基态为窄带隙半导体，在导带边具有0.19 eV的自旋劈裂；弱钉扎的Néel矢量可依据磁点群开启或关闭螺旋度分辨光学响应。该材料在高压下热力学稳定、常压下动力学稳定，表明可按该家族既有路线制备。

Conclusion: 客体-主体框架可把交错磁的旋转对称性与晶体结构解耦，实现对称性强制的i波交错磁并对应变调控为d/g波；GdB3C3展示了可切换光学响应的窄带隙交错磁半导体平台，并具备可合成性。

Abstract: The spin splitting of an altermagnet arises from a rotation that relates its two magnetic sublattices. In known cases it is fixed by the crystal structure. Here we show that guest-host frameworks separate the two roles. In the boron-carbon clathrates $M$B$_3$C$_3$ the ordered cage removes the translation between the two guest sites and enforces cubic $i$-wave altermagnetism, with six symmetry-enforced nodal planes, for any magnetic guest in the compensated state; strain retunes the class to $d$- or $g$-wave. We demonstrate the platform with GdB$_3$C$_3$: its compensated antiferromagnetic ground state is a narrow-gap semiconductor with spin splitting of 0.19 eV at the conduction-band edge, and its weakly pinned N\'eel vector switches the helicity-resolved optical response on and off according to the magnetic point group. Thermodynamic stability at high pressure and dynamical stability at ambient pressure indicate that it can be made by the route established for this family.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Dysfunctional Functionals and their Therapies in Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00377)
*Neepa T. Maitra*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文讨论TDDFT中交换关联泛函的记忆效应被绝热近似忽略所导致的问题，并指出超越绝热近似的改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确TDDFT的交换关联泛函依赖历史密度和初始态，但实际计算几乎总采用绝热近似，忽略记忆依赖；同时绝热TDDFT在响应性质和完全非平衡动力学中广泛成功，其原因尚未完全理解。

Method: 文章分析因忽略记忆依赖而产生的“dysfunction”，并讨论超越绝热近似的治疗性方案；同时基于精确理论的严格基础和有利的系统尺寸标度，论证改进泛函近似的必要性。

Result: 绝热TDDFT在响应性质与完全非平衡动力学等多类体系中取得成功，但这种成功的适用范围和原因仅被部分理解；忽略记忆效应会造成特定缺陷。

Conclusion: 需要继续发展更接近精确泛函的近似、超越绝热近似，并深入理解近似在应用中的影响；精确TDDFT的严格基础和良好标度行为为此提供强大动力。

Abstract: The exchange-correlation functional in Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory (TDDFT) is known to have dependence on past densities and initial states, and yet this memory-dependence is ignored in practical calculations which almost always use an adiabatic approximation. We discuss the dysfunction that arises due to this deficiency and point to therapies that go beyond the adiabatic approximation. Still, adiabatic TDDFT has had success in a wide range of systems for response properties and for fully non-equilibrium dynamics, and why this is so is only partially understood. This, together with the rigorous foundation of the exact theory and its favorable system-size scaling, provides tremendous impetus for continuing to strive towards improved approximations to the exact functionals, and a deeper understanding of the impact of approximations in applications.

</details>


### [122] [Graph-Covariant Bottom-Up Scale-Bridging Reconstruction of Ground and Excited States of Extended Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01349)
*Bartosz B{\l}asiak,Irene Burghardt*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种名为“封装”的分层尺度桥接构造，用自底向上的第一性原理方法构建含无序扩展分子聚集体电子结构模型，并在四联噻吩激子体系中数值演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展分子聚集体存在结构无序，直接做大规模第一性原理计算困难；需要一种无需对光谱数据拟合、也无需计算更大体系截断片段，就能从单体/二体构建聚集体级算符并连接激子-振动动力学模拟的方法。

Method: 采用递归算符值层级，将离散图论结构与连续构型空间结合：粗层变量以图协变、几何保持方式参数化下一细层算符。应用中以多体展开构造聚集体基态和激发态算符，基组由聚集体基态与局域Frenkel激发组成；单体和二体构件由DFT/TDDFT计算，得到聚集体级单粒子跃迁密度矩阵以及Hamiltonian、偶极矩等算符。

Result: 从实验或分子动力学结构模型出发，可自底向上生成聚集体级算符；在晶体四联噻吩的激子流形上进行了数值演示，表明封装中编码的构型依赖性可桥接静态电子结构计算与激子-振动量子或量子-经典动力学模拟。

Conclusion: 该封装框架为现实结构无序聚集体的第一性原理电子结构建模提供了可扩展的分层方案，并可作为连接静态电子结构与激子-振动动力学模拟的桥梁。

Abstract: We propose a hierarchical scale-bridging construction for building first principles electronic structure models of extended molecular aggregates with disorder, and its practical realization in exciton systems. The general construction, referred here as to the encapsulation, is based on a recursive operator-valued hierarchy cast in a dual graph-theoretic (discrete) and configuration-space (continuous) structure, in which variables associated with a coarser level parameterize operators governing the degrees of freedom at the next finer level in a graph-covariant, geometry-preserving fashion. In an exemplary application of this scheme, aggregate-level ground- and excited-state operators are constructed through a many-body expansion (MBE), with matrix elements evaluated in a basis comprising the aggregate ground state and local Frenkel excitations. The required monomer and dimer building blocks are obtained from DFT and TDDFT calculations, yielding aggregate-level 1-particle transition density matrices as well as Hamiltonian, dipole moment and other operators that can be evaluated at the level of the building blocks. Starting from structural models obtained from experiment or molecular dynamics, this approach generates aggregate-level operators in a bottom-up fashion without fitting parameters to spectroscopic data or performing calculations on larger system cutouts. A numerical demonstration is given for an excitonic manifold in crystalline quaterthiophene. In particular, through its intrinsic configuration-dependence encoded in the encapsulations, it will provide a bridge between static electronic structure calculations for realistic, structurally disordered aggregates and quantum or quantum-classical simulations of exciton-vibrational dynamics.

</details>


### [123] [VESPER: Online Learning of Free Energies with a Multi-Tempered Variational Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00401)
*Jay L. Kaplan,Gustavo R. P\'erez-Lemus,Mengjian Hua,Juan J. de Pablo,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: VESPER 将分子动力学采样与神经网络自由能学习耦合起来，通过单一凸变分目标和多层级 tempering 加速集体变量空间探索，并在多个分子体系上实现自洽的自由能重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算集体变量上的分子自由能景观对理解机制至关重要，但先验训练数据不可得；MD 采样易被困于亚稳态，难以跨越决定构象变化或化学反应的自由能垒。

Method: VESPER 让 MD 提供构型用于学习，同时用演化中的神经网络自由能模型偏置 MD 以加速探索并生成新数据；它最小化一个对自由能凸的变分目标，可结合多个 flattening 自由能景观的 tempering 层级，且各层级共享同一物理自由能，从而无需重加权即可训练共享模型。学习信号来自偏置 MD 构型与在 CV 空间探索平坦模型的辅助 walker 之间的平均参数梯度差；作者在 log-Sobolev 和 Polyak-Lojasiewicz 假设下证明了无 replica exchange 的耦合过阻尼种群动力学的条件收敛性。

Result: 在 alanine dipeptide 上，VESPER 匹配或优于已有增强采样方法；在 alanine tetrapeptide 上，从 3 到 6 个 CV 得到自洽的自由能重构；在 Ni(511) 上甲烷脱氢反应中，结合机器学习原子间势恢复了二维 CV Boltzmann 边缘分布以及沿三维 CV 路径的反应物到鞍点自由能差，并与低维参考定性一致；在 chignolin 上同时偏置 17 个 CV 仍能采样 RMSD 定义的折叠与未折叠区域。

Conclusion: VESPER 提供了一种无需先验训练数据、可扩展至高维 CV 的耦合采样与自由能学习方法，并能与机器学习势结合用于复杂分子和表面反应体系的自洽自由能景观重建。

Abstract: Computing molecular free-energy landscapes over collective variables (CVs) is central to understanding molecular mechanisms, but poses a difficult learning problem without training data available a priori. Samples of the marginal CV distribution must be generated by molecular dynamics (MD), whose trajectories can remain trapped in metastable states and rarely cross the free-energy barriers governing conformational changes or chemical reactions. We introduce VESPER, which couples the generation of these samples to neural-network-driven free-energy learning: MD supplies configurations for learning, while the evolving model biases the MD to accelerate exploration and generate further training data. VESPER minimizes a single variational objective that is convex in the free energy and can combine several tempering levels, each flattening the free-energy landscape to promote barrier crossing. Every level has the same physical free energy as its minimizer, allowing data from all levels to train one shared model without reweighting. Learning uses the difference in mean parameter gradients between biased MD configurations and auxiliary walkers that explore the flattened model in CV space. We prove conditional convergence of the coupled overdamped population dynamics without replica exchange under log-Sobolev and Polyak-Lojasiewicz assumptions. VESPER matches or improves on established enhanced-sampling methods on alanine dipeptide and yields self-consistent free-energy reconstructions from three to six CVs on alanine tetrapeptide. For methane dehydrogenation on Ni(511) with a machine-learned interatomic potential, it recovers two-CV Boltzmann marginals and a reactant-to-saddle free-energy difference along a three-CV path, both qualitatively consistent with the lower-dimensional reference. It also samples RMSD-defined folded and unfolded regions of chignolin while biasing seventeen CVs.

</details>


### [124] [Molecular conical intersections with odd electron number are realizations of the topological Yang monopole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00429)
*Chenchen Song*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了无磁场下奇电子数分子的 T^2=-1 锥形交叉与 Yang 磁单极子之间的数学联系，并给出基于几何代数与四元数的推导及 SU(2) Berry 连接的 Hopf 纤维化可视化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T^2=-1 锥形交叉因 Kramers 简并具有五维分支空间，类似磁场中 T^2=0 锥形交叉对应 Dirac 磁单极子，它对应具有 SU(2) 规范场和 SO(5) 对称性的 Yang 磁单极子；因此可借助高能物理中 Yang 磁单极子的已知结果研究其化学中的拓扑性质。

Method: 利用几何代数与四元数自然处理 T^2=-1 时间反演对称性和缩放坐标下的 SO(5) 对称性，简化本征函数、Berry 连接与 Berry 曲率的推导；并证明从上/下能态看，该锥形交叉分别表现为自对偶/反自对偶 Yang 磁单极子；同时通过 S^3 经度空间上的 Hopf 纤维化及球极投影可视化 SU(2) Berry 连接。

Result: 得到 T^2=-1 锥形交叉的上、下态分别对应第二 Chern 数 C2=+1/4 与 C2=-1/4 的自对偶/反自对偶 Yang 磁单极子；Berry 连接的非零部分诱导 S^3 上的 Hopf 纤维化，并可经球极投影可视化。

Conclusion: 该工作为 T^2=-1 锥形交叉的拓扑性质研究提供了简洁的数学工具和可视化方案，并加强了化学中的锥形交叉与高能物理中 Yang 磁单极子之间的概念与理论联系。

Abstract: The two-level conical intersection of a molecule with odd electron number in the absence of magnetic field obeys time-reversal symmetry $T^{2}=-1$ (referred to as the $T^{2}=-1$ conical intersection), and has a five-dimensional branching space due to the Kramer degeneracy. Similar to how the conical intersection of a molecule in a magnetic field ($T^{2}=0$) behaves as the Dirac monopole, the $T^{2}=-1$ conical intersection behaves as the Yang monopole, an mathematical generalization of Dirac monopole with SU(2) gauge field and SO(5) symmetry. This implies that we can study topological properties of $T^{2}=-1$ conical intersections in chemistry based on what is known about Yang monopole in high energy physics. In this work, we present a few mathematical tools to study this connection. First, we show that geometric algebra and quaternion numbers together provides a natural way to utilize the $T^{2}=-1$ time reversal symmetry and the SO(5) symmetry in scaled coordinates, making it simple to derive eigenfunctions, Berry connection and Berry curvature of $T^{2}=-1$ conical intersection. In particular, this approach provides a simple proof that when viewed from the upper or lower states, the $T^{2}=-1$ conical intersection behaves as the self-dual or self-antidual Yang monopole with second Chern number $C_{2}=+\frac{1}{4}$ or $-\frac{1}{4}$ respectively. In addition, we propose a visualization method for the SU(2) Berry connection of $T^{2}=-1$ conical intersection. This is achieved by showing that the nonzero part of the Berry connection induces Hopf-fibration on the $S^{3}$ longitude space, and is further visualized through stereographic projection.

</details>


### [125] [Simple algorithm for evaluating SU(2) non-abelian geometric phase in the vicinity of molecular conical intersection of odd electron number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00434)
*Chenchen Song*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算奇电子数分子锥形交叉中SU(2)非阿贝尔几何相位的简单算法，并揭示其在5维空间反演/非正常等距变换下的态交换性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇电子数锥形交叉会产生非阿贝尔几何相位，但已有方法缺乏对5维分支空间中离散轨迹进行简便SU(2)几何相位计算的方案。

Method: 利用该类锥形交叉在3维子空间中类似阿贝尔锥形交叉的性质，先构造由球面三角形组成且具有相同几何相位的增广轨迹，再用Stokes定理计算每个球面三角形的贡献，并通过路径有序乘积得到总几何相位。

Result: 证明了增广轨迹与球面三角形贡献计算的有效性；表明轨迹反演时上下态的几何相位发生交换，并将该关系推广为：在任意5维非正常等距变换下，Wilson环（几何相位的迹）在两个态之间交换；还给出5维多面体上轨迹的示例计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为5维分支空间中奇电子数锥形交叉的SU(2)几何相位提供了可操作算法，并建立了反演及非正常等距变换下几何相位与Wilson环的对称性关系。

Abstract: Molecular conical intersection of odd-electron number are known to give arise to non-abelian geometric phases. In this work, we present a simple algorithm for evaluating the SU(2) geometric phases given a trajectory represented as a discrete set of coordinates in the 5-dimensional branching space. Motivated by the special property that such conical intersection behaves as abelian ones in 3-dimensional subspace, we take two steps to establish the algorithm. We first prove that it is possible to construct an augmented trajectory consisting of spherical triangles that has the same geometric phases as the original trajectory. We then prove that the geometric phase contribution from each spherical triangle can be evaluated from its properties using Stokes' theorem in a way that closely resembles abelian conical intersections. The total geometric phases are thus evaluated by path-ordered products of contributions from each spherical triangle. Based on the augmented trajectory, we further show that the geometric phases of the upper and lower states will switch under inversion transformation of the trajectory. This relation is further generalized to show that the Wilson loop, defined as the trace of the geometric phases, will switch between the two states under any 5-dimensional improper isometric transformation. Example calculations of geometric phases for trajectories generated on 5-dimensional polytopes are also presented and discussed.

</details>


### [126] [PLUMED-JAX: GPU-native enhanced sampling for atomistic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00483)
*Venkat Kapil*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: PLUMED-JAX 将部分 PLUMED 集合变量、伞形采样和元动力学适配到 JAX，并与 JAX-MD 集成，实现编译执行、批量副本、共享偏置的多 walker 和多 GPU 运行，在经典力场与机器学习势上取得数倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLUMED 的增强采样方法缺乏对 JAX 原生编译、批处理和 GPU 并行的高效支持，需要将其核心功能引入 JAX 原子模拟生态以提升性能与可扩展性。

Method: 借助 gpt-6.1-sol（Codex harness 中高推理强度）进行 AI 辅助适配，选取 PLUMED 集合变量、伞形采样和元动力学实现为 PLUMED-JAX，并与 JAX-MD 集成，支持编译分子动力学、批量副本、共享偏置的 walkers 及多 GPU 执行。

Result: 框架经 PLUMED 与精确自由能基准验证，并应用于丁烷、硅结晶和锂输运；在经典力场和机器学习原子间势上均实现数倍计算吞吐提升。

Conclusion: PLUMED-JAX 表明 AI 辅助的 JAX 化增强采样可与 JAX-MD 高效结合，在保持基准一致性的同时显著提升 GPU 并行性能和实际模拟吞吐。

Abstract: We present PLUMED-JAX, an AI-assisted adaptation of selected PLUMED collective variables, umbrella sampling, and metadynamics for JAX-based atomistic simulations, developed using gpt-6.1-sol with reasoning effort set to high within the Codex harness. Integration with JAX-MD supports compiled molecular dynamics, batched replicas, walkers sharing a bias, and execution across multiple graphics processing units. We validate the framework against PLUMED and exact free energy benchmarks, and demonstrate applications to butane, silicon crystallization, and lithium transport, with severalfold improvements in computational throughput for both classical forcefields and machine learning interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [127] [Energy Transfer and Strong Coupling in Plasmonic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01410)
*Janak Bhandari,Ajinkya Shingote,Guangming Liu,Bikram Ghosh,Hsing-Ta Chen,Gregory V. Hartland*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该 Perspective 文章用经典电磁模拟与基于宏观量子电动力学的量子模型，系统分析金属纳米结构等离激元与分子/半导体电子跃迁耦合时，局域表面等离激元共振谱及传播表面等离极化激元的色散与衰减如何随耦合强度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离激元共振与分子/半导体电子激发的耦合对表面增强光谱、热电子催化、等离激元诱导共振能量转移及杂化光-物质态等应用十分重要；已有大量实验和理论工作，但仍需统一理解从能量转移极限到强耦合的演化及衰减过程。

Method: 采用经典电磁模拟研究金属纳米颗粒的局域表面等离激元共振以及扩展金属纳米结构（线/板/膜）中传播表面等离极化激元的响应；并用基于宏观量子电动力学框架、包含Holstein振动的量子模型描述等离激元-激子耦合，与经典模拟互补。

Result: 通过模拟展示：随着分子/半导体电子跃迁强度增加，LSPR谱以及SPP色散曲线/衰减常数如何改变，系统如何从能量转移极限演化至强耦合；并从经典电磁与MQED两种视角给出对衰减过程的理解。

Conclusion: 经典电磁模拟与MQED量子模型相结合，可为等离激元-分子/半导体耦合提供互补且统一的描述，连接能量转移与强耦合区间，并加深对谱学响应、色散、衰减及衰变机制的理解。

Abstract: The coupling between plasmon resonances of metal nanostructures and electronic excitations of molecular/semiconductor systems is important in a number of applications, including surface enhanced spectroscopy, hot-electron catalysis, plasmon-induced resonance energy transfer and the creation of hybrid light-matter states. The past several decades has seen a large number of experimental studies of these effects, as well as the development of theory to explain the results. In this Perspectives article we use classical electromagnetic (EM) simulations to examine how the localized surface plasmon resonances (LSPRs) of metal nanoparticles and propagating surface plasmon polaritons (SPPs) in extended metal nanostructures (wires/plates/films) are affected by interactions with a molecular/semiconductor system. The classical EM simulations are complemented by a quantum model for plasmon-exciton coupling based on the macroscopic quantum electrodynamics (MQED) framework, along with Holstein vibrations. The goals are to show how LSPR spectra and SPP dispersion curves/attenuation constants changes as the strength of the molecular/semiconductor electronic transition increases and the system evolves from an energy transfer limit to strong coupling, and to provide an understanding of the decay processes from both classical EM and MQED perspectives.

</details>


### [128] [An Electrolyte Equation of State Based on openCOSMO-RS: From Aqueous Solutions to Non-Aqueous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01686)
*Leander Rausdzus,Simon M\"uller*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出首个基于 openCOSMO-RS 的开源预测型电解质状态方程 openCOSMO-RS-ES-Phi，可同时描述电解质溶液活度系数与密度/压力效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COSMO-RS-ES 等预测型电解质模型虽基于量子化学信息，但将液体视为不可压缩，无法给出密度或压力效应。

Method: 将 openCOSMO-RS-Phi 状态方程与 COSMO-RS-ES 的离子相互作用能及长程项结合，并先在简化短程项上重新拟合；采用介电减量改善平均离子活度系数（MIAC），排除会重复计算离子溶剂化的 Born 项；再加入拟合自水溶液 MIAC 和密度的离子体积、色散及温度参数。

Result: 该模型在稀水溶液中复现 COSMO-RS-ES，在浓溶液中优于后者，并能给出溶液密度；由水体系外推可像拟合后的 COSMO-RS-ES 一样准确预测水+助溶剂体系的 MIAC，以及水-醇-盐基准数据库的 MIAC 和泡点；但对有机物盐析描述不够准确，盐在有机溶剂中溶解度偏高。

Conclusion: COSMO-RS-ES 的相互作用模型在很大程度上可迁移至状态方程，openCOSMO-RS-ES-Phi 可作为进一步开发的可靠基础模型，离子在溶剂间的转移是后续非水数据拟合的明确目标。

Abstract: Predictive COSMO-RS-based electrolyte models such as COSMO-RS-ES describe electrolyte solutions from quantum-chemical information, but treat the liquid as incompressible and give no access to densities or pressure effects. This work presents openCOSMO-RS-ES-Phi, to our knowledge the first open-source implementation of a predictive electrolyte equation of state based on openCOSMO-RS. It combines the COSMO-RS-based equation of state openCOSMO-RS-Phi with the ion interaction energies and the long-range term of COSMO-RS-ES, which is first refitted on the same simplified short range. With this refit, a dielectric decrement improves the mean ionic activity coefficients (MIAC), whereas a Born term counts the solvation of the ions a second time and is therefore left out. The equation of state then adds only volume, dispersion and temperature parameters of the ions, fitted to aqueous MIAC and densities. openCOSMO-RS-ES-Phi reproduces COSMO-RS-ES in dilute aqueous solutions, improves on it in concentrated ones and also yields solution densities. Extrapolating from aqueous systems, it predicts the MIAC in water + cosolvent mixtures as accurately as the fitted COSMO-RS-ES, as well as the MIAC and bubble points of a benchmark database of water-alcohol-salt systems. Salting-out of organic components is not described as accurately, and salts come out too soluble in organic solvents. Overall, the interaction model developed for COSMO-RS-ES carries over to a large extent to an equation of state, which makes openCOSMO-RS-ES-Phi a sound base model for further development, with ion transfer between solvents as a clear target for fitting to non-aqueous data.

</details>


### [129] [Optimal Control of Chemical Reaction Networks with Thermodynamic Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01734)
*Tetsuya J. Kobayashi,Alix Robin,Shuhei A. Horiguchi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了适用于马尔可夫跳跃过程与化学反应网络的热力学最优控制理论，突破慢驱动与状态间输运限制，可同时优化状态和反应通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热力学控制框架多局限于慢驱动或状态到状态输运，难以在远离平衡、有限时间条件下对通量和状态目标进行统一优化。

Method: 推导热力学Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，证明最优动力学为受价值函数驱动的扰动广义梯度流；用广义均值给出熵产生率的凸表示，并将其Legendre-Fenchel对偶识别为耗散函数；进一步以热力学力重述控制问题，推导热力学Pontryagin方程。

Result: 最优动力学具有化学反应网络的热力学结构并可作信息几何刻画；热力学Pontryagin方程提供最优轨迹的互补描述，并厘清与热力学最优输运的联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为超越单纯输运的MJP与CRN控制提供热力学与几何基础，为生物过程优化及远离平衡区有限时间热力学操作开辟途径。

Abstract: Thermodynamic control seeks to realize desired functions at minimal thermodynamic cost, yet existing frameworks remain largely confined to slow driving regimes or state-to-state transport. Here we establish a thermodynamic optimal-control theory for Markov jump processes (MJPs) and chemical reaction networks (CRNs) that accommodates objectives over both states and reaction fluxes beyond slow-driving limit. By deriving the thermodynamic Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equation, we demonstrate that the optimal dynamics take the form of a perturbed generalized-gradient flow driven by the value function. A convex representation of the entropy-production rate via generalized means identifies its Legendre--Fenchel dual as the dissipation function. The resulting dynamics shares the thermodynamic structure of CRNs and admits an information-geometric characterization. We further derive a thermodynamic Pontryagin equation by recasting the control problem in terms of thermodynamic forces, providing a complementary description of optimal trajectories and clarifying connections to thermodynamic optimal transport. Our results establish a thermodynamic and geometric foundation for controlling MJPs and CRNs beyond mere transport, opening avenues for optimizing biological processes and finite-time thermodynamic operations in far-from-equilibrium regimes.

</details>


### [130] [Modeling and Experimental Validation of Bubble-Induced Performance Losses in Microstructured Copper Electrodes for Alkaline Water Electrolysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02226)
*Auronno Ovid Hussain,Adith Saha Dipro,Muhammad Anisuzzaman Talukder*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出耦合两相CFD与电化学动力学的多物理场理论模型，用于量化气泡对微结构电极电化学活性面积和过电位的影响，并指导高效水电解制氢电极设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水电解制氢中气泡生成会显著降低微结构电极的电化学活性面积和电催化活性，而现有理论模型未能有效耦合CFD与电化学以分析气泡动力学对产气微结构电极性能的影响。

Method: 建立多物理场理论模型，将两相CFD与Butler-Volmer及Faradaic动力学耦合，纳入交换电流密度、电池电流、重力加速度、表面张力以及流体密度和黏度等参数；量化A_EC增强和过电位η，并结合极化曲线、循环伏安、电化学阻抗谱和线性极化电阻实验验证微结构电极性能。

Result: 模型预测的A_EC增强和过电位与实验偏差分别小于7%和15%；所制备微结构电极使析氧和析氢过电位分别降低2.49–21.39%和9.55–24.32%；模型揭示了增强几何表面积与气泡传输限制之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了基于物理机理的优化路线，可用于设计最优、稳健且适合工业放大的微结构电极，以提升水电解制氢效率。

Abstract: Water electrolysis is a promising technology for sustainable hydrogen (H$_2$) production. However, gas bubble formation significantly degrades the electrochemical surface area ($A_{\rm EC}$) and the electrocatalytic activity of microstructured electrodes in water electrolyzer cells. Existing theoretical models fail to connect computational fluid dynamics (CFD) and electrochemistry to analyze how bubble dynamics affect the performance of gas-evolving microstructured electrodes. This work develops a multiphysics-based theoretical model that couples two-phase CFD with Butler-Volmer and Faradaic kinetics, incorporating exchange current density, cell current, gravitational acceleration, surface tension, and fluid properties such as density and viscosity. The model quantifies essential electrochemical parameters, such as the enhancement in $A_{\rm EC}$ and the overpotential ($\eta$), which deviate by less than 7\% and 15\%, respectively, from the experimental results. Evaluations of the fabricated microstructured electrodes using polarization curves, cyclic voltammetry, electrochemical impedance spectroscopy, and linear polarization resistance techniques demonstrate reduced $\eta$ by 2.49--21.39\% and 9.55--24.32\% for oxygen (O$_2$) and H$_2$ evolution, respectively. Furthermore, the model reveals the interplay between enhanced geometric surface area and bubble-transport limitations, facilitating the optimization and analysis of structural parameters. These findings establish a physics-informed roadmap for designing optimal, robust, and industry-scalable microstructured electrodes for efficient H$_2$ generation via water electrolysis.

</details>


### [131] [GPU infrastructure for ML/MM with electrostatic embedding for enzyme chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02315)
*Venkat Kapil*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个全GPU原生的ML/MM增强采样框架，采用静电嵌入并在卤代烷脱卤酶亲核取代反应上演示；用约200 H100 GPU小时计算自由能剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ML/MM增强采样与静电嵌入常受CPU-GPU数据搬运、框架割裂和计算成本限制，需要端到端GPU编译化流程以高效研究酶反应。

Method: 构建JAX版预训练MACE-POLAR-1-M模型，设计与MACE-POLAR兼容的静电嵌入方案，并集成JAX-MD与PLUMED-JAX实现全流程编译的增强采样；使用Codex中的GPT-6.1-Sol高推理强度辅助开发；采用副本交换伞形采样，蛋白和反应物用MACE-POLAR-1-M-JAX处理，大部分溶剂用分子力学处理。

Result: 在约200 H100 GPU小时内获得卤代烷脱卤酶中亲核取代反应的自由能剖面。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了全GPU原生、静电嵌入的ML/MM增强采样框架可高效用于酶反应自由能计算，并展示了AI辅助开发与JAX生态集成的可行性。

Abstract: We present a fully GPU-native framework for machine learning/molecular mechanics (ML/MM) enhanced sampling simulations with electrostatic embedding, demonstrated on an enzymatic reaction. The framework combines a JAX port of the pretrained MACE-POLAR-1-M model, an electrostatic embedding scheme compatible with the MACE-POLAR architecture, and integration with JAX-MD and PLUMED-JAX for end-to-end compiled enhanced sampling. The implementation is developed using GPT-6.1-Sol within Codex, with reasoning effort set to high. Using replica exchange umbrella sampling, we calculate the free energy profile of nucleophilic substitution in haloalkane dehalogenase in approximately 200 H100 GPU hours, treating the entire protein and reactants with MACE-POLAR-1-M-JAX and most of the solvent with molecular mechanics.

</details>


### [132] [Relativistic Kohn--Sham Theory of Paramagnetic Magnetic-Resonance Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03170)
*Christof Holzer,Yannick J. Franzke*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了基于密度泛函理论的相对论统一框架，用于计算顺磁NMR位移、EPR g张量、超精细耦合张量和磁化率，并用于评估相对论开壳层体系（含锕系配合物）中DFT的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺磁与重元素开壳层体系的NMR/EPR谱学需要同时处理自旋轨道耦合、磁场规范依赖和复杂响应收敛问题，因此需要一个统一、可解析实现的相对论DFT理论框架。

Method: 采用自旋轨道精确二分量X2C理论、规范包含原子轨道、广义复数Kohn-Sham响应方程，并用Goldstone模式和方向约束屏蔽解析实现；提出二分量增广Roothaan-Hall求解器以保证稳健收敛并同时找到驻点和极小点；还将常用布居分析推广到二分量理论以辅助谱学解释。

Result: 实现了对pNMR位移、EPR g张量、超精细耦合张量和磁化率的相对论DFT计算，并据此分析DFT在相对论开壳层体系（包括锕系配合物）中成功与失效的情形。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可用于相对论开壳层体系谱学解释的统一DFT工具，并揭示了DFT在处理此类顺磁重元素体系时的适用范围和局限。

Abstract: A general density functional theory based relativistic theory for paramagnetic NMR (pNMR) shifts, EPR g-tensors and hyperfine coupling tensors as well as magnetizabilities is derived. The formulation uses spin--orbit exact two-component (X2C) theory, gauge-including atomic orbitals, and generalized complex Kohn--Sham response equations with all ingredients being implemented analytically with the help of Goldstone modes and an orientation-constrained shielding. Additionally, we describe a two-component augmented Roothaan--Hall (ARH) solver for robust and stable convergence, which ensures that both a stationary point and a minimum are found. This is crucial for the convergence of the response equations for properties of paramagnetic systems. The extension of common population analyses for the two-component theory to aid in the interpretation of the spectra is further described. Using these new techniques, we are able to analyze the success and failure of density functional theory for relativistic open-shell systems including actinide complexes.

</details>


### [133] [FACTO: fast atom-centered tensor operator for accurate and efficient foundational machine-learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03276)
*Siyuan Liu,Chang Han,Sheng Gong,Yanping Yang,Tianze Zheng,Yu Xia,Kuang Yu,Shenzhen Xu,Wen Yan*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: FACTO 是一种面向高通量推理的原子中心等变机器学习原子间势架构，通过将演化状态与稠密通道混合限制在原子内、把邻域交互表达为可完全融合的归约，并结合定制聚合核与 Tensor Core FP32 模拟，在保持 FP32 级能量守恒的同时显著提升推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变机器学习原子间势的推理吞吐仍是核心瓶颈，需要同时兼顾预测精度、物理可靠性与高通量计算效率。

Method: 提出原子中心等变架构 FACTO：将学习状态和稠密通道混合限制在原子内部，将邻居交互转化为可完全融合的归约操作；配套寄存器累加式聚合内核与 Tensor Core FP32 模拟，以加速推理并在模拟算术下保持 FP32 级能量守恒。

Result: 在 Matbench Discovery 上，四个 FACTO-OAM 变体在 46 个完整评分模型中总体排名居前，推进精度-吞吐 Pareto 前沿；在 MLIP Arena 上于极端加热与压缩下取得最先进稳定性，并在氢燃烧中保持高保真；在 MatCalc 上结构和弹性预测优于对比基线，能量与热性质精度具竞争力。

Conclusion: FACTO 通过架构与内核协同设计，在高吞吐推理、预测精度和物理可靠性之间取得强平衡，可作为高性能等变原子间势的候选方案。

Abstract: High-throughput inference remains a central challenge for equivariant machine-learned interatomic potentials. Here we introduce FACTO, an atom-centered equivariant architecture that confines evolving learned state and dense channel mixing to atoms, expressing neighbor interactions as fully fusible reductions. We complement this design with custom register-accumulated aggregation kernels and tensor-core FP32 emulation, accelerating inference while retaining FP32-level energy conservation under emulated arithmetic in molecular dynamics. Comprehensive evaluations on Matbench Discovery, MLIP Arena and MatCalc demonstrate high throughput alongside strong predictive accuracy and physical reliability. On Matbench Discovery, the four FACTO-OAM variants show top-tier performance by overall rank sum among 46 models with complete scores, advancing the accuracy-throughput Pareto frontier. On MLIP Arena, FACTO achieves state- of-the-art stability under extreme heating and compression, alongside high fidelity in hydrogen combustion. On MatCalc, FACTO outperforms the compared baselines in structural and elastic predictions while retaining competitive accuracy for energetic and thermal properties.

</details>


### [134] [Shadow Molecular Dynamics for General Nonlinear Flexible-Charge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03499)
*Jos Thomas,Anthony P. Baldo,Rae A. Corrigan Grove,Mehmet Cagri Kaymak,Joshua Finkelstein,James Goff,Maksim Kulichenko,Sergei Tretiak,Nicholas Lubbers,Vishikh Athavale,Christian F. A. Negre,Michael Wall,Anders M. N. Niklasson*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种用于柔性电荷模型的 shadow molecular dynamics 框架：用部分线性化电子能泛函精确平衡得到的 shadow potential 替代精确 Born-Oppenheimer 势，将全局非线性电荷平衡转化为低成本准对角线性求解，并通过扩展拉格朗日动力学传播辅助电子自由度，实现含长程静电与环境依赖电子响应的稳定高效模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势扩展了分子动力学模拟范围，但复杂化学环境常需显式处理长程电子响应，包括电荷转移与极化。基于物理的柔性电荷模型能描述这些效应，却需在每个时间步进行全局非线性电荷平衡以得到 Born-Oppenheimer 能量和力，计算昂贵且若收敛不紧易导致不稳定。

Method: 构建任意局部非线性和长程多极静电下的 shadow molecular dynamics 框架。以部分线性化电子能泛函的精确平衡构造邻近 shadow potential，替代精确 Born-Oppenheimer 势，从而提供保守力，并将全局非线性平衡降为可直接低代价求解的准对角线性问题。引入辅助动力学电子自由度，通过扩展拉格朗日动力学传播，以捕捉长程电子响应并为能泛函线性化定义参考态。

Result: GPU 加速模拟超过 100,000 个原子表明，shadow potential 紧密跟随严格收敛的 Born-Oppenheimer 势面，同时保持长期稳定性，计算成本接近固定电荷模型。

Conclusion: 这些进展为显式包含长程静电相互作用和环境依赖电子响应的机器学习原子间势建立了一个高效且广泛适用的框架。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials have greatly expanded the reach of molecular dynamics simulations, but complex chemical environments often require explicit treatment of long-range electronic response, including charge transfer and polarization. Physics-based flexible-charge models capture these effects but require global nonlinear charge equilibration at every time step to obtain Born-Oppenheimer energies and forces. This process is computationally expensive and can lead to instabilities unless tightly converged. Here, we introduce a shadow molecular dynamics framework for flexible-charge models with arbitrary local nonlinearities and long-range multipolar electrostatics. We replace the exact Born-Oppenheimer potential with a nearby shadow potential obtained by exactly equilibrating a partially linearized electronic energy functional. This provides conservative forces and reduces the global nonlinear equilibration to a quasi-diagonal linear problem that can be solved directly at low cost. Auxiliary dynamical electronic degrees of freedom, propagated through an extended-Lagrangian dynamics, capture the long-range electronic response and define the reference state for the linearization of the energy functional. GPU-accelerated simulations with over 100,000 atoms demonstrate that the shadow potential closely follows the tightly converged Born-Oppenheimer surface, while maintaining long-term stability at a computational cost approaching that of a fixed-charge model. These advances establish an efficient and broadly applicable framework for machine-learned interatomic potentials that explicitly incorporate long-range electrostatic interactions and environment-dependent electronic response.

</details>
