### [135] [Synchronization and Spatiotemporal Phases in Driven Precessing Domain Walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03110)
*Luz P\'erez Herrera,Enzo Fabrizio Pusiol,Alejandro B. Kolton*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文用集体坐标 u-φ 模型研究恒定与交变磁场驱动下扩展磁畴壁动力学，揭示交变分量可通过 Shapiro 型锁频产生同步相，并给出由空间序与时间序组合而成的五类动力学相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒定场驱动下扩展磁畴壁会出现确定性时空混沌与粗糙化；作者希望理解叠加交变场后畴壁进动频率能否被锁定，以及同步、空间有序与混沌之间的相界和动力学机制。

Method: 采用集体坐标 u-φ 模型描述扩展磁畴壁；结合线性稳定性分析与 Floquet 理论确定稳定同步平直畴壁态的相边界；再通过扩展系统的非线性动力学数值研究识别动力学相区。

Result: 纯恒定场驱动表现为确定性时空混沌和粗糙化；交变分量可产生 Shapiro 型台阶并锁定进动频率。相图包含五种动力学区，由畴壁空间序（平直、粗糙、周期）与进动时间序（钉扎、同步、非同步）组合表征。同步相具有相干进动，可对应平直畴壁或由窄带线性不稳定模式主导的周期结构；非同步相可为相干进动的平直态，也可为具有时空混沌和非相干进动的粗糙态。

Conclusion: 交变驱动可诱导磁畴壁进动同步并显著重塑其非线性动力学相图；宏观时间序与畴壁空间形貌之间存在强耦合，同步并不必然对应平直畴壁，非同步也可存在平直且相干进动的状态。

Abstract: The dynamics of extended magnetic domain walls driven by constant and alternating magnetic fields are studied using a collective coordinate $u$-$\phi$ model. While purely constant-field driven systems exhibit deterministic spatiotemporal chaos and roughening, the alternating component can lock the precessional frequency via Shapiro-like steps. Using linear stability analysis and Floquet theory, the phase boundaries of stable, synchronized flat wall states are mapped. By studying the nonlinear dynamics of the extended system, a rich phase diagram is identified, composed of five dynamical regimes characterized by the combination of domain wall spatial order (flat, rough, or periodic) and domain wall precession temporal order (pinned, synchronized, or unsynchronized). Notably, the macroscopic temporal order of the synchronized phases can lead to a flat domain wall or to a periodic structure dominated by a narrow band of linearly unstable modes, both with coherent precession of the domain wall magnetization, while unsynchronized phases can be flat, with coherent precession, or rough with spatiotemporal chaos and incoherent precession.

</details>


### [136] [Fabry-P\'erot Cavity formed by Magnonic waveguide using holes and notches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00178)
*Rajesh Kumar,Yat-Yin Au,Snorri Ingvarsson*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于自旋波的Fabry-Pérot（FP）腔，通过在磁振子波导中嵌入两个空气孔和凹口构成，并利用微磁模拟分析其传输、反射及共振调谐特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP型磁振子波导中的反射谱分析对磁振子集成电路性能至关重要，但仍有必要深入研究，以支撑低功耗计算与数据处理等下一代自旋电子技术。

Method: 在磁振子波导中嵌入两个空气孔和凹口构建FP腔，通过微磁模拟改变孔与凹口中心间距，获得自旋波分布及传输/反射谱；提出理论模型描述传输与反射特性，并通过改变外部磁场主动调谐共振峰。

Result: 模拟展示了FP型磁振子波导的自旋波剖面以及随孔-凹口间距变化的传输和反射谱；理论模型可描述其传输与反射特性；外部磁场可实现共振峰的有效调谐。

Conclusion: 所提出的FP型磁振子波导结构及其分析有助于发展面向低功耗计算和数据处理的磁振子集成电路，并可服务于下一代自旋电子技术。

Abstract: In this paper, we have investigated a spin-wave-based Fabry-P\'erot (FP) cavity using two air holes and notches embedded in a magnonic waveguide. Through micromagnetic simulations, we have demonstrated the spin-wave profile, as well as the transmission and reflection spectra of the FP type magnonic waveguide, by varying the distance between the center of holes and notches. We have introduced an analysis of the reflection spectra in an FP type magnonic waveguide, which is essential and considerable for the performance of magnonic integrated circuits. We have also presented a theoretical model to describe the transmission and reflection characteristics of the FP-type magnonic waveguide. Furthermore, active tuning of the resonance peak of the FP type magnonic wavguide by varying the external magnetic field has been demonstrated. Our proposed structure and its study may contribute to the development of magnonic integrated circuits for low-power computing and data processing in next-generation spintronic technologies.

</details>


### [137] [Zeeman Spin Photocurrents without Spin Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00655)
*Xihua Xiao,Ruizhi Dong,Ruixiang Fei*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文提出并证明：在中心对称磁体中，利用光电场偶极耦合与自旋塞曼耦合之间的干涉，可产生塞曼自旋位移电流和自旋注入电流，从而突破反演对称性对纯电偶极二阶自旋光电流的禁止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反演对称性禁止仅由电偶极耦合驱动的体二阶自旋光电流，因此需要寻找在中心对称磁体（包括反铁磁体和交错磁体）中产生自旋光电流的新机制。

Method: 建立微观理论并进行系统对称性分析，给出自旋简并和自旋劈裂反铁磁体在有/无自旋轨道耦合下的允许响应与偏振选择定则；结合模型分析验证机制，并对MnF2进行第一性原理计算。

Result: 发现电场偶极与自旋塞曼耦合干涉可产生塞曼自旋位移和自旋注入电流；在无自旋轨道耦合的共线磁体中自旋注入消失，但纯自旋位移电流存在且与载流子散射时间无关，其来源于光磁场诱导的横向电子自旋相干，而有序磁矩保持共线固定；MnF2计算预测了实验可及的自旋光电流。

Conclusion: 研究建立了中心对称磁体中光学自旋电流产生的一般框架，并给出一种无需自旋轨道耦合的纯自旋机制，尤其适用于交错磁体。

Abstract: Inversion symmetry forbids bulk second-order spin photocurrents driven solely by electric-dipole coupling. We show that interference between the electric-dipole and spin Zeeman couplings of light enables Zeeman spin-shift and spin-injection currents in centrosymmetric magnets. Our microscopic theory and systematic symmetry analysis establish the allowed responses and polarization selection rules for both spin-degenerate and spin-split antiferromagnets, with and without spin--orbit coupling (SOC). In collinear magnets without SOC, spin injection vanishes, whereas a pure spin-shift current survives independently of the carrier scattering time. This response arises from transverse electronic spin coherences generated by the optical magnetic field, while the ordered moments remain collinear and fixed. Model analyses validate the mechanism, and first-principles calculations for $\mathrm{MnF_2}$ predict experimentally accessible spin photocurrents. These findings establish a general framework for optical spin-current generation in centrosymmetric magnets, including a SOC-free pure-spin mechanism particularly relevant to altermagnets.

</details>


### [138] [A Julia implementation of Floquet-Bloch theory for high-harmonic generation in periodic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00354)
*Ashwin Pillai,Aurelien Manchon,Claudio Attaccalite*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文介绍一个开源 Julia 代码库，用 Floquet-Bloch 理论计算周期紧束缚系统中的高次谐波产生（HHG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为周期晶格在任意光偏振驱动下的 HHG 提供一个灵活、高效且可直接计算 Floquet 态、准能带与谐波电流的实现。

Method: 采用频域 Fourier-Sambe 形式，将含时 Schrödinger 方程转化为静态本征值问题；支持通过 Peierls 代换的解析 Fourier-Bessel 展开与数值 FFT，适用于 1D/2D/3D 晶格和任意光偏振。

Result: 代码用一维两带模型验证，并展示二维蜂窝体系应用：有隙石墨烯中 HHG 截止频率随跃迁参数缩放，以及 Kane-Mele 模型在线性与圆偏振驱动下的场致准能隙和拓扑转变。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了 Floquet 形式论的灵活开源 Julia 实现，可用于紧束缚周期体系的 Floquet 态、准能带和 HHG 研究。

Abstract: We present a flexible Julia codebase implementing Floquet-Bloch theory for high-harmonic generation (HHG) in periodic tight-binding systems. Using the frequency-domain Fourier-Sambe formalism, the package maps the time-dependent Schroedinger equation onto a static eigenvalue problem. This allows direct computation of Floquet states, quasienergy bands, and harmonic currents for 1D, 2D, or 3D lattices under arbitrary light polarizations. The implementation supports both analytical Fourier-Bessel expansions (via Peierls substitution) and numerical Fast Fourier Transforms (FFT). We validate the code against a canonical 1D two-band model and showcase its capabilities on 2D honeycomb systems: gapped graphene mapping HHG cutoff scaling with hopping parameters and the Kane-Mele model, exploring field-induced quasienergy gaps and topological transitions under linear and circular driving fields. In addition to the theoretical presentation, we provide a flexible and efficient open-source Julia implementation of the Floquet formalism: https://github.com/Ashwin-A-Pillai/Floquet-Fourier-Numericals/tree/main.

</details>


### [139] [Anomalous response, re-emerging localization-transition and robust corner sates in self-similar peano fractal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01099)
*Rhiddha Acharjee (Department of Physics,University of Calcutta,92,Acharya Prafulla Chandra Road,Kolkata,West Bengal-700009,India),Sayan Bhattacharya (Department of Physics,Acharya Prafulla Chandra College,New Barrackpore,Kolkata,West Bengal-700131,India),Atanu Nandy (Department of Physics,Acharya Prafulla Chandra College,New Barrackpore,Kolkata,West Bengal-700131,India),Debnarayan Jana (Department of Physics,University of Calcutta,92,Acharya Prafulla Chandra Road,Kolkata,West Bengal-700009,India)*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 在紧束缚框架下研究Peano分形上非相互作用电子的反常谱响应，发现微小非对角各向异性即可调控输运，并可通过准周期重叠积分诱导可重入局域化-离域化转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究分形晶格中非对角无序/各向异性如何影响电子态、输运与局域化，并检验自相似结构中是否存在迁移率边、量子蝴蝶谱及受对称性保护的边缘态。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型；通过能级间距统计分析本征态性质；引入二维准周期Aubry-André与Harper型重叠积分调制；结合逆参与率、分形维数和量子动力学响应进行验证。

Result: 最小非对角各向异性可显著改变输运特性并剧烈修改本征态性质；准周期调制可产生可重入局域化-离域化转变，并暗示单粒子迁移率边存在；谱随调制频率形成量子蝴蝶图案；手性对称保护的边缘态对中等对角无序不敏感。

Conclusion: Peano分形上的非对角准周期调制可产生丰富的谱与局域化物理，包括可重入转变、迁移率边迹象、量子蝴蝶谱以及稳健的手性边缘态。

Abstract: Anomalous spectral response from the non-interacting electrons on a peano fractal is recorded within tight-binding framework. It is observed that a minimal off-diagonal anisotropy can modulate the transport characteristics of the lattice. Drastic modification in the nature of the eigenstates has been validated through the workout of level spacing statistics. Further, a two-dimensional quasiperiodic Aubry-Andr\'e and Harper variation in the overlap integral can invite re-entrant localization-delocalization transition for the self-similar model. This is associated with the possible existence of single-particle mobility edge in the energy landscape. Our results are corroborated by the evaluation of inverse participation ratio, fractal dimension and quantum dynamical response. The spectrum is shown to formulate a quantum butterfly pattern with the frequency of the modulation. Chiral symmetry protected edge states are found to be insensitive with respect to moderate diagonal disorder.

</details>


### [140] [Galilean electrodynamics fluctuation in a superfluid: from general coordinate invariance to Tkachenko mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02482)
*Neng-Chun Chiu,Tzu-Ken Shen,Siau Ihan*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 将 Galilean 电动力学（GED）与 s-wave 超流体耦合，构建受广义坐标不变性约束的有效拉格朗日量，并研究其电磁/引力响应及与涡旋晶格软模的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索 GED 规范多重态如何修正超流体质量并影响其低能集体激发，同时揭示 2+1 维涡旋晶格软扇区与 GED 的对应关系。

Method: 基于广义坐标不变性构造 GED-s-wave 超流体有效拉格朗日量；在线性响应区计算电磁与引力响应；分析长波极限下的压力响应和集体模式；并将涡旋晶格模型的软扇区与 GED 及最低朗道能级下的 Lifshitz 标量场进行比较。

Result: 发现超流体质量被 GED 规范多重态移动；长波极限下压力响应消失；集体模式呈二次色散，类似 2+1 维振荡涡旋晶格中的 Tkachenko 模；进一步证明涡旋晶格模型的软扇区本质上是 GED，并给出规范多重态与最低朗道能级区域 Lifshitz 标量场的关系。

Conclusion: GED 与 s-wave 超流体的耦合导致非传统超流体动力学，并建立了 GED、涡旋晶格软模和 Lifshitz 标量场之间的低能对应关系。

Abstract: We explore coupling the Galilean electrodynamics (GED) to an s-wave superfluid. Governed by the general coordinate invariance, the proposed effective Lagrangian hosts an unconventional superfluid whose mass is shifted by the GED gauge multiplet. We compute its electromagnetic and gravitational responses in the linear regime. In the long-wavelength limit, we discover a vanishing pressure response, and remarkably the collective mode assumes a quadratic dispersion relation reminiscent of the Tkachenko mode in an oscillating vortex lattice in (2+1) dimensions. We further demonstrate that the soft sector of the vortex lattice model is a GED in disguise, and illustrate the relation between the gauge multiplet and the Lifshitz scalar in the lowest-Landau-level regime of the vortex lattice.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [The Halperin (111) state as a paired composite-fermion wave function: Two exact finite-size identities on the sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00204)
*Glenn Wagner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文解析比较了 ν=1/2+1/2 量子霍尔双层中两类配对复合费米子波函数，并证明它们在特定配对形式下均可精确给出 Halperin (111) 态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前用 p 波复合费米子配对和复合费米子-反复合费米子 s 波配对两种波函数描述 ν=1/2+1/2 量子霍尔双层，需要厘清两者关系及是否存在统一重参数化。

Method: 在球面几何中，采用 Jain-Kamilla 最低朗道能级投影方案，解析推导特定配对形式，并比较两类配对复合费米子波函数生成的电子态集合。

Result: 两类配对复合费米子波函数在特定配对形式下均精确复现 Halperin (111) 态；还发现两类波函数共有的第二个波函数。但证据表明两变分族生成的电子态集合虽有重叠但并不完全相同。

Conclusion: 两类波函数族不能被统一重参数化视为同一描述；它们共享部分精确态，但整体上不等价。

Abstract: Two wavefunctions have previously been proposed to describe the $\nu=1/2+1/2$ quantum Hall bilayer: $p$-wave pairing of composite fermions and $s$-wave pairing of composite fermions with anti-composite fermions. We show analytically in the spherical geometry and within the Jain-Kamilla lowest Landau level projection scheme that both of these paired composite fermion wavefunctions exactly reproduce the Halperin (111) state for a specific form of the pairing that we derive. We also find a second wavefunction that is common between both classes of wavefunctions. However, we provide evidence the two variational families generate overlapping but not identical sets of electronic states, ruling out a universal reparametrization that identifies their wavefunctions.

</details>


### [142] [Disorder, Interactions, and the Fate of the Two-Dimensional Electron System: Wigner Crystal, Metal-Insulator Transition, and Amorphous Solid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01899)
*Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在统一理论框架下研究二维电子系统中库仑无序与库仑相互作用的竞争，涉及Wigner晶化、金属-绝缘体转变及Wigner晶体向非晶局域固体的交叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以近期二维电子系统扫描隧道显微成像实验和二维金属-绝缘体转变中“无序 versus 相互作用”的长期争论为动机。

Method: 采用Hartree-Fock理论、变分Monte Carlo，以及渗流和强局域化论证；对金属-绝缘体转变使用带退距d的长程库仑无序的非线性Thomas-Fermi模型，并比较半耗尽密度与Born-RPA输运的Ioffe-Regel-Mott密度。

Result: 在Imry-Ma-Larkin框架下，任何非零带电杂质密度都会在热力学极限破坏真正长程Wigner晶体序；有限系统表现为最多几个晶格间距相干长度的非晶固体。金属-绝缘体转变中，半耗尽密度与Ioffe-Regel-Mott密度在相关参数范围内仅相差数量级为1的因子，呈缓慢的n_i^(-1/10)d^(-1/5)散布；一致性源于转变处共同能量尺度E_dis~E_F而非定理，短程输运通道可使比值移动约2倍。

Conclusion: 该工作是在明确近似下的理论研究，给出可与实验对照的计算特征，但不直接完成与已发表实验分析的比较，比较留给读者。

Abstract: We present a unified theoretical study of the interplay between Coulomb disorder and Coulomb interactions in two-dimensional electron systems, covering the Wigner crystallization transition, the metal-insulator transition, and the crossover from a Wigner crystal to an amorphous localized solid in both zero magnetic field and the lowest Landau level. Using Hartree-Fock theory, variational Monte Carlo, and percolation and strong-localization arguments, we show that quenched charged disorder destroys true long-range Wigner crystalline order in the thermodynamic limit at any nonzero charged-impurity density, within the Imry-Ma-Larkin framework; the finite-system density image is that of an amorphous solid with crystalline coherence over at most a few lattice spacings. For the two-dimensional metal-insulator transition we show, in a controlled nonlinear-Thomas-Fermi model of long-range Coulomb disorder at setback d, that the half-depletion density of the semiclassical landscape and the Ioffe-Regel-Mott density of Born-RPA transport track each other to within a factor of order unity over the physically relevant range of impurity density and setback, with a slow n_i^(-1/10) d^(-1/5) spread; the agreement reflects the common energy scale E_dis ~ E_F at the transition rather than a theorem, and modest independent short-range transport channels can shift the ratio by factors of order two. The article is a theoretical study under well-defined approximations; it takes the recent scanning tunneling microscopy imaging experiments on two-dimensional electron systems and the longstanding disorder-versus-interaction debate on the two-dimensional metal-insulator transition as motivation, and leaves to the reader the comparison between its calculated signatures and the published experimental analyses.

</details>


### [143] [Lattice-Induced Splitting, Connectivity, and Topology of Anyon Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02268)
*Siddhartha Sarkar,Xiaohan Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在分数量子陈绝缘体中，通量-晶格公度性可使分数化准空穴的朗道能级折叠为多个具有非平凡拓扑的任意子能带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子陈绝缘体无需外磁场即可实现分数量子霍尔态，且晶格周期性使原本无色散的任意子获得有限带宽；作者进一步探究其多带拓扑与对称性保护行为。

Method: 研究多带涡旋可系统中等效磁单胞与晶格周期性的公度性，将分数电荷准空穴朗道能级折叠为多个能带，并引入相互作用解除简并；进一步在保持电子能带严格平坦的连续形变下分析陈数重分布与晶体对称性约束。

Result: 得到具有不同陈数的色散任意子能带，其陈数可不同于母准空穴朗道能级，并可通过狄拉克交叉在形变中重分配；晶体对称性可强制保护能带接触。

Conclusion: 分数化激发可展现类似电子布洛赫能带的丰富能带拓扑，为超越传统朗道能级物理的非平凡任意子动力学提供可能。

Abstract: Fractional Chern insulators realize fractional quantum Hall states without external magnetic fields, while their underlying lattice periodicity allows otherwise dispersionless anyons to acquire finite bandwidth. Here we show that flux--lattice commensurability can also give rise to multiple anyon bands with nontrivial topology and symmetry-protected connectivity. In multiband vortexable systems, commensurability between the lattice periodicity and the effective magnetic unit cell of fractionally charged quasiholes leads to the folding of a quasihole Landau level into multiple bands. Interactions lift their degeneracy, producing dispersive bands with Chern numbers distinct from that of the parent quasihole Landau level. We further demonstrate that these Chern numbers can be redistributed through Dirac crossings under continuous deformations that preserve exact electronic flatness, while crystalline symmetries can enforce protected band touchings. Our results establish that fractionalized excitations can exhibit the rich band topology familiar from electronic Bloch bands, opening possibilities for nontrivial anyon dynamics beyond conventional Landau-level physics.

</details>


### [144] [Quantized pumping of boundary Hilbert space in chiral Floquet phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02785)
*Ryota Nakai,Taozhi Guo,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出Hilbert空间泵浦：在时间平移扭曲的边界条件下，量子化维数的边界Hilbert空间在两端之间转移；非相互作用情形由缠绕数决定，相互作用情形由GNVW指标决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性Floquet相具有边界手性流，但需要理解其补充的体-边现象，并刻画相互作用体系与非可逆对称性中的边界Hilbert空间输运。

Method: 将时间平移扭曲视为量子霍尔效应中Laughlin磁通插入的时间类比；解析边界动力学并数值研究反常Floquet Anderson绝缘体；对边界动力学为量子元胞自动机的相互作用体系，用矩阵乘积幺正推导GNVW指标关系并在随机幺正线路中数值验证；应用于非可逆对称性的矩阵乘积算符。

Result: 非相互作用费米子转移的边界模式数由缠绕数确定；相互作用情形每周期扭曲转移的Hilbert空间维数由GNVW指标给出，扭曲边界Floquet算符对应倾斜时间片上的Floquet算符；对非可逆对称性MPO，同一扭曲秩同时返回流指标与对称性算符的量子维度。

Conclusion: Hilbert空间泵浦给出Floquet边界输运的量子化刻画，将非相互作用缠绕数、相互作用GNVW指标及非可逆对称性的量子维度统一起来。

Abstract: Chiral Floquet phases support quantized chiral flow along their boundaries even when the bulk is fully localized. In this Letter, we establish a complementary phenomenon, which we call Hilbert-space pumping: under a boundary condition twisted by time translation, a part of the boundary Hilbert space, of quantized dimension, is transferred between the opposite boundaries. The twist is the temporal analogue of Laughlin's flux insertion in the quantum Hall effect. For noninteracting fermions, the number of transferred boundary modes is determined by the winding number, as we show analytically for the boundary dynamics and numerically for the anomalous Floquet Anderson insulator. For interacting systems whose boundary dynamics is a quantum cellular automaton, the transferred dimension is set by the Gross-Nesme-Vogts-Werner (GNVW) index: the twisted boundary Floquet operator is the Floquet operator on a tilted time slice, whose Hilbert-space dimension is multiplied by the index for each period of twist. We derive this relation for matrix product unitaries and test it numerically in random unitary circuits. Applied to the matrix product operators of non-invertible symmetries, the same twisted rank returns the quantum dimension of the symmetry operator in addition to its flow index.

</details>


### [145] [RIXS study of strain-tuned coupled valence and spin-state transition in epitaxial $(\mathrm{Pr}_{0.85}\mathrm{Y}_{0.15})_{0.7}\mathrm{Ca}_{0.3}\mathrm{CoO}_{3}$ films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00540)
*Yiu-Fung Chiu,Jaewon Choi,Nileena Nandakumaran,Mirian Garcia-Fernandez,Jonathan Pelliciari,Shiyu Fan,Valentina Bisogni,Chris Leighton,Ke-Jin Zhou,Stefano Agrestini*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用Co L2,3/Pr M4,5 XAS与Co L3 RIXS研究应变调控的(Pr,Y,Ca)CoO3薄膜金属-绝缘体转变，揭示Pr与Co-O亚晶格间电荷补偿及电荷转移、自旋态和晶格结构的协同耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变调控的(Pr0.85Y0.15)0.7Ca0.3CoO3薄膜可作为一个模型体系，用于研究钙钛矿钴氧化物中价态、自旋态、结构和金属-绝缘体转变（VSST）的耦合调控。

Method: 采用Co L2,3和Pr M4,5边X射线吸收谱（XAS）以及Co L3边共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS），并结合多重态计算，表征外延薄膜在应变控制VSST前后的局域电子结构。

Result: 在YAlO3（YAO）衬底上的压应变薄膜中，降温穿过转变时出现Pr4+-like谱权重、抑制Co4+-like特征，并主要有利于低自旋Co3+；Pr和Co边的变化定量对应于Co-O网络中每个Co约减少0.15个空穴。在La0.18Sr0.82Al0.59Ta0.41O3（LSAT）衬底上的拉应变薄膜谱几乎与温度无关。多重态计算复现主要XAS线形和高能损处局域dd激发；转变以上恢复的低能RIXS强度与Co4+-like局域激发及金属相相关的较宽粒子-空穴连续谱一致。

Conclusion: 结果提供了元素分辨证据，表明Pr与Co-O亚晶格之间存在电荷补偿，并支持应变控制VSST中电荷转移、自旋态和晶格结构之间的协同耦合。

Abstract: Strain-tuned $(\mathrm{Pr}_{0.85}\mathrm{Y}_{0.15})_{0.7}\mathrm{Ca}_{0.3}\mathrm{CoO}_{3}$ thin films provide a model system for controlling coupled valence, spin-state, structural and metal-insulator transitions (VSSTs) in perovskite cobaltite materials. Here, we use Co $L_{2,3}$ and Pr $M_{4,5}$ X-ray Absorption Spectroscopy (XAS), along with Co $L_{3}$ edge Resonant Inelastic X-ray Scattering (RIXS), to probe the local electronic structure across the strain-controlled VSST in epitaxial (Pr,Y,Ca)CoO$_{3}$ thin films. In compressively strained films on YAlO$_{3}$ (YAO) substrates, cooling through the transition produces Pr$^{4+}$-like spectral weight, suppresses Co$^{4+}$-like character, and favors predominantly low-spin Co$^{3+}$. The changes at the Pr and Co edges are quantitatively consistent with a reduction of approximately 0.15 holes per Co in the Co--O network. By contrast, tensile-strained thin films on La$_{0.18}$Sr$_{0.82}$Al$_{0.59}$Ta$_{0.41}$O$_{3}$ (LSAT) substrates have nearly temperature-independent spectra. Multiplet calculations reproduce the principal XAS lineshapes and local $dd$ excitations at high energy loss. Additionally, low-energy RIXS intensity recovered above the transition is consistent with a combination of Co$^{4+}$-like local excitations and a broader particle--hole continuum associated with the metallic phase. These results provide element-resolved evidence for charge compensation between the Pr and Co--O sublattices and support cooperative coupling among charge-transfer, spin-state, and lattice structure at the strain-controlled VSST.

</details>


### [146] [X-ray magnetic circular dichroism in altermagnets: insights from symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00622)
*Hana Schiff,Ishmam Nihal,John Vinson,Robin Msiska,Judit Romh\'anyi,David Lederman,Christoph Klewe,Sujoy Roy,Eric E. Fullerton*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了交错磁体中X射线磁圆二色（XMCD）的完整对称性框架，并用朗道理论与第一性原理Bethe–Salpeter计算阐明了XMCD与Néel矢量、价态自旋轨道耦合及交叠磁序之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XMCD对Néel矢量奇宇称的响应使其成为探测交错磁基态序的自然手段；但其L边信号主要来自核心能级自旋轨道耦合，需系统理解它如何反映价电子的非相对论交错磁序并指导实验几何选择。

Method: 在保持晶胞不变的条件下，对所有磁点群列表化对称允许的光学霍尔矢量、各向异性磁偶极（AMD）及八极分量；扩展共线交错磁体的朗道理论，将相对论约束与无自旋轨道耦合序联系起来；并对5个代表性交错磁体和1个常规反铁磁体进行L边XMCD的第一性原理Bethe–Salpeter计算。

Result: 只有12个磁点群同时兼容交错磁性与有限XMCD，且均不含三级或更高阶旋转轴；常规反铁磁体在零价态自旋轨道耦合下AMD被迫为零，而所有交错磁子晶格均允许霍尔矢量和AMD以Néel矢量的一阶或三阶出现，XMCD是否非零取决于有限自旋轨道耦合下的Néel矢量取向；该理论区分了信号继承自非相对论AMD的d波交错磁体与需要价态自旋轨道耦合或核-价多重态效应的g波和i波交错磁体。

Conclusion: 朗道理论可统一确定所有交错磁候选材料中XMCD对Néel矢量的依赖，并为利用XMCD识别和区分不同波形的交错磁序提供对称性判据与实验几何指导。

Abstract: Altermagnets are a unique subset of antiferromagnets possessing ferromagnet-like responses that are odd in the N\'eel vector, making X-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD) a natural probe of ground state altermagnetic order. Because the relativistic ingredient of XMCD at the $L$-edge is supplied predominantly by core-level spin--orbit coupling (SOC), the dichroic signal can reflect the non-relativistic order of the valence electrons. Here we develop a complete symmetry framework for XMCD in altermagnets (AMs) preserving the crystalline unit cell. We tabulate the symmetry-allowed optical Hall vector, anisotropic magnetic dipole (AMD), and underlying octupolar components for all magnetic point groups, thereby identifying the experimental geometries that maximize the XMCD signal. We show that only twelve magnetic point groups, none with a rotation axis of order three or higher, are compatible with both altermagnetism and finite XMCD. By extending the Landau theory of collinear AMs, we connect these relativistic constraints to the spin--orbit-free order. We show that the AMD is forced to vanish at zero valence SOC in conventional antiferromagnets, and that every AM sublattice structure allows the Hall vector and AMD at linear or cubic order in the N\'eel vector; whether the resulting XMCD is nonzero depends on the N\'eel vector orientation at finite SOC. The Landau theory thus fixes the N\'eel-vector dependence of the XMCD for all AM candidates, and separates $d$-wave AMs, whose signal is inherited from a non-relativistic AMD, from $g$- and $i$-wave AMs, whose signal requires valence SOC or core--valence multiplet effects. We illustrate these predictions with first-principles Bethe--Salpeter calculations of $L$-edge XMCD for five representative altermagnetic materials and one conventional antiferromagnet.

</details>


### [147] [Charge-Order pattern in $\kappa$-BEDT-TTF salts: A model independent, selection rule approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00798)
*Alberto Girlando*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用振动选择定则作为“试金石”，重新判定κ-BEDT-TTF盐中电荷有序（CO）的模式，指出现有拉曼谱更支持二聚体间CO而非普遍接受的二聚体内电荷歧化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: κ-BEDT-TTF盐及其分子类似物中CO模式通常被默认认为二聚体内电荷歧化，但缺乏模型无关的直接判据；作者希望用振动光谱选择定则检验这一基本假设。

Method: 依据已知振动选择定则：若发生二聚体内电荷歧化，应出现一个易识别的拉曼带。将该判据应用于表现静态或涨落CO的κ-BEDT-TTF盐的可用拉曼光谱。

Result: 在这些盐的可用拉曼谱中未观察到该特征拉曼带，表明CO模式很可能涉及二聚体间电荷有序，与普遍接受的二聚体内电荷歧化图像相反。

Conclusion: 该结果对相关盐的理论建模提出挑战：要么解释为何对二聚体内CO敏感的特征拉曼带缺失，要么从不同基态出发重新建模κ相盐的自旋阻挫、电子铁电性等多变量独特物理性质。

Abstract: A well known vibrational selection rule is invoked to determine the charge order (CO) pattern displayed by the most interesting members of the widely studied class of $\kappa$-BEDT-TTF salts and their molecular analogues. The selection rule indicates that an easily identifiable Raman band should be present if intra-dimer charge disproportionation occurs, constituting a sort of model independent ``litmus test''. The absence of such a band in the available Raman spectra of $\kappa$-BEDT-TTF salts exhibiting static of fluctuating CO, strongly suggests that the CO pattern involves inter-dimer CO, in contrast with the universally accepted intra-dimer disprortionation. This paper therefore poses a challenge for the theoretical modeling of the concerned salts: either the lack of the Raman band sensitive to intra-dimer CO pattern is explained, or the modeling of the multivariate, unique physical properties of these $\kappa$-phase salts (spin frustration, electronic ferroelectricity etc.) has to start from the perspective of a different ground state.

</details>


### [148] [Melting noncoplanar order on the square-kagome lattice: Chiral, flat-band, and staggered spin liquids from projective symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00987)
*Harsh Raj,Atanu Maity,Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究方kagome晶格上非共面磁序经量子涨落熔化能否生成手性自旋液体，发现空间交替手性使保持母序对称的液体陈数为零且无自发热霍尔，能量学也不支持手性液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共面磁序具有标量自旋手性，其量子熔化被视为获得手性自旋液体的可能途径；方kagome晶格上跨八边形耦合可稳定cuboc与锥形序，因此需检验该路线。

Method: 用投影对称群分类该晶格的费米型自旋液体，确定各非共面序熔化所对应的对称类，并用Gutzwiller投影波函数比较候选态与能量。

Result: 提出磁选择规则：cuboc和锥形序的手性在空间交替，故保持母序对称的液体陈数为零、无自发热霍尔响应；陈态只能源于均匀手性序，常规情形需铁磁bow-tie耦合。能量上强跨八边形耦合时cuboc1有序态最低，较弱耦合时非手性价键晶体或时间反演对称液体更优；两个不等价格点轨道引入相对时间反演符号使平带钉扎费米能级，且p4mm平移非对易导致六重对称晶格中不存在的交错手性液体。

Conclusion: 在方kagome晶格上，非共面序熔化生成手性自旋液体受手性选择规则和能量学双重限制，通常并非基态；但晶格对称性允许交错手性液体等特殊态，需特定耦合条件才可能出现陈手性液体。

Abstract: Noncoplanar magnetic orders carry a scalar spin chirality, and their melting by quantum fluctuations has been proposed as a route to chiral spin liquids. We ask what this route can produce on the square-kagome lattice, where couplings across the octagons stabilize noncoplanar cuboc and conical orders. We classify the fermionic spin liquids of this lattice by their projective symmetry groups, identify the symmetry class into which each noncoplanar order would melt, and compare candidate states with Gutzwiller-projected wave functions. A magnetic selection rule constrains the route: the chirality of every cuboc and conical order alternates in space, so a liquid that keeps the symmetry of its parent order has zero Chern number and no spontaneous thermal Hall response. A Chern state can descend only from uniformly chiral orders, and the regular ones we find need ferromagnetic bow-tie couplings. The energetics do not favor a chiral liquid either: among the projected wave functions we compare, a cuboc1-ordered state is lowest for strong cross-octagon couplings, and nonchiral states, a valence-bond crystal or a time-reversal-symmetric liquid, for weaker ones. The two inequivalent site orbits shape the spinons: a relative time-reversal sign between them pins flat bands to the Fermi level, and because the translations of $p4mm$ are not commutators, staggered chiral liquids exist, which are absent on sixfold-symmetric lattices.

</details>


### [149] [Stability, Collective Modes, and Quasiparticle-Phonon Coupling of Metallic Wigner Crystals in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02151)
*Sunghoon Kim,Yang-Zhi Chou,Jay D. Sau,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用自洽Hartree-Fock与时变Hartree-Fock研究菱形多层石墨烯中的金属Wigner晶体，发现自掺杂载流子会显著改变其稳定性、集体激发与准粒子耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期输运实验在菱形多层石墨烯中观察到金属Wigner晶体（mWC），其晶格有序与少量空穴型载流子共存。这引出关键问题：在无能隙费米子存在时，mWC是否稳定，以及其集体激发会如何演化。

Method: 采用自洽Hartree-Fock与时间依赖Hartree-Fock计算，系统分析电子口袋型与空穴掺杂型mWC的稳定性，并研究声子/集体模式与粒子-空穴激发的杂化及准粒子耦合。

Result: 电子口袋型mWC通常对粒子-空穴涨落不稳定；较低密度下的空穴掺杂mWC可局部稳定。在稳定空穴掺杂mWC中，纵向声子与粒子-空穴激发强烈杂化，而横向声子仍相对清晰；准粒子耦合具有显著模式依赖，纵向类激发通常强于横向模式。

Conclusion: 自掺杂载流子不仅使mWC具有金属性，还从根本上重塑电子晶体的稳定性、集体动力学和准粒子相互作用。

Abstract: Recent transport experiments in rhombohedral multilayer graphene have reported evidence for metallic Wigner crystals (mWCs), characterized by the coexistence of crystalline order and a small density of hole-like carriers. This coexistence raises fundamental questions about the stability of mWCs and the fate of their collective excitations in the presence of gapless fermions. Using self-consistent Hartree-Fock and time-dependent Hartree-Fock calculations, we demonstrate that itinerant carriers profoundly influence both. We find that mWCs with electron pockets are generally unstable to particle-hole fluctuations, while hole-doped mWCs at lower densities remain locally stable. In the stable hole-doped mWC, the longitudinal phonon strongly hybridizes with particle-hole excitations, while the transverse phonon remains relatively well defined. Quasiparticle coupling is strongly mode-dependent and is typically stronger for longitudinal-like excitations than for transverse modes. Our results demonstrate that self-doped carriers not only enable metallicity but also fundamentally reshape the stability, collective dynamics, and quasiparticle interactions of electronic crystals.

</details>


### [150] [For any static interaction the self-energy and vertex corrections to the electron-gas polarizability combine exactly into one integrand free of the Coulomb singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02514)
*Carl A. Kukkonen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明：对于仅依赖动量转移的相互作用，均匀电子气极化率的一阶自能插入与顶点修正可在积分前精确合并为单一被积函数，从而消除Coulomb对数发散并简化计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两项一阶修正对Coulomb相互作用各自在q^2系数上出现对数发散，虽符号相反并在求和中抵消，但分别计算不便；屏蔽相互作用还需数值处理，因此需要更稳健的合并方案。

Method: 对任意仅依赖转移动量的相互作用解析推导两个图的自能与顶点被积函数的精确合并，分析零温与有限温情形、Coulomb奇异性和化学势抵消项，并用裸Coulomb、Yukawa和屏蔽相互作用进行检验。

Result: 零温下合并后的被积函数无Coulomb奇异性且无化学势抵消项；有限温仅需额外加入一项；对屏蔽相互作用应数值计算合并式而非两个图；对频率依赖相互作用，该合并不再精确。

Conclusion: 对静态的动量依赖相互作用，可精确合并自能插入与顶点修正，为均匀电子气极化率提供更简洁且数值更稳健的计算框架；若相互作用依赖频率，则合并失效，需保留原两项。

Abstract: The first-order corrections to the polarizability of the uniform electron gas are a self-energy insertion and a vertex correction. For the Coulomb interaction the coefficient of q^2 in each diverges logarithmically at small wave vector, and the two divergences have opposite signs and cancel in their sum. This Note shows that for any interaction that depends only on the momentum it transfers, the two integrands combine exactly into one before any integration is done. At zero temperature the combined integrand has no Coulomb singularity and no chemical-potential counterterm. For screened interactions, which must be integrated numerically, it should be computed in place of the two diagrams. The Note gives the derivation and its physical reason, the one term added at finite temperature, and checks for bare, Yukawa and screened interactions. With a frequency-dependent interaction the combination is no longer exact.

</details>


### [151] [Competing interaction driven broad magnetic entropy distribution in triangular net compounds \text{Ba$_3$RE(BO$_3$)$_3$}, RE= Dy and Ho](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02703)
*Krishna Nand Prajapati,Prabuddha Kant Mishra,Shashi B Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了三角晶格稀土硼酸盐Ba3RE(BO3)3（RE=Dy、Ho）的磁性与磁热效应，并评估其低温磁制冷应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角磁性格子是研究阻挫磁性与量子自旋液体的重要模型平台，稀土基材料同时有望用于低温磁制冷。

Method: 采用固相反应法合成多晶样品，确认其六方P63mc结构及约8.8 Å间隔的三角网；通过磁化率、等温磁化及磁熵变分析研究磁性与磁热性能。

Result: 低温（T<20 K）偏离纯顺磁行为，M vs H/T不重合及磁矩降低表明局域f态存在显著晶体电场劈裂；在70 kOe场变下，Dy基与Ho基化合物的最大磁熵变分别为4.2与7.3 J kg^-1 K^-1，相对制冷能力分别为204与230 J kg^-1。

Conclusion: Arrott图正斜率、ΔSm/ΔSm^max对约化温度的普适标度行为及RCP与H关系的场指数c均表明熵变呈二级特征，Ba3RE(BO3)3（RE=Dy、Ho）是毫开尔文温区磁热应用的候选材料。

Abstract: Triangular magnetic lattices have attracted significant attention as a model platform for exploring frustrated magnetism and quantum spin liquids, particularly in rare-earth-based systems. These materials also hold considerable promise for cryogenic magnetic refrigeration. In this work, we investigate the magnetic and magnetocaloric properties of the rare-earth (RE) based compounds $\text{Ba}_3\text{RE}(\text{BO}_3)_3$ ($\text{RE} = \text{Dy}$ and $\text{Ho}$). Polycrystalline samples synthesized via a solid-state reaction method crystallize in a hexagonal structure with the space group $P6_3mc$, featuring triangular nets separated by approximately $8.8~\text{\AA}$ along the $c$-axis. Detailed magnetic investigation reveals a deviation from pure paramagnetic behavior at low temperatures ($T < 20\text{ K}$). This is further corroborated by isothermal magnetization measurements, which exhibit non-overlapping $M$ vs. $H/T$ plots and reduced magnetization moments, suggesting significant crystal electric field (CEF) splitting of localized $f$-states. For an applied magnetic field change of $70\text{ kOe}$, the maximum entropy change (Relative Cooling Power) reaches $4.2\text{ J kg}^{-1}\text{ K}^{-1}$ ($204\text{ J kg}^{-1}$) and $7.3\text{ J kg}^{-1}\text{ K}^{-1}$ ($230\text{ J kg}^{-1}$) for the Dy- and Ho-based compounds, respectively. Overall, the positive slopes of Arrott plots, the universal scaling behaviour of $\Delta S_{\text{m}}/\Delta S_{\text{m}}^{\text{max}}$ versus reduced temperature ($t$), and the field exponent $c$ of the RCP versus $H$ curves indicate a second-order nature of entropy distribution, making them promising candidates for magnetocalorics in the millikelvin range.

</details>


### [152] [Hidden variable $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological order in a bilayer kagom\'e antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03073)
*Hao Tian,Pengwei Zhao,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在双层kagomé反铁磁体中，将自旋二聚体的四个局域态编码为两个赝自旋-1/2变量，可在隐藏的τ扇区实现Z₂拓扑序，而μ扇区为平庸顺磁体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实体系中的拓扑序十分罕见，因此需要回答拓扑序是否可能隐藏在内部自由度中，而非直接体现在常规自旋或电子自由度上。

Method: 提出双层kagomé反铁磁体中的隐藏变量及其有效相互作用；把自旋二聚体的四个局域态组织为两个赝自旋-1/2变量τ和μ，并识别出可映射为有效Balents-Fisher-Girvin模型的参数区间。

Result: 隐藏τ扇区呈现Z₂拓扑序，辅助μ扇区为平庸顺磁体；物理自旋对应复合对象，实验信号通常表现为分数化任意子连续谱与非拓扑磁振子激发的卷积，但纵向层内响应等特定对称选择通道可无集体模修饰地直接显现裸双vison连续谱。

Conclusion: 拓扑序可埋藏在隐藏且涌现的自由度中；这为在隐藏内禀自由度中寻找难以观测的拓扑态提供了有希望的途径。

Abstract: Topological orders in realistic systems are quite rare, raising one fundamental question of whether they might be harbored with hidden degrees of freedom rather than directly exposed in conventional spins or electrons. We here propose a set of hidden variables and their effective interactions in a bilayer kagom\'e antiferromagnet. By properly organizing four local states of the spin dimer into two pseudospin-$1/2$ variables ($\boldsymbol{\tau}$ and $\boldsymbol{\mu}$), we identify a regime where an effective Balents-Fisher-Girvin model emerges, realizing a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological order in the hidden $\boldsymbol{\tau}$ sector while the auxiliary $\boldsymbol{\mu}$ sector remains a trivial paramagnet. This hidden nature drastically alters the experimental manifestations. Physical spins map to composite objects, typically convolving fractionalized anyon continua with non-topological magnon excitations. Nevertheless, specific symmetry-selected channels, such as the longitudinal intralayer response, exclusively reveal the bare two-vison continuum without collective-mode dressing. Our findings demonstrate that the topological order could be buried in certain hidden and emergent degrees of freedom, pointing to a promising pathway for seeking elusive topological states disguised within hidden internal degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [153] [Enforced Gaplessness by Combining Non-Anomalous Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03164)
*Takamasa Ando,Kantaro Ohmori*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文研究由整体对称性强制的无能隙性：即使两个较大的对称性各自可平凡有隙且非反常，只要它们对共同对称性的嵌入相差一个与SPT相相关的twist，所生成的对称性仍禁止有限多基态的有隙相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作主要依赖反常对称性来解释对称性强制的无能隙性；作者希望推广其提出的“symmetry span”机制，并检验当两个较大对称性均为可逆且非反常时是否仍能强制无能隙。

Method: 定义并分析两个较大对称性对同一共同对称性的不同嵌入构成的symmetry span；若两种嵌入相差一个SPT相相关的twist，则论证生成对称性不能有有限基态的有隙相。构造1+1维有限群与U(1)^2的span，并给出连续场实现和具有有限维局域Hilbert空间、有限参数的格点Hamiltonian；另讨论2+1维例子及仅含有限对称性的span。

Result: 证明symmetry span可在两个较大对称性各自非反常且可平凡有隙时仍强制无能隙；给出1+1维具体模型在其参数空间任意区域均无能隙，并提供连续与格点实现；还给出2+1维例子和纯有限对称性情形。

Conclusion: 对称性强制的无能隙性不必依赖单个对称性的反常；通过嵌入间的SPT twist，symmetry span提供了更一般的无能隙性判据和可构造模型。

Abstract: Symmetry-enforced gaplessness is a phenomenon in which a quantum system is forced to be gapless due to its global symmetry. Previous works have mainly focused on anomalous symmetries. Another approach, using symmetry spans, which specify two embeddings of a common symmetry into two larger symmetries, was proposed by the authors. In this work, we show that a symmetry span can enforce gaplessness even when both larger symmetries are invertible and non-anomalous: each alone admits a trivially gapped phase, but because the embeddings differ by a twist associated with a symmetry-protected topological phase, the symmetry they generate admits no gapped phase with finitely many ground states. We present such a span of a finite group and $\mathrm{U}(1)^2$ in 1+1 dimensions, with continuum realizations and a lattice Hamiltonian, with a finite-dimensional local Hilbert space and finitely many parameters, that is gapless in any region of its parameter space. We also discuss a 2+1-dimensional example and spans of only finite symmetries.

</details>


### [154] [Theory of the Ferroaxial Charge Density Wave in 1T-TiSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03478)
*Ilaria Maccari,Rafael M. Fernandes,Fernando de Juan,Maria N. Gastiasoro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出1T-TiSe2中CDW的丰富应变响应不能仅用标准L1^-序参量解释，而需要引入次级的L2^- CDW序参量；耦合的L1^-与L2^- Landau理论可重现弹性热效应和弹性电阻观测到的额外相变、铁轴序及低温向列涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期应变实验在1T-TiSe2的CDW态内发现额外转变与对称性破缺：T_CDW以下出现第二转变并与破坏所有竖直镜面对称但保留反演的铁轴序相关，更低温度还报道了向列磁化率发散。作者旨在用一个统一的理论框架解释这些现象并判断其所需的最小序参量结构。

Method: 基于对称性分析、微观机制和衍射考虑，作者论证标准L1^- CDW序参量不足，提出次级的L2^- CDW序参量；随后构建L1^-与L2^-耦合的Landau理论，并在存在应变时分析相图、序参量耦合及对称性破缺。

Result: 耦合L1^-与L2^-的Landau模型能够重现弹性热测量中的相边界以及CDW转变以下铁轴序的出现；模型还支持在更低温度发生三重旋转对称性破缺的第三次转变，与弹性电阻中报道的向列磁化率发散一致。

Conclusion: 1T-TiSe2的CDW物理需要至少两个CDW序参量：主导的L1^-和次级的L2^-。耦合L1^-/L2^-序参量框架可统一解释应变诱导的额外相变、铁轴序和低温向列涨落，并预测更低温度的三重旋转对称性破缺。

Abstract: Recent experiments using strain to probe the charge-density wave (CDW) of 1T-TiSe$_2$ have uncovered additional transitions and broken symmetries inside the CDW state. The elastocaloric effect reveals a second transition below $T_\mathrm{CDW}$, which elastoresistivity shows to be associated with ferroaxial order, breaking all vertical mirror symmetries while preserving inversion. In addition, at even lower temperatures, a diverging nematic susceptibility has been reported through elastoresistivity. In this work, we argue that this rich phenomenology cannot be explained by the standard $L_1^-$ CDW order parameter, and instead requires the existence of a secondary CDW order parameter. Based on symmetry, microscopic, and diffraction considerations, we propose this subleading CDW to have $L_2^-$ symmetry. By analyzing the Landau theory of coupled $L_1^-$ and $L_2^-$ orders in the presence of strain, we reproduce the phase boundaries observed in the elastocaloric measurements and the emergence of ferroaxial order below the CDW transition. Moreover, the model supports a third threefold-rotational symmetry breaking transition at lower temperatures, consistent with the reported divergence of the nematic susceptibility.

</details>


### [155] [An Intrinsically Gapless Topological Phase with Dipole Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03538)
*Amogh Anakru,Linhao Li,Zhen Bi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文表明，拓扑绝缘体中的边界分数化可在偶极守恒系统中强制体态无能隙，并给出一维费米子模型中的本征无能隙对称保护拓扑（igSPT）相：偶极Luttinger液体与半量子化边界电荷共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨边界分数化是否能反过来约束体态，即在电荷守恒、偶极守恒和粒子-空穴对称下实现受对称性与晶格平移保护的本征无能隙SPT相。

Method: 构造一维费米子模型，结合解析可解极限与DMRG数值计算，分析边界电荷、边缘简并及偶极分辨纠缠谱。

Result: 发现igSPT相具有半量子化边界电荷；两个电荷中性边缘构型因偶极守恒和粒子-空穴对称在有限尺寸下严格简并；纠缠谱中出现平移的共形塔，对应Luttinger液体的不同共形边界条件；该相在广泛参数范围内稳定。

Conclusion: 边界分数化可作为一种强制体态无能隙的机制；所得igSPT相阻止保持对称性与晶格平移的唯一有能隙体态，并由解析与数值结果共同确认。

Abstract: We show that the boundary fractionalization familiar from gapped topological insulators can enforce bulk gaplessness in a dipole-conserving system. In a one-dimensional fermion model with charge conservation, dipole conservation, and particle-hole symmetry, we realize an intrinsically gapless symmetry-protected topological (igSPT) phase combining a dipolar Luttinger liquid with half-quantized boundary charges. This boundary structure obstructs a unique gapped bulk preserving these symmetries and lattice translation. Remarkably, the two charge-neutral edge configurations remain exactly degenerate even at finite size due to dipole conservation and particle-hole symmetry. The boundary fractionalization also manifests as shifted conformal towers in the dipole-resolved entanglement spectrum, reflecting distinct conformal boundary conditions of the Luttinger liquid. We support our analysis of the igSPT by studying exactly-solvable limits and performing DMRG computations, establishing its stability for a wide range of tuning parameters.

</details>


### [156] [Ultrafast X-ray-induced dephasing of valence electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03635)
*J. Vonka (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),B. Pedrini (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),J. Ravnik (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),A. Reiche (PSI Center for Accelerator Science and Engineering,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),D. G. Mazzone (PSI Center for Neutron and Muon Sciences,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),P. Anand (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland,Laboratory for Solid State Physics and Quantum Center,ETH Z\"urich,Z\"urich,Switzerland),S. Allenspach (PSI Center for Neutron and Muon Sciences,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),Y. Ekinci (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),U. Staub (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),S. -W. Huang (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),M. Sander (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),R. Mankowsky (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),H. T. Lemke (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland),G. Aeppli (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland,Institut de Physique,EPF Lausanne,Lausanne,Switzerland),S. Gerber (PSI Center for Photon Science,Paul Scherrer Institute,Villigen PSI,Switzerland)*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在单晶硅中用宽带能量啁啾X射线实现少飞秒时间编码，发现价键退相干可在20飞秒内发生；因此当价电子对布拉格峰有显著贡献时，X射线晶体学需要短于当前约50飞秒标准的脉冲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “先衍射后破坏”方案假设超短X射线脉冲快于辐射损伤；但原子核虽在数十飞秒尺度运动，外层价电子响应更快，可能破坏其位置信息，需要实验检验这一时间极限。

Method: 使用宽带、能量啁啾的X射线脉冲在元素硅中编码少飞秒时间分辨；将价电子敏感的结构禁戒反射与同时测量的核主导布拉格峰进行比较，并在直接电离效应可忽略的脉冲能量下测试。

Result: 观察到亚20飞秒时间尺度上的价键退相干，即使脉冲能量下直接电离效应可忽略；表明价电子显著贡献布拉格峰时，约50飞秒标准脉冲不足以保持价电子结构信息。

Conclusion: 价电子敏感的硬X射线晶体学需要更短脉冲；该方案为利用硬X射线区分价电子与晶格对有序性和动力学的贡献提供路径，可应用于分子生物学、量子物质与器件研究。

Abstract: Diffract-before-destroy schemes for atomic-scale electron mapping assume that ultrashort X-ray pulses outrun radiation damage. Although atomic cores move over tens of femtoseconds, outer electrons respond much faster, compromising their locations. We test this limit in elemental silicon using broadband, energy-chirped X-ray pulses to encode time with few-femtosecond resolution. Comparing valence-sensitive structurally forbidden reflections with simultaneously measured core-dominated Bragg peaks reveals valence-bond dephasing on sub-20-fs timescales even at pulse energies where direct ionization effects are negligible. Our findings demonstrate that crystallography where outer electrons contribute substantially to Bragg peaks requires pulses shorter than today's standard $\sim50\,fs$ standard. They also establish a path to disentangle valence electron and lattice contributions to order and dynamics with hard X-rays, enabling studies across molecular biology, quantum matter and devices.

</details>
